tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ

patippuri
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolinōkkup pārviyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum cērttu avaruṭiya maṟivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭurikaḷi ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭurip putiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷi naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamiyuṭivar eṉṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñari iṉṉum peṟṟut taravilli eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturiyarum, kuṟimatiyar tēkkivitta kariyiruḷi nīkka vanta maṟimaliyār vaḻivanta niṟimaliyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravittalivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷi mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam urippaṉa. tamiḻi āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyi mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟi ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamiyākum.
pāvāṇarit tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷi veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟiyum, citikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putiyalkaḷi tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyi mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷi vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷi ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tanti periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷki
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.

vāṉmaḻi vaḷaccurappu
āḻattuḷ āḻamāy akaḻntu taṅkam ēṉ eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu? āḻkaṭaluḷ ceṉṟu, uyirip paṇayam vittu muttuk kuḷikkap paṭuvatu ēṉ? pavaḻap pāḷaṅkaḷi eṭuppatu ēṉ? vayiram mutalām maṇikkulaṅkaḷi iṭiyaṟāmal tēṭit tēṭi eṭuppatu ēṉ?
aṇikalap poruḷkaḷ eṉpataṟku maṭṭumā? vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭṭukkum īṭṭutaṟku ariya vippu vaḷamāka iruntu, nāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyal mēmpāṭṭukku aṭimaṇi
yākavum ivi iruppatāl tāṉē!
ivaṟṟiṉum mēlām vippu vaḷamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu! uṇṭu! avi cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ!
kālam kālamāka aḷḷik koṇṭālum vaṟṟā vaḷamāyt tikaḻum aṟivuk karuvūlamām nūlkaḷ!
oṉṟik kaṟṟāl oru nūṟāy ōrāyiramāyp palkip perukat takka peṟaṟkariya tāy nūlkaḷām pēṟaminta nūlkaḷ! cēynūlkaḷi īṉavalla ceḻu nūlkaḷ!
nuṇ māṇ nuḻipulat tāy nūlkaḷāy iruppiṉum! avaṟṟit tāṅkiya tāḷkaḷ, ettakiya pēṇutal uṭiyavi eṉiṉum kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyak kūṭiyavi tāmē!
kalleḻuttē, katir veppālum kaṭaluppālum kāttup pēṇum kaṭappāṭillār kippaṭalālum aḻintu paṭṭami kaṇkūṭu tāṉē!
ēṭṭu varivukaḷ, nīrē neruppē nīṭitta kālamē puṟṟē pōṟṟā maṭamiyē eṉpavaṟṟāl aḻintu paṭṭamikku aḷavu tāṉum uṇṭō? makkaḷukku māṇap periya payaṉām nūlkaḷ, kaṟkum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum, piṟantu perukivarum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum takka vakiyil avarkaḷukkup pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyar vitta paḻantēṭṭeṉṉa annūlkaḷ, mīḷamīḷat taṭṭillāmal kiṭikkac ceyya vēṇṭuvatu nūṟpatippar tavirāk kaṭamiyallavō!
akkaṭamiyi avarkaḷ kālantōṟum kaṭappāṭākak koṇṭu ceyya, āḷum aracum vāḻum aṟivarum arunnuṇiyātal tāṉē kaṭṭāyat tēvi! ivvāṟu patipparum aṟivarum āḷnarum tattam mūccuk kaṭaṉāka, nūṟkoṭi puritalukku mēṟkoṭi illiyām!
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ oṉṟā iraṇṭā? namakku eṭṭātavi pōka eṭṭiya nūlkaḷ 32. kaṭṭuri poḻivu mutalām vakiyāl kiṭṭiya tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ 12. iṉṉum eṭṭāta tokuppum aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟum vara vāyppuṇṭu!
kaṭita varivu pā varivu eṉa mēlum perukavum vāyppuṇṭu!
ivaṟṟi ellām āyiram acciṭṭu avvaḷavil niṉṟu viṭṭāl, tēṭuvārkkut tēṭum poḻutil vāykkum tiruvākat tikaḻak kūṭumō?
ātalāl, cēy nūlkaḷukku mūlamām tāynūlkaḷ, kālam tōṟum vāṉmaḻi vaḷac curappāka veḷippaṭa vēṇṭum kaṭṭāyam uṇarntu kaṭami puriyum vīṟuṭiyār vēṇṭum! mika vēṇṭum!
ittakiya viḻumiya vīṟuṭiyar - iṉamāṉac cemmal - tamiḻp pōrāḷi - tiruttaku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār, tām muntu muḻutuṟak koṇarnta pāvāṇar paṭippukaḷi mīḷamīḷat tamiḻulāk koḷḷa vikkum muṉ muyaṟciyāy, im mīḷpatippi vaḻaṅkukiṟār! tamiḻulakam payaṉ koḷvatāka! payaṉ ceyvatāka!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.


cāṉṟitaḻ
paṇṭita ñā. tēvanēyaṉār, pi.ō.el. potuvāka moḻinūl āyvu muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟit tamiḻccol ārāyccipaṟṟi eḻutiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nīṇṭakālat tēviyiṉi niṟivu ceytaṉa. tammuṭiya iḷantik kālattilē talimik kaṇkāṇiyār tavattiru tirañcu (Trench) eḻutiya collārāycci, pērāciriyar mākkacu mullar eḻutiya moḻiyaṟiviyal, pērāciriyar cāycu eḻutiya oppiyal moḻinūl mutaliya āṅkiya nūlkaḷi yāmē peruviruppuṭaṉ paṭittukkoṇṭirukkumpōtu anta muṟiyil tamiḻc coṟkaḷi ārāya vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉēm. tamiḻp pēraṟiñarkaṭkup pulappaṭāmal maṟintu
kiṭanta virivākavum viyappākavumuḷḷa tamiḻmoḻiyaṟivup parappu, paṇṭārakar kālṭuvel eḻutiya tirāviṭamoḻi
kaḷiṉ oppiyal āyvu nūlāl pulappaṭalāyiṟṟu. eṉiṉum paṇṭārakar kālṭuvel, aṟiyappaṭāta vaṭṭārattil ceytatoru muyaṟciyātalāl tamiḻccoṟkaḷi yellām viṭāmal niṟivāka eṭuttārāyntuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu etir
pārppataṟkilli. ituvē moḻiyiyali ārāya vēṇṭu meṉṟu emmit tūṇṭiyatu. eṉavē `ñāṉacākaram (aṟivukkaṭal) eṉṉum emmuṭiya itaḻiṉ mutal tokutiyil at tuṟiyil oṉṟiraṇṭu kaṭṭurikaḷi eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭēm. āṉāl appōtu camayam, meypporuḷiyal, ilakkiya varalāṟu ākiya tuṟikaḷil emmuṭiya moḻiyārāyccit tuṟiyil toṭarntu īṭupaṭak kūṭavilli. āyiṉum takutiyuṭiya aṟiñar yārāvatu it tuṟiyil ārāyvataṟku muṉvarakkūṭumā eṉṟu etirpārttuk koṇṭiruntēm. appōtu yāḻppāṇam tiruttanti ñāṉappirakācartām ekipatiya moḻiyiyal ārāycci nūli emakku aṉuppivittār.  atu ōraḷavu emakku maṉaniṟivu aḷittatu. eṉiṉum moḻiyiyal ārāyccit
tuṟi mikavum virivum āḻamu muṭiyatātalāl paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷik kaṟṟu avaṟṟik koṇṭu naṉku purintu
koḷḷum vakiyil mēlum moḻiyiyal ārāycci nūlkaḷ veḷivarutal vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutiṉēm. anta nērattil 
tiru. tēvanēyaṉār yām etirpārttati ēṟattāḻa muṟṟum niṟivēṟṟiyatu kaṇṭu perumakiḻvuṟṟēm. at tuṟiyil avar mikavum uḻippeṭuttu ārāyntu eḻutiyiruppavaṟṟit tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ naṉṟāka nampalām.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tiru. tēvanēyaṉār oppaṟṟa taṉit tiṟamiyuṭiyavar eṉṟum, avarukku oppāka iruppavar arumiyākum eṉṟum yām uṇmiyākavē karutukiṉṟēm.
mēlum tiru. tēvanēyaṉār patiyac collum āciriyarum iṉpuṟuttum coṟpoḻivāḷarumāvar. pala tamiḻk kaḻaka āṇṭu viḻākkaḷil ematu talimiyiṉkīḻ avar coṟ
poḻivukaḷ āṟṟiyuḷḷār. avi kēṭṭārp piṇikkun takiyavāy amintu aviyōrik kiḷarcciyuṟac ceytu makiḻccik kaṭalil āḻttiṉa. mt® tUªâíiH¤J MuhŒ¢á brŒJtU« m¿P® Mjyhš mtiu¥ gÂÆš mk®¤J« vªj Ãiya¤J¡F« mtuhš ngU« òfG« »il¡f¥

- maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ
*  maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār āṅkilattil eḻutiya cāṉṟitaḻiṉ        		   tamiḻākkam.

mukavuri
viḷiyāṭṭāvatu virumpiyāṭum āṭṭu. (viḷi = viruppam, āṭṭu = āṭṭam) atu ciṟuvar periyōr ākiya iru cārārkkum potuvēṉum, muṉṉavarkkē ciṟappāka uriyatām. makkaḷ nilitta kuṭumpavāḻkki vāḻat toṭaṅkiya kālattiliruntu, viḷiyāṭṭu viṉi ulakil nikaḻntu vantirukkiṉṟatu. vēli ceyyāta paruvattil allatu ōyvu nērattil, ciṟuvar periyōriṉ ceyali allatu iyaṟki nikaḻcciyi naṭittu makiḻnta tiṟamē viḷiyāṭṭut tōṟṟamākat teritaliṉ, atu mutaṉmutal ciṟuvariṭiyē tōṉṟiṟṟeṉak koḷḷutal tavaṟākātu.
viḷiyāṭṭu nilikkaḷaṉ, vāḻkkit toḻil, pōr, aruñceyal, ciṟappu nikaḻcci mutaliyavākap palatiṟappaṭum. uḻavar ceyyum payirttoḻilic ciṟuvar naṭittāṭum āṭṭu paṇṇiyeṉṟum; ōr iḷaṅkaṉṉikkuk kaḷiṟu, puli mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷālum āḻnīrālum nēravirunta kēṭṭi, taṟceyalāka avvaḻi vanta kāḷip paruvattāṉoruvaṉ nīkkiya ceytiyic ciṟār naṭittāṭuvatu keṭavaral eṉṟum peyar peṟṟatāka ūkikka iṭamuṇṭu. (paṇṇi = vayal)
``keṭavaral paṇṇi āyiraṇṭum viḷiyāṭṭu''  
(tol. uri. 21)
piṟkālattil avviru peyarkaḷiṉ ciṟappup poruḷi aṟiyātār, avaṟṟi viḷiyāṭṭu eṉṉum potup
poruḷilēyē vaḻaṅkiṉar pōlum!
virumpappaṭutal, ceyaṟkeḷimi, iṉpantaral ākiya mūṉṟum viḷiyāṭṭiṉ iyalpākum. oruvaṉ ōr aruviṉiyi eḷitākac ceytuviṭiṉ, avaṉ ati oru viḷiyāṭṭuppōṟ ceytuviṭṭāṉ eṉpar. viḷiyāṭṭu iṉpan taruvatupaṟṟiyē,
``celvam pulaṉē puṇarvu viḷiyāṭṭeṉ
	ṟallal nītta uvaki nāṉkē' 	 (tol. 1205)
eṉa, ati iṉpavaḻi nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟākak kūṟiyatōṭu, iṉpa nukarcciyiyē `paṇṇi' (1195) eṉṉuñ collāṟ kuṟittaṉar tolkāppiyar. ap paṇṇi yeṉṉuñ coṟku, ``muṭiyuṭi mūvēntaruṅ kuṟunila maṉṉaru mutalāyiṉōr nāṭakamakaḷir āṭalum pāṭaluṅ kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭuṅ kāmanukarum iṉpa viḷiyāṭṭu'' eṉṟu pērāciriyar uri kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka. eḷiyatum iṉpan taruvatumāṉa ceyalellām virumpappaṭuvatē. iṟivaṉ aṭiyārik kākkum tiruvaruṭceyalkaḷ mēṟkūṟiya mūviyalpuṅ koṇṭaṉa veṉṉuṅ koḷkipaṟṟiyē, avi tiruviḷiyāṭal eṉappaṭuvaṉa.
viḷiyāṭṭāl oruvarkku uṭaluram, uḷḷakkiḷarcci, maṟappaṇpu, mativaṉmi, kūṭṭuṟavuttiṟam, vāḻnāḷ nīṭṭippu mutaliyaṉa uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. ikkālattil cilarkku, `karumpu tiṉṉak kikkūlipōl' viḷiyāṭṭāl piḻippu vaḻiyum ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. nīṇṭakālamāka vāḻkkit toḻil
vakiyāka iruntuvarum nāṭaka naṭaṅkaḷum, mutaṟkālattil viḷiyāṭṭākat tōṉṟiyaviyē.
orucila viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ ulaka muḻumikkum potu
vēṉum, pala viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ vevvēṟu nāṭṭiṟkut taṉic ciṟappūṭṭuvaṉa. āṅkila āṭci nīṅkit tamiḻāṭci varavirukkum
pōtu, vaḻakkuk kuṉṟiya tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭukaḷi nāṭu muḻutum parappuvatu nallateṉṟu kaṇṭu, civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻaka āṭcittalivarum tūya tamiḻarumāṉa tiruvāḷar va. cuppiyā piḷḷiyavarkaḷiṉ viruppattiṟ
kiṇaṅki, ic ciṟu nūli eḻutalāṉēṉ.
vaḻakkaṟṟa viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ iṟutiyiṟ kuṟippiṭap
paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. in nūlil iṭampeṟāta tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭu
kaḷi evarēṉum eḻutiyaṉuppiṉ, avi naṉṟiyaṟivoṭu aṭutta patippiṟ cērttuk koḷḷappeṟum.
cēlam,

tamiḻnāṭṭu 
viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ





1
iḷiñar pakkam
1. āṇpāṟ pakuti
ēṟattāḻa 5 akavi mutal 25 akavi variyuḷḷōr āṭum viḷiyāṭṭut tokuti iḷiñar pakkam ākum. 
(akavi = vayatu.)
(1) pakalāṭṭu
1. kōli
(1) pāṇṭināṭṭu muṟi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : kallāluṅ kaṇṇāṭiyālum iyaṉṟa ciṟṟuruṇṭikaḷit teṟittum uruṭṭiyum āṭum āṭṭu, kōli eṉappaṭum. (kōli = uruṇṭi).
āṭuvār toki : ciṟuvaruḷḷum iḷiñaruḷḷum, perumpāṉmi iruvarum ciṟupāṉmi mēṟpaṭṭavarum iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu ēṟattāḻa nālaṭit tolivil, akalaḷavāṉa vāyum ōraṅkula āḻamumuḷḷaṉavāka, variciyāy nilattiṟ killappaṭṭa mūṉṟu kuḻikaḷum, āṭakaṉ ovvoruvaṉukkum ovvoru kōliyum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām. kōliyāṭṭiṉ ēṉi muṟikaṭkuriya kuḻiyum, iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa aḷaviṉatē.
āṭiṭam : akaṉṟa muṟṟamum puṟakkaṭiyum poṭṭalum iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : āṭakar, kuḻivaricikku nērāṉa iru ticikaḷuḷ oṉṟil, kaṭicik kuḻikku mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku kacat toliviṟ kīṟappaṭṭa kōṭṭil niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoruvaṉāyt taṉ taṉ kōliyi ak kuḻinōkki uruṭṭal vēṇṭum. kuḻikkuḷ vīḻttiyavar muṉṉum, vīḻttātavar piṉṉum, āṭal vēṇṭum. oruvarum kuḻikkuḷ vīḻttāviṭiṉ, kuḻikku neruṅka uruṭṭiyavar muṉṉum, ataṟkaṭutta aṇmikku uruṭṭiyavar piṉṉum āṭal vēṇṭum. āṭuvār palarāyiṉ, iṅṅaṉamē aṇmi cēymi muṟippaṭi muṉṉum piṉṉum āṭal vēṇṭum.
mutalil āṭuvāṉ kuḻikkuḷ vīḻttātavaṉāyiṉ, taṉ kōliyik kuḻikkuḷ teṟittu vīḻttiyapiṉ, ataṟkaṭutta naṭukkuḻikkuḷḷum, ataṉpiṉ ataṟkaṭutta etirppakka iṟutik kuḻikkuḷḷum, piṉpu toṭarntu muṉṉum piṉṉumāka aṭuttaṭutta kuḻikkuḷḷum, pattām eṇvari muṉpōṉṟē vīḻttavēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam vīḻttuvataṟku nāṉkumuṟi muṉṉum piṉṉumākat tici tirumpa nērum. pattām vīḻttu naṭuk kuḻikkuḷ nikaḻum. ataṉpiṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyit taṉ kōliyāl teṟittu aṭittuviṭiṉ kelippākum. pala etirikaḷāyiṉ av aṉivar kōliyiyum aṭittal vēṇṭum.
mutalil āṭuvāṉ ētēṉum oru kuḻikkuḷ vīḻttat tavaṟiṉ, etiri āṭal vēṇṭum. etiriyum tavaṟiṉ mutalāvāṉ āṭal vēṇṭum.  iṅṅaṉam tavaṟuntoṟum āṭakaṉ māṟuvāṉ.
oruvaṉ āṭumpōtu etiriyiṉ kōli arukiliruppiṉ, atu aṭutta muṟi kuḻikkuḷ vīḻvatit taṭukkumāṟum, ataṉ aṭiyiṉiṉṟu tappumāṟum, ataṉi aṭittut toliviṟ pōkkiviṭuvatu vaḻakkam. āṭṭiṉ iṭiyilāyiṉum iṟutiyilāyiṉum etiriyiṉ kōliyi aṭikkat tavaṟiṉ, etiri āṭal vēṇṭum. etiriyiṉ kōli tolivilirukkumpōtu       ati aṭikkum āṟṟal allatu uṟutiyillāviṭiṉ, taṉ kōliyic caṟṟē muṉtaḷḷi aṭutta muṟi etiriyiṉ aṭikkut tappumāṟu ceyvatumuṇṭu.
oruvaṉ tavaṟi maṟṟoruvaṉ āṭumpōtu, āṭṭiṉ toṭakkattil āṭiya muṟippaṭiyē āṭal vēṇṭum.
āṭuvār iruvarāyiṉum palarāyiṉum tōṟpavaṉ oruvaṉē. palarāyiṉ, iṟutiyil tōṟpavaṉoḻinta ēṉiyarellārum kelikkumvari āṭṭut toṭarum.
āṭṭu muṭintapiṉ, tōṟṟavaṉ kelittavariṭam muṭṭu vāṅkal vēṇṭum. tōṟṟavaṉ taṉ muṭṭik kiyi, iru kaṇuviṟkum iṭippaṭṭa pakuti kelittavarkku etirākat tōṉṟumāṟu, nilattil ūṉṟi vittukkoṇṭirukka, kelittavar tam kōliyāl avaṉ muṭṭiyil aṭippar. itu muṭṭuppōṭutal eṉappaṭum. potuvāka mūṉṟu muṭṭu aṭippatu vaḻakkam. muṭṭup pōṭuvatuṭaṉ ōr āṭṭi muṭiyum. aṭutta āṭṭi āṭuvatu āṭakariṉ viruppattip poṟuttatu. oru viḷiyāṭṭi oru muṟi āṭi muṭippatu ōr āṭṭi eṉappaṭum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : orukāl, arukiluḷḷa paḷḷattiliruk kum kāṭṭuppaṟaviyik kiyil villillāviṭattu viralkoṇṭu kallāl teṟikkum vēṭṭi viṉiyiṉiṉṟu, iv āṭṭut tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : viral narampu uraṅkoḷvatum, kuṟi tappāmal teṟittaṭikkap payilvatum, iv āṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
(2) cōḻa koṅkunāṭṭu muṟi
cōḻa koṅku nāṭukaḷiṟ potuvākak kōliyik kuṇṭu allatu kōlikkuṇṭu eṉṟum, kōliyāṭṭattik kuṇṭāṭṭam eṉṟum kūṟuvar. av āṭṭam, pēntā, añcalakuñcam, irukuḻiyāṭṭam, mukkuḻiyāṭṭam mutaliya pala vakippaṭum.
1. pēntā
(i) caturap pēntā
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pēntā eṉṉum nīḷcaturak           kōṭṭiṟkuḷ uruṭṭiyāṭuṅ kōliyāṭṭup pēntā eṉap         paṭum. iti vaṭṭap pēntāvuṭaṉ oppunōkkic caturap pēntā eṉpar. itu koṅkunāṭṭil ciṟappāy viḷiyāṭappaṭuvatu paṟṟi, tirucci rāppaḷḷi vaṭṭārattil oru cilar iti īrōṭṭup pēntā eṉpatumuṇṭu.
āṭuvār toki : ciṟāruḷḷum iḷiñaruḷḷum iruvar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : ēṟattāḻa ōraṭi nīḷamum mukkālaṭi akalamum nīṭṭuppōkkil naṭukkōṭum uḷḷa oru nīḷcaturakkōṭum, āṭuvāṉ ovvoruvaṉukkum ovvoru kōliyum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām. peruṅ kōliyum kallumāyiṉ, teṟittaṭikkap paṭāmal uruṭṭiyaṭikkappaṭum. pēntāviṟku ēṟattāḻap pattaṭit tolivil ōraṭi nīḷam oru kuṟuṅkōṭu kīṟappeṟum. atu utti yeṉappaṭum.
āṭiṭam : āṭiṭam pāṇṭi nāṭṭuk kōliyāṭṭiṟkup pōṉṟē itaṟkum.
āṭumuṟi : āṭakar iruvarum, uttiyeṉṉum kōṭṭiṉ mēl niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoruvaṉāyk kōliyip pēntānōkki uruṭṭal vēṇṭum. pēntāviṟku uḷḷē allatu arukē yār kōli niṉṟatō, avaṉ mēṟkoṇṭu āṭal vēṇṭum. pēntāviṟku veḷiyē allatu mika veḷiyē yār kōli niṉṟatō, avaṉ taṉ kōliyip pēntāviṉ naṭukkōṭṭu naṭuvil vittal vēṇṭum.
āṭum valiyuṭiyavaṉ mīṇṭum uttiyil niṉṟu         koṇṭu taṉ kōliyip pēntānōkki uruṭṭal vēṇṭum. kōli pēntāviṟkuḷḷēṉum pēntāviṉiṉṟu nālviraṟ kiṭikkuḷḷēṉum pōy niṟpiṉ tavaṟām. ataṉpiṉ          aṭuttavaṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamaṉṟip pēntāviṉiṉṟu nālviraṟkiṭikkappāṟ pōy niṟpiṉ, pēntāviṟ kuḷḷiruk kuṅ kōliyi aṭittal vēṇṭum. avvāṟu aṭitta piṉ, aṭikkappaṭṭa kōliyaṉṟi aṭitta kōli pēntāviṟkuḷḷum pēntāviṉiṉṟu nālviraṟ kiṭikkuḷḷum niṟṟal kūṭātu; niṟpiṉ tavaṟām. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavaṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamaṉṟip pēntāviṉiṉṟu nālviraṟ kiṭikkappāṟ pōy niṟpiṉ, aṭittavaṉ mīṇṭum uttikkuc ceṉṟu ataṉmēl niṉṟukoṇṭu, etiriyiṉ kōliyi aṭittal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam aṭikkumuṉ, etiriyiṉ kōli pēntā viṟku veḷiyil niṟpiṉ, pēntā cettu āṟu mācam eṉṟu kūṟutal vēṇṭum; allākkāl tavaṟām. tavaṟiya pōtellām āṭakaṉ māṟal vēṇṭum.
aṭippavaṉ mūṉṟām muṟi uttiyil niṉṟu aṭikkum pōtu, etiriyiṉ kōliyi mūṉṟu taram toṭarntu aṭittal vēṇṭum. ataṉāl etiriyiṉ kōli pēntāviṟkut toliviṟ pōkkappaṭuvatuṇṭu.
mūṉṟām muṟi uttiyil niṉṟu aṭikkumpōtu, etiriyiṉ kōli pēntāviṟkuḷ illākkāl, aṭikkuṅ kōli pēntāviṟkuṭ ceṉṟu niṟpiṉum kuṟṟamilli.
oruvaṉ pēntāviṉ arukiṟpōy niṉṟa taṉ kōliyāl etiriyiṉ kōliyi aṭikkumpōtu, pēntāmēṟ kipaṭum eṉṟu aṭippavaṉ muntic colliṉum, pēntāmēṟ kipaṭakkūṭātu eṉṟu aṭikkappaṭum kōlikkāraṉ muntic colliṉum, avvāṟē niṟivēṟṟappaṭal vēṇṭum.
aṭikkappaṭum kōli oru paḷḷattiliruppiṉ, aṭippavaṉ nēr eṉṟu colvāṉ. uṭaṉē, aṭikkappaṭum kōliyi etiri eṭuttu aṭippavaṉukku etirākap paḷḷanērukkuc camanilattil vittal vēṇṭum.
aṭikkappaṭum kōli ōr iṭukkiliruppiṉ aṭippavaṉ cāṇcūṭi caṭāpuṭā eṉṟu colvāṉ. uṭaṉē etiri ati eṭuttu aṭippavaṉukkut teriyumpaṭi vittal vēṇṭum. cāṇcūṭi caṭāpuṭā illi eṉṟu etiri muntic colliṭiṉ, ati eṭuttu vikka vēṇṭuvatilli.
oruvaṉ taṉ kōliyāl aṭikkumpōtu, ati vaḻiccelvōr yārēṉum taṭuttuviṭiṉ, aṭippavaṉ taṭuttāl tūr eṉṟu colvāṉ. ataṉpiṉ, avvaḻic celvōr tam kālāl atit taḷḷumpaṭi vēṇṭappaṭuvar.
aṭikkappaṭum kōli uttikkuk kiṭṭē irukkum pōtu ataṟkuriyavaṉ avviṭattiṉiṉṟu uttikkut tāṇṭiviṭiṉ, aṭippavaṉ taṉ kōliyit taṉ kaṇṇil vittāvatu uyarttiya kiyil vittāvatu etiriyiṉ kōliyiṉ mēliṭṭaṭittal vēṇṭum. av aṭi tavaṟiṉ, mīṇṭum nilamaṭṭattiṟ kiyāl teṟittaṭikkalām. av vaṭiyum tavaṟiṉ tōlviyām.
mūṉṟām muṟi uttiyil niṉṟu aṭippavaṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyit toṭarntu mūṉṟu taram aṭittuviṭiṉ etiri taṉ kōliyit taṉ muṭṭikkiyāl oru taṭavi allatu pala taṭavi taḷḷip pēntāviṟkuḷ koṇṭuvantu niṟuttal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam niṟuttiviṭiṉ, avaṉē kelittavaṉāvaṉ; allākkāl tōṟṟavaṉāvaṉ. muṭṭikkiyāl kōliyit taḷḷuvati muṭṭitaḷḷal eṉpar.
(ii) vaṭṭappēntā
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pēntā eṉappaṭum vaṭṭak kōṭṭiṟkuḷ uruṭṭiyāṭuṅ kōliyāṭṭu vaki vaṭṭap pēntāvām. itu caturap pēntāvuṭaṉ oppunōkki vaṭṭap pēntā eṉappaṭṭatu.
āṭuvār toki : mūvar mutal ettuṇip pērāyiṉum iti āṭalām. āyiṉum, aivarkkuk kuṟiyātum patiṉ markkuk kūṭātum iruppiṉ ciṟappām.
āṭukaruvi : āṭakaruḷ ovvoruvaṉukkum pala kōlikaḷ iruttal vēṇṭum. avaṟṟōṭu tel eṉṉum oru peruṅkōli ovvoruvaṉiṭattumāvatu ellārkkum potuvākavāvatu iruttal vēṇṭum.
ēṟattāḻa mukkaca viṭṭamuḷḷa oru vaṭṭakkōṭu kīṟappaṭum. ataṉ naṭuvil oru kuḻi killappaṭum. vaṭṭattiṉ aṭiyil kāl vaṭṭam amiyumāṟu oru kuṟukkuk kōṭum kīṟappaṭum.
kāl vaṭṭamuḷḷa pakkattil, vaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu ēṟattāḻa mukkacat tolivil, utti kīṟappaṭum. ataṟku mēlum iru pakkattum kaviyumāṟu oru talikīḻāṉa pakarakkōṭu iṭappaṭum. atu mūṭi eṉappaṭum. ati iṉṟu ṭāppu (Top) eṉṉum āṅkilac collāṟ kuṟippar.
āṭiṭam : muṟṟamum puṟakkaṭiyum poṭṭalum veḷi nilamum iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : āṭakarellārum mutaṉ mutal uttiyil niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoruvaṉāyt taṉ taṉ kōliyi vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa kuḻiyi nōkki uruṭṭal vēṇṭum. yār kōli kuḻikku aṇṇaṇittāy (atāvatu mika neruṅki) niṟkiṉṟatō, avaṉ muṉṉum; ataṟku aṭuttu niṟkuṅ kōlikkāraṉ piṉṉum; ataṟkaṭuttu niṟkuṅ kōlikkāraṉ ataṉ piṉṉumāka; iṅṅaṉam kuḻiyaṇmi varicippaṭi ellārum āṭal vēṇṭum. kōliyi uruṭṭumpōtu oruvaṉ kōli iṉṉoruvaṉati aṭittuviṭṭāl, ellār kōliyum mīṇṭum ovvoṉṟāy uruṭṭappaṭum. kōlikaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu aṭipaṭuvatu caṭapuṭā eṉappaṭum.
āṭuvār varici yoḻuṅku ivvāṟu tuṇiyappaṭṭa piṉ, munti yāṭukiṟavaṉiṭam ēṉiyōrellārum āḷukkoru kōli koṭuttuviṭal vēṇṭum. avaṉ avaṟṟuṭaṉ taṉ kōliyiyuñ cērttu, ellāvaṟṟiyum mottamāy vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ uruṭṭuvāṉ. ataṉpiṉ, tellāl (atāvatu kuṇṭāl) mukkāl vaṭṭattiluḷḷa kōlikaḷuḷ etiyēṉum evaṟṟiyēṉum aṭippāṉ. kāl vaṭṭattuḷ uḷḷavaṟṟi aṭittal kūṭātu; aṭippiṉ, paccāvām. ataṟku oru kōli pōṭṭuviṭa vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, aṭuttavaṉ āṭuvāṉ.
uruṭṭuvāṉ yārāyiṉum, mūṭiyi mitikkavāvatu tāṇṭavāvatu kūṭātu. aṅṅaṉañ ceyyiṉ tavaṟiyavaṉāvaṉ. ataṉāl aṭuttavaṉ āṭa nērum. mūṭi potuvāka mutaliṟ pōṭappaṭuvatilli. palamuṟi colliyuṅ kēḷātu, oruvaṉ uttikku muṉṉāṟ kāl vittu muṉpuṟamākac cāyntu aṭippiṉ, mūṭi pōṭappaṭum. atu pōṭappaṭṭa piṉ ati mitippiṉ allatu tāṇṭiṉ paccā eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. ataṟku oru kāy pōṭṭu viṭavēṇṭum.
uruṭṭappaṭṭa kōlikaḷuḷ ētēṉum oṉṟu nērē kuḻiyil viḻuntuviṭiṉ, veṟṟiyām. avaṉ kāykaḷi aṭikkat tēviyilli. uruṭṭiṉavaṉ tirumpavum āṭalām.
mukkāl vaṭṭattuḷ uḷḷa kōliyi allatu kōlikaḷi aṭippiṉ, aṭipaṭṭa kōliyaṉittum vaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu veḷiyēṟiviṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam veḷiyēṟiviṭiṉ atu oru veṟṟiyām. kuḻiyiṟ pōṭṭālum kāyaṭittālum, ēṉiyōrellārum kelittavaṉukku ovvoru kāy koṭuttuviṭal vēṇṭum. kuḻiyuḷ pōṭṭuk kāyum aṭittu viṭiṉ iraṭṭik kelippātaliṉ, ēṉiyōr ivviru kāy koṭuttuviṭal vēṇṭum. mukkāl vaṭṭattuḷ uḷḷa kāykaḷi aṭikkumpōtu, kuḻik kāyiyum aṭikkalām; veḷik kāyiyum aṭikkalām. veṉṟavaṉ eṉṟum tirumpiyāṭuvāṉ.
kuḻiyilum pōṭāmal mukkāl vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ uḷḷa kāyiyum aṭikkāmal pōṉavaṉ, taṉ āṭṭatti iḻantu viṭuvāṉ. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavaṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. aṉṟu oruvarum kāy pōṭuvatilli.
aṭitta kāyāvatu aṭikkappaṭṭa kāyāvatu vaṭṭatti viṭṭu veḷiyē pōkāviṭiṉ, paccā eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. ataṟku oru        kōli aṭuttavaṉiṭam koṭuttuviṭṭu, āṭṭatti viṭṭuviṭal vēṇṭum. kuḻiyiṟ pōṭṭaviṭattum aṭitta kāy allatu aṭikkappaṭṭa kāy veḷiyēṟāviṭiṉ, iraṭṭip paccāvām. aṉṟu, aṭittavaṉ iru kāy koṭuttuviṭṭu āṭṭatti          viṭṭuviṭal vēṇṭum. aṭuttavaṉ avaṟṟiyuñ cērttu  uruṭṭuvāṉ. avaṉ kelittuviṭiṉ, ap paccāk kāykaḷiyum vaḻakkappaṭi koṭukkiṟavaṟṟiyum eṭuttuk koḷḷalām; kelikkāviṭiṉ paccāk kāykaḷ paḻaṅkāykaḷuṭaṉ cērttu uruṭṭappeṟum. evaṉ eṅṅaṉam kelippiṉum, paḻaṅkāykaḷi orupōtum kelippuk kāyākap peṟal muṭiyātu. āṭṭam muṭiyum vari, avi oru mutalpōl iruntukoṇṭē yirukkum. āṭṭam muṭintapiṉ, avaṉavaṉ mutaṟkāy avaṉavaṉic cērum.
oruvaṉ kelittaviṭattu iṉṉoruvaṉukkup pōṭak kāyillāviṭṭāl, avaṉ kaṭaṉākavāvatu vilikkāvatu piṟaṉiṭam vāṅkikkoḷḷalām; allākkāl, avaṉ mutalil iṭṭa kāyi iḻantuviṭuvāṉ.
ellārum āṭi muṭintapiṉ, oruvariṭamāvatu palariṭamāvatu kāy mikutiyāyc cērntirukkum. avaṟṟuḷ avaravar contak kāykku mēṟpaṭṭavellām kelippuk kāyākum.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : kōliyāṭṭiṉ potuppayaṉāka muṟkūṟappaṭṭavaṟṟoṭu, peruntokiyāṉa kōlikaḷi eḷitāy īṭṭikkoḷvatum, ciṟu mutaliyiṭṭup pērūtiyam peṟum karuttuṟavum, iv āṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
II. añcala kuñcam
āṭṭiṉ peyar : aintām eṇṇic collum pōtu añcala kuñcam eṉṟu collappaṭum kōliyāṭṭu vaki, at toṭarmoḻiyiyē peyarākak koṇṭatu. itaṟku orē kuḻiyuḷḷamiyāl, itu oṟṟik kuḻiyāṭṭam eṉavumpaṭum.
āṭuvār toki : ciṟuvaruḷḷum iḷiñaruḷḷum iruvar iti āṭuvar.
āṭiṭam : pēntāviṟkuriyatē itaṟkum.
āṭukaruvi : pāṇṭināṭṭuk kōliyāṭṭiṟkuk kūṟiya aḷavuḷḷa oṟṟik kuḻiyum, ovvoruvaṉukkum ovvoru kōliyum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām.
āṭumuṟi : āṭakar, kuḻikku 8 aṭi allatu 10 aṭit toliviluḷḷa uttik kōṭṭiṉmēl niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoruvaṉāyt taṉ taṉ kōliyi uruṭṭal vēṇṭum.
oruvaṉatu kōli mutalilēyē kuḻikkuḷ viḻuntuviṭṭāl oṉpatu eṉṉum eṇṇām. ataṉ piṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyi aṭittuviṭṭāl pattām. atu paḻamākum. atōṭu ōr āṭṭi muṭiyum.
mutaliṟ kuḻikkuḷ viḻāviṭṭāl, kuḻikkuk kiṭṭa irukkiṟa kōlikkāraṉ muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum. avaṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyi aṭikkalām; allatu kuḻikkuṭ pōṭalām. etiriyiṉ kōliyit tavaṟātu aṭittuviṭiṉ aintām; aṅṅaṉamiṉṟik kuḻikkuṭ pōṭṭuviṭiṉ nāṉkām.
nāṉkām eṇṇiliruntu toṭaṅkiṉum aintām eṇṇiliruntu toṭaṅkiṉum, pattām eṇvari toṭartal vēṇṭum. cila eṇkaṭkuk kuḻiyum cila eṇkaṭku aṭiyum ākum. kuḻi eṉpatu kuḻikku aṭittal. nāṉkām eṇṇiliruntu toṭaṅkiṉ 5, 8, 9, 10 eṉpaṉa aṭiyām; 6, 7 eṉpaṉa kuḻiyām; aintām eṇṇiliruntu toṭaṅkiṉ 6, 7 eṉpaṉa kuḻiyām; 8, 9, 10 eṉpaṉa aṭiyām. pattāvatu, kōliyi aṭittaṟkup patilākak kuḻikkuḷ aṭippiṉ, mīṇṭum nāṉkām.
kuḻikkuḷ aṭikkum pōtum kāyi (atāvatu etiriyiṉ kōliyi) aṭikkumpōtum tavaṟiviṭiṉ, aṭuttavaṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. āṭumpōtu, aintām eṇ mutal pattām eṇvari ovvōr eṇṇiṟkum ovvoru maraput toṭarmoḻi kūṟikkoḷvatuṇṭu. avi,
añcala kuñcam,
aṟuvakka tāṭi,
eḻuvakka miṉā,
eṭṭacuk kōṭṭi, (eṭṭōṭaṭṭak kōṭṭi)
tompaṉip pēṭṭi, (tomparap pēṭṭi, tompalappāṭi).
tocukkaṭṭurācā, (tēciṅkurācā, campāviḻuntatu) eṉpaṉavām.
pattām aṭi tavaṟātu muntiyaṭittavaṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyi mūṉṟu taṭavi aṭittut taḷḷa vēṇṭum. aṭittut taḷḷappaṭṭa kōlikkāraṉ muṭṭi taḷḷa vēṇṭum; atāvatu taṉ kōliyit taṉ muṭṭik kiyāl kuṟippiṭṭa taṭavi taḷḷikkoṇṭu vantu kuḻikkuḷ vīḻttavēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam vīḻttiviṭiṉ kelippām; illāviṭiṉ tōṟpām.
pattām aṭi tavaṟātu aṭittavaṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyi aṭittut taḷḷumpōtu, etiri taṉ kōli neṭun toliviṟkup pōy viṭātavāṟu taṉ muṭṭik kiyāl atit taṭukkalām; kālāl taṭuttal kūṭātu; kālāl taṭuppiṉ 7 taṭavi muṭṭi taḷḷal vēṇṭum.
muṭṭi taḷḷukiṟavaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa taṭavi taḷḷiyum taṉ kōliyik kuḻikkuṭ koṇṭu vantu niṟutta muṭiyāviṭiṉ, aṭittavaṉ ati mīṇṭum aṭittup pōkkuvāṉ. muṭṭi taḷḷiṉavaṉ mīṇṭum muṭṭi taḷḷuvāṉ. iṅṅaṉam nāṉku muṟi aṭippum taḷḷum nikaḻalām. ovvoru muṟikkum aṭippiṟkum taḷḷiṟkum kaṇakkuṇṭu.
mutaṉmuṟi	3 aṭi	3 taḷḷu
2ām    	2    	2    
3ām    	1    	1    
4ām    	1    	3    
muṭṭi taḷḷukiṟavaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa taṭavi taḷḷiyum taṉ kōliyik kuḻikkuṭ koṇṭuvantu niṟutta muṭiyāviṭiṉ, kīḻvarumāṟu oru valakkārattik kiyāḷvatuṇṭu. avaṉatu         kōli aṭikka muṭiyāta tolivilirukkumpōtu, aṭikkiṟa         vaṉ taṉakku vacatiyuṇṭākumāṟu mēluñ caṟṟut taḷḷac           colvāṉ. appōtu taḷḷukiṟavaṉ, aṭikkiṟavaṉukkut teriyātavāṟu taṉ kōliyi oru kikkuḷiṭṭu maṟittu iru kikaḷiyum muṭṭiyāka vittukkoṇṭu, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟic caṟṟu muṉṉāka eṭuttu vittup pōtumā? eṉṟu kēṭpāṉ. aṭikkiṟavaṉ pōtātu eṉṟu colliṉ, taḷḷukiṟavaṉ iṉṉoru muṭṭik kiyi muntiṉatiṉum caṟṟu muṉpāka eṭuttu vittup pōtumā? eṉṟu kēṭpāṉ. aṭikkiṟavaṉ mīṇṭum pōtātu eṉṟu colliṉ, taḷḷukiṟavaṉ mīṇṭum piṉṉāka irukkum kiyi muṉṉāka eṭuttu vippāṉ. aṭikkiṟavaṉ kōlik ki etuveṉṟum veṟuṅki etuveṉṟum teriyāmal, pōtātu, pōtātu eṉṟu mēlum mēlum collac collat, taḷḷukiṟavaṉ taṉ iru kikaḷiyum māṟi māṟi muṉṉāka eṭuttu vittu, iṟutiyil kōlik kiyik kuḻimēl vittukkoṇṭu, pōtumā? eṉṟu kēṭpāṉ. aṭikkiṟavaṉ kuḻik kiyi veṟuṅki eṉṟu karutikkoṇṭu, pōtum eṉpāṉ; uṭaṉē taḷḷukiṟavaṉ taṉ kōliyik kuḻikkuḷ iṭṭuviṭṭut taṉ kiyi eṭuttuviṭuvāṉ; aṭittavaṉ ēmāṟip pōvāṉ; taḷḷiṉavaṉ kelittuviṭuvāṉ. itaṟku mīṉ piṭittal eṉṟu peyar.
taḷḷukiṟavaṉ mīṉ piṭikkalāmā piṭikkak kūṭātā eṉpatu, muṭṭi taḷḷu muṉṉarē muṭivu ceyyappeṟum.
III. irukuḻiyāṭṭam (iṣṭam)
āṭṭiṉ peyar : cuvaraṭi araṅkiṟkuḷ iru kuḻi vittāṭum kōliyāṭṭu vaki irukuḻiyāṭṭam eṉappaṭum. itaṟku iṭṭam (iṣṭam) allatu kicēpi eṉṟum peyar.







iv āṭṭu perumpālum tiruccirāppaḷḷi vaṭṭārattil āṭappeṟuvatākum.
āṭuvārtoki : ciṟāruḷḷum iḷiñaruḷḷum iruvar allatu (iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa) palar iti āṭuvar. ōr āṭṭiyil iruvarē iti āṭa muṭiyumātalāl, palar āṭuvatāyiṉ ivviruvarākavē āṭakkūṭum.
āṭukaruvi : cuvaraṭi (allatu mēṭṭaṭi) nilattil, ēṟattāḻa 
3 aṭi nīḷamum 2 aṭi akalamumuḷḷa oru nīḷ catura araṅkamiyumāṟu ōr akaṉṟa pakarakkōṭu kīṟik koḷḷal vēṇṭum. av varaṅkiṉ neṭumpakkaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu cuvaraṭiyāyirukkum vaṇṇam, pakarattiṉ iru pakkak kōṭṭu muṉikaḷum cuvarit toṭutal vēṇṭum. av varaṅkiṟkum iṭavalamāka iru kuḻikaḷ killal vēṇṭum.
araṅkiṟku ēṟattāḻa mukkacat tolivil ōraṭi nīḷam utti kīṟappaṭum. ataṟku ṭāppu (Top) eṉṉum mūṭi iṭappaṭum.
oruvaṉ āṭumpōtu iṉṉoruvaṉukkuk kōli tēviyāyirāmiyiṉ, āṭakar ellārkkum potuvāka mukkōli yiruntāṟ pōtum. avaṟṟuḷ, iraṇṭu uruṭṭuvaṉa; oṉṟu avaṟṟi aṭippatu. aṭikkuṅ kōli caṟṟup peritākavirukkum. ataṟkut tiruccirāppaḷḷip pāṅkaril kuṭṭu allatu tel eṉṟu peyar.
āṭiṭam : cuvaraṭi nilamum mēṭṭaṭi nilamum iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : tōṟṟavaṉ kelittavaṉukku ivvaḷavu kācu koṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉṟu muṉṉatāka muṭivu ceytu koṇṭu, uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉpaṭiyō tiruvuḷac cīṭṭiṉpaṭiyō yārēṉum oruvaṉ muntiyāṭuvaṉ. āṭuvār ellārkkum kōli potuvāyiṉ, kōlikkuc contakkāraṉ muntiyāṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
āṭukiṟavaṉ uttiyil niṉṟukoṇṭu, mutalāvatu iru         ciṟu kōlikaḷiyum araṅkiṟkuḷ uruṭṭi, avaṟṟuḷ eti          aṭikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu etiriyik kēṭpāṉ. av          iraṇṭiṉuḷ āṭukiṟavaṉukkuk kiṭṭa iruppati aṭikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉ kīḻ eṉṟum, caṟṟu eṭṭa (atāvatu tolivil) iruppati aṭikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉ mēl eṉṟum, etiri colvāṉ. kīḻ eṉpatiṟkup patilāka etir eṉṟum colvatuṇṭu. āṭukiṟavaṉ etiri coṉṉa kōliyi aṭittuviṭṭāṟ kelittavaṉāvaṉ. avaṉukkup pēciṉapaṭi etiri kācik koṭuttuviṭal vēṇṭum. mutalil eṟiyappaṭṭa iru kōlikaḷum iruviralukkumēl iṭiyiṭṭirup piṉ, etiri coṉṉatittāṉ aṭittal vēṇṭum; iruviraṟku uṭpaṭṭiruppiṉ, etiyum aṭikkalām. iṅṅaṉam viruppamāṉati aṭikkuṅ kāraṇampaṟṟiyē,  iv āṭṭiṟku  iṭṭam allatu kicēpi eṉṟu peyar.
iṭiyīṭu iruviraṟ kuṭpaṭṭa niliyil etiyum aṭikkumpōtu iraṇṭilum paṭṭuviṭṭāl, paccā eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. atōṭu, aṭitta kōliyāyiṉum aṭikkappaṭṭa kōliyāyiṉum kuḻikkuḷ vīḻntuviṭiṉ, iraṭṭippaccā eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām.  kuṟṟamellām tōlvittāṉamē.
mutalil eṟinta iru kōlikaḷum araṅkiṟku uḷ vīḻiṉum veḷi vīḻiṉum, mūṉṟāṅ kōli nērē kuḻikkuḷ viḻuntuviṭiṉ, eṟintavaṉukku etiri pēciṉa tokiyi iraṭṭippāyk kaṭṭiviṭal vēṇṭum.
mutalil eṟinta iru kōlikaḷuḷ oṉṟu araṅkiṟku veḷiyē niṟpiṉ, etiri ati eṭuttu araṅkuk kōṭṭiṉmēl vippāṉ; allatu muṭṭikkiyāl araṅkiṟkuḷ taḷḷiviṭu vāṉ.  ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉataṟku mūṭṭutal eṉṟu peyar.
aṭikkuṅ kōli mutal vīḻvilēyē araṅkiṟkuḷ viḻuntu viṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi ataṟku veḷiyē vīḻiṉ, atu veḷimaṭṭu eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamātaliṉ āṭiṉavaṉ tōṟṟa vaṉāvaṉ.
mūṉṟāṅ kōli (atāvatu aṭikkuṅ kōli) araṅkiṟkuḷ kāyi aṭikkātu kōṭṭiṉmēl niṟpiṉ, kōṭu eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. ati liṉ (line) allatu lāk (lock) eṉṉum āṅkilac  collāṟ kuṟippar.
uttiyil niṉṟu aṭittavaṉ ativiṭṭu nīṅkumpōtu, mūṭiyi mitikkātu ōreṭṭup piṉvittu iṭamākavāvatu valamākavāvatu cuṟṟi muṉvaral vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi mūṭiyi mitittuviṭiṉ tavaṟiṉavaṉāvaṉ.
āṭukiṟavaṉ aṭitta kōliyum iṉṉoṉṟum araṅkiṟ kuḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟu toṭṭukkoṇṭu niṟpiṉ, avaṉ avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi iṉṉoṉṟu alukkātavāṟu eṭuttal vēṇṭum. alukkiviṭiṉ tōṟṟavaṉāvaṉ. aluṅkāmal eṭuttaṟkāka iraṇṭiṟkum iṭiyil ciṟitu maṇṇit tūvuvatu vaḻakkam. āṭṭiṟ kelittavaṉ maṟu āṭṭiyil muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum. āṭakar palarāyiṉ, tōṟṟavaṉ nīṅki vēṟoruvaṉ etiriyāvaṉ.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : kuṟitappāmal uruṭṭiyaṭikkap payilvatum, oṉṟiyoṉṟu toṭṭuniṟkum poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟip piṟitu allatu piṟa alukkātavāṟu eṭukkap paḻakuvatum, iv vāṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
IV. mukkuḻiyāṭṭam
(i) cēlam vaṭṭāra muṟi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : cuvaraṭi yaraṅkiṟkuḷ irukuḻikkup patilāka mukkuḻi vittāṭum kōliyāṭṭa vakiyē mukkuḻiyāṭṭam.
āṭumuṟi : mēṟkūṟiya irukuḻiyāṭṭamum iṅkuk kuṟitta mukkuḻiyāṭṭamum oṉṟē. āyiṉ, iṭa vēṟupāṭu kāraṇamākap piṉvarumāṟu mūvaki vēṟṟumi yuṇṭu.
		tiruccirāppaḷḷi	  cēlam
(1) karuvi :	irukuḻi	mukkuḻi
(2) muṟi :	mūṭṭal orē taḷ	mūṭṭal 3 taḷ vari
(3) peyar :	iṣṭam allatu kicēpi	mukkuḻiyāṭṭam
	veḷimaṭṭu	veḷi ṭippu
	liṉ allatu lākku	kīr allatu kīṟu
2. tel
tellit teṟittu viḷiyāṭuvatu tel.
kōliyum tellum karuvi vakiyālaṉṟi āṭṭu vakiyālum ēṟattāḻa oṉṟē. kōlikkup patilāyt tellukkāyip payaṉpaṭuttuvatē tellāṭṭu. āyiṉum, karuvi vēṟupāṭṭiṟkut takkapaṭi teṟikkum vakiyum vēṟupaṭṭatām. iṭakkic cuṭṭuviraṟkum peruviraṟkum iṭiyil iṭukkuvatu irukaruvikkum potuveṉiṉum, valakkic cuṭṭuviralāl teṟippatu kōlikkum, valakki naṭuviralāl teṟippatu telliṟkum, ciṟappām. cilar valakki mōtira viralit telliṟkup payaṉpaṭuttuvar. kōliyit teṟikkumpōtu valakki yakaṅki muṉṉōkki niṟkum; tellit teṟikkum pōtu atu mēṉōkki niṟkum.
tellut teṟittal pāṇṭināṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭu.
tellukkāy kuṟiñcinilattil iyaṟkiyāy vaḷarum oruvaki marattiṉ viti. tel eṉṉuñcol teṟikkappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruṭ kāraṇattiyuṭiyatu.
3. cillāṅ kuccu
(1) pāṇṭināṭṭu muṟi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : cillāṅkuccu eṉṉum oru ciṟu kucci oru kōlāl taṭṭi āṭum āṭṭu cillāṅkuccu eṉappaṭum. ip peyar ciṟupāṉmi cīyāṅkuccu eṉavum maruvi vaḻaṅkum.
āṭuvār toki : iv viḷiyāṭṭi āṭak kuṟintatu iruvar vēṇṭum. palarāyiṉ uttikaṭṭic camatokiyāṉa irukaṭciyākap pirintukoḷvar.
āṭukaruvi : iruviral mutal aṟuviral vari nīḷamum, iruviral mutal nālviral vari cuṟṟaḷavum, oru nuṉiyil kūrmiyum uḷḷa ōr uruṇṭa kuccum; oru muḻam mutal irumuḻam vari (avaravar kikkēṟṟavāṟu) nīḷamum, iruviral mutal nālviral vari cuṟṟaḷavum, oru taliyiṟ ciṟitu kūrmiyum, uḷḷa ōr uruṇṭa kōlum; iv viḷiyāṭṭiṟkuriya karuvikaḷām. perumpālum kuccum kōlum orē cuṟṟaḷaviṉavāka irukkum. kōlik kampu eṉpar.
āṭuvār iruvarāyiṉum palarāyiṉum, ovvōr iṇiyarkkum ovvoru kōluṅ kuccum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa. cila camiyaṅkaḷil ovvoruvaṉum tāṉ tāṉ payiṉṟa allatu taṉ taṉ kikkēṟṟa karuvikaḷit taṉittaṉi vittukkoḷvatu muṇṭu.
kucci mēṉōkkiya cāyvāka vittu ataṉ kūrnuṉiyiṟ kōlāl taṭṭiyeḻuppumāṟu, kucciṟ pātiyaḷavu nīḷamum ariviral mutal oruviral vari āḻamum uḷḷa oru ciṟu paḷḷam nilattiṟ killappaṭum. kuccip paḷḷattil vittirukkumpōtu, ataṉ aṭippakkam (allatu moṭṭip pakkam) paḷḷattilum, ataṉ nuṉippakkam (allatu kūriya pakkam) veḷiyilum irukkum.
āṭiṭam : arip paṭiccāl (½ furlong) catura allatu nīḷcatura nilappakutiyilāvatu, paranta veḷi nilattilāvatu iv āṭṭu āṭappaṭum.
āṭumuṟi : āṭuvār iruvarāyiṉum palarāyiṉum, āṭumuṟi yoṉṟē. iruvarāyiṉ, iruvarum orē kuḻiyil muṉpiṉṉākavāvatu, vevvēṟu kuḻiyil uṭaṉikaḻvākavāvatu, tam kuccivittu nuṉiyiṟ kōlāl taṭṭiyeḻuppi, atu nilattil viḻumuṉ aṭittu iyaṉṟa tolivu pōkkuvar. yār kuccu mikat toliviṟpōy viḻuntatō avaṉ muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum. eḻumpiya kuccu nilattil viḻumuṉ ati aṭikkat tavaṟiṉum, kuṟinta tolivu kuccip pōkkiṉum, pinti yāṭal vēṇṭum.
viḷiyāṭṭit toṭaṅkupavaṉ, muṉ coṉṉavāṟu kucci yeḻuppi yaṭittu iyaṉṟa tolivu pōkkuvaṉ. eḻumpiya kuccu nilattil viḻumuṉ aṭikkat tavaṟiṉ aṭuttavaṉ aṭikka vēṇṭum. yār aṭippiṉum kucciyaṭittup pōkkiya piṉ, eṭuttaṭikkum niliyil kōlik kuḻiyarukē kiṭatti niṟpaṉ.
iṉṉoruvaṉ (etiri), tolivil, kuccuppōkum ticiyilum atu viḻakkūṭiya iṭattilum niṉṟukoṇṭiruntu, atu nilattil viḻumuṉ atip piṭikka muyalvāṉ. piṭittuviṭiṉ, avaṉ kuccaṭippavaṉākavum, muṉpu aṭittavaṉ ati eṭuppavaṉākavum, māṟavēṇṭum. piṭikka muṭiyāviṭiṉ, kuccu nilattil viḻuntavuṭaṉ atiyeṭuttu, aṭittavaṉ kuḻiyarukē kiṭattiyirukkum kōliṟ paṭumpaṭi yeṟiyavēṇṭum. kōliṟ paṭṭuviṭiṉ, aṉṟum iruvar nilimiyum māṟum. paṭāviṭiṉ, aṭippavaṉ virivāyk kucci yeṭuttu ati muṉpōl aṭittup pōkkuvāṉ. avaṉ ati aṭikkumuṉ kucceṭuppavaṉ vēkamāy ōṭivantu avaṉit toṭṭuviṭiṉ, aṉṟum, iruvar nilimiyum māṟum. toṭāviṭiṉ, kucceṭuttavaṉ muṉpōṟ kucceṭukka vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam, iruvarum virumpiya vari toṭarntu āṭuvar. aṭitta kucci eṭutteṟitalukku eṭuttūṟṟutal eṉṟu peyar.
kuccaṭikkiṟavaṉ evvakiyilum tavaṟātum piṭi koṭātum aṭippiṉ, viḷiyāṭṭi niṟuttumvari ettaṉi muṟiyum toṭarntu aṭikkalām.
pala iṇiyar cērntu āṭiṉ, toṭaṅkuṅ kaṭciyit tīrmāṉikkum āṭṭattil kucci mikat toliviṟ pōkkiya kaṭciyār muntiyāṭuvar; itaṟku, oru kaṭciyār aṉivarum mikat toliviṟ pōkka vēṇṭum eṉṉum yāppuṟavilli. avaruḷ oruvar pōkkiṉum pōtum. muntiyāṭuṅ kaṭciyār aṭippārum pintiyāṭuṅ kaṭciyār eṭuppārumāy, āṭṭan toṭaṅkum.
aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyāruḷ ovvoruvaṉum aṭikkuṅkucci, avvavaṉuṭaṉ uttikaṭṭiya etirkkaṭci iṇiñaṉē eṭuppāṉ. iṇiñaṉukku uttiyāḷ eṉṟu peyar. aṭitta kucci uttiyāḷ uṭaṉē vantu eṭukkāviṭiṉ, aṭittavaṉ atit toṭarntu aṭittu, mikat toliviṟ pōkkuvatumuṇṭu. eṭukkuṅ kaṭciyār ellāruṅ kuccukkaḷi yeṟinta piṉputāṉ, aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyār orē camiyattil mīṇṭum aṭippar.
tavaṟum vakiyum tavaṟātu āṭum vakiyum, iruvar āṭiṉum iru kaṭciyār āṭiṉum oṉṟē. ovvoruvaṉākavō oruṅkēyō aṭikkum kaṭciyār aṉivarum toliyumvari (atāvatu tōṟkumvari), etirkkaṭci nilimi māṟātu. āyiṉ, aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyār toliyat toliya, avaruṭiya iṇiñarāṉa etirkkaṭciyār niṉṟukoṇṭē varuvar. aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyiṉ iṟutiyāḷun tolinta piṉ, irukaṭciyum viṉimāṟum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : iv viḷiyāṭṭu vēṭṭi viṉiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākat terikiṉṟatu. kuṟiñci nilattiluḷḷa kuṟavarāyiṉum, kuṟiñci nilattilum pāli nilattilumuḷḷa vēṭarāyiṉum, piṟarāyiṉum, kāṭṭiluḷḷa kōḻi muyal mutaliya orucār uyirkaḷik kuṟuntaṭikoṇṭē yeṟintu kolvatu vaḻakkam. iv vaḻakkattiṉiṉṟē kōḻiyaṭikkak kuṟuntaṭi vēṇṭumā? eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum eḻuntuḷatu.
oru kuṟavaṉum avaṉ kiyāḷāṉa ciṅkaṉ eṉṉuṅ kuḷuvaṉum kuṟuntaṭikoṇṭu vēṭṭiyāṭiṉ, kuṟavaṉ putar putarāyk kuṟuntaṭiyāl taṭṭip pārppāṉ. oru putariṉiṉṟu tiṭumeṉṟu oru kāṭṭuk kōḻi paṟakkum; allatu oru muyal kutikkum. an noṭiyē, kuṟavaṉ atik kuṟuntaṭiyāl aṭippāṉ; allatu eṟivāṉ. kōḻi allatu muyal aṭipaṭṭuc caṟṟut toliviṟ pōy viḻum. kuḷuvaṉ ōṭippōy ati eṭuttu varuvāṉ. vēṟu nilattiṉiṉṟu iruvar vēṭṭiyāṭac celliṉum ivvakiyē nērum.
ittaki vēṭṭi viṉiyiyē cillāṅ kuccuk kuṟikkiṉṟatu. kucci yaṭippavaṉ atit taṭṭiyeḻuppuvatu, kuṟavaṉ putarit taṭṭik kōḻi muyali yeḻuppuvatu pōṉṟatu. kucci mīṇṭum aṭippatu, av vuyirikaḷik kuṇilāl aṭip patum eṟivatum pōṉṟatu. kuccu toliviṟpōy viḻuvatu, aṭipaṭṭa vuyirikaḷ toliviṟpōy viḻuvatu pōṉṟatu. kucci eṭuppavaṉ ati eṭutteṟivatu, aṭipaṭṭu viḻunta vuyirikaḷik kuḷuvaṉ eṭutteṟivatu allatu eṭuttu varuvatu pōṉṟatu.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : mēleḻum oru poruḷi virintu kuṟitappātu vaṉmiyāy aṭippatum; vāṉiṉṟu viḻum poruḷi atu takāta iṭattil viḻumuṉṉum, tāḻap paṟakkum paṟaviyi atu taṉṉiviṭṭuk kaṭakkumuṉṉum piṭippatum; toliviluḷḷa poruḷik kuṟitappātu oru karuviyāl aṭippatum; oru poruḷit toliviluḷḷa kuṟitta iṭattil eṟivatum; oru kuṟitta iṭattiṟku virintu ōṭuvatum; ākiya viṉippayiṟciyē iv āṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
(2) cōḻa koṅkunāṭṭu muṟi
I. killittāṇṭu
pāṇṭināṭṭuc cillāṅkuccum cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭuk killittāṇṭum oṉṟē. āyiṉum, iṭavēṟupāṭu kāraṇamāka, īriṭa āṭṭiṟkum piṉvarumāṟu cila vēṟṟumikaḷ uḷa:
(1) peyar : cillāṅkuccu eṉpatu killi eṉṟum, kampu eṉpatu tāṇṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum.
pāṇṭi nāṭṭil, kucceṭuppavaṉ kuccu nilattil viḻumuṉ ati antarattiṟ piṭittukkoḷvataṟku oru ciṟappup peyarum vaḻaṅkavilli. cōḻa koṅku nāṭukaḷil atu uttam allatu puṭṭam eṉap peyarpeṟum. itu kīḻvarum kiṭṭippuḷḷiṟkum okkum.
eṭuttu ūṟṟutal eṉṉum pāṇṭināṭṭuk kuṟiyīṭṭiṟku nērāṉa cōḻa koṅkunāṭṭuk kuṟiyīṭu kañcivārttal eṉpatākum.
(2) karuvi : cillāṅkuccu oru kaṭiyil maṭṭuṅ kūrāyirukkum. āyiṉ, killi irukaṭiyuṅ kūrāyirukkum. itu irumuṉiyum aṭittaṟku vacatiyām.
(3) muṟi : iru kaṭciyār āṭuvatāyiṉ, pāṇṭi nāṭṭil aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyār aṉivarum orē camiyattil aṭippar. avaṉavaṉ aṭikkuṅ kucci avaṉavaṉ uttiyāḷē eṭuppaṉ. cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭilō, aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉē oru camiyattil aṭippaṉ. avaṉ aṭitta kucci etirkkaṭciyār ellārum āṭukaḷattil niṉṟu avaruḷ yārēṉum piṭikkalām allatu eṭukkalām. aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉ tolinta piṉ iṉṉoruvaṉ āṭuvaṉ. iṅṅaṉam ovvoruvaṉāka ellārun tolintapiṉ ōr āṭṭi oruvāṟu muṭiyum. iṅṅaṉam āṭuvatu kirikkaṭṭu (cricket) eṉṉum āṅkila viḷiyāṭṭi orupuṭi yottiruppatāl, iv āṭṭi intiyak kirikkaṭṭu eṉa nakiccuviyākak kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam.
pāṇṭināṭṭil, aṭikkappaṭṭa kuccup pōyviḻun  tolivi aḷantu atukoṇṭu veṟṟiyik kaṇippatilli. aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyar aṉivarun tolintapiṉ, etirkkaṭciyār āṭalvēṇṭum. cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭilō, tāṇṭi aḷavukōlāka vittukkoṇṭu, ovvoru muṟiyum aṭikkappaṭṭa kuccup pōyviḻun tolivik karuvi        koṇṭō matippākavō aḷaviṭṭu, kuṟitta toki vanta
vuṭaṉ etirk kaṭciyārik kattik kāvaṭi yeṭukkac ceyvar.         kuṟitta toki varuvataṟku ettuṇippēr aṭittaṉarō, attuṇip pērum kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu kucci mummūṉṟu taṭavi toṭarntaṭittup pōkkiya toliviliruntu, etirkkaṭciyār kattik kāvaṭi kavāṉak kāvaṭi eṉṟu iṭiviṭāmal maṭakki maṭakkic collikkoṇṭu kuḻivari varutal vēṇṭum.  ituvē kattik kāvaṭi yeṭuttal eṉpatu. kattik kāvaṭiyeṭukkumpōtu iṭiyiṟ colli niṟuttiviṭṭāl, muṉpu pōkkiya toliviṉiṉṟu mīṇṭum kucci muṉpōl aṭittuppōkki, atu viḻunta iṭattiliruntu maṟupaṭiyum kattik kāvaṭiyeṭukkac ceyvar. ati yeṭuttu muṭintapiṉ, muṉpu aṭittavarē tirumpavum āṭuvar. ākavē, kattik kāvaṭiyeṭut tal tōṟṟavarkku vitikkum oruvakit taṇṭaṉi yeṉappaṭum.
mutalil aṭitta kaṭciyār aṉivarum aṭittapiṉpum kuṟitta toki varāviṭiṉ, avar tōṟṟavarāvar. āyiṉ, avar kattik kāvaṭiyeṭukka vēṇṭiyatilli. aṭutta āṭṭiyil etirkkaṭciyār aṭittal vēṇṭum. ituvē at tōlviyiṉ viḷivām. ākavē, kattik kāvaṭit taṇṭaṉi eṭukkuṅ kaṭciyārkkaṉṟi aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyārkkilli yeṉpatum aṟiyappaṭum.
pāṇṭināṭṭil eṭukkuṅ kaṭciyārkku av eṭuttalē
yaṉṟi vēṟoru taṇṭaṉiyumilli.
II. kiṭṭippuḷ
kiṭṭippuḷ eṉpatu killittāṇṭiṉ maṟṟoru vakiyē. iraṇṭiṟkumuḷḷa vēṟupāṭāvaṉa :
peyar : killi eṉpatu puḷ eṉṟum, tāṇṭu eṉpatu kiṭṭi eṉṟum peyarpeṟum.
muṟi : killittāṇṭil, kuccu kuḻiyiṉ nīṭṭup pōkkil vittuk kōlāl taṭṭiyeḻuppi yaṭikkap peṟum. kiṭṭip puḷḷilō, kuccuk kuḻiyiṉ kuṟukkē vittu ataṟku naṭṭuk             kuṟukkāka oru kōlip piṭittu atu kiyiṉāl taṭṭiyeṭukkap peṟum. killittāṇṭil, aṭikkappaṭṭa kucci eṭuppavaṉ eṭutteṟintapiṉ, ati aṭippavaṉ enniliyilum         aṭikkalām. āyiṉ, kiṭṭippuḷḷil, atu iyakkattil          (acivil) irukkum pōtē ati aṭittalvēṇṭum. acivu niṉṟu kiṭiyāyk kiṭantapiṉ, ati aṭittal kūṭātu. aṭippiṉ, aṭittavaṉ tavaṟiyavaṉāvaṉ. ataṉāl, eṭutteṟin tavaṉ aṭikkavēṇṭiyirukkum. iyaṅkuṅ kuccu naṭṭukku niṟkumpōtu aṭikkappaṭiṉ, atu killikkuttu eṉappaṭum.
kuccup pōm tolivi aḷappatum kattikkāvaṭi eṭuppatum kiṭṭippuḷḷil illi. piṟavakiyil itu perumpālum cillāṅkucci ottirukkum.
perumpālum ciṟuvarē, avaruḷḷum iruvarē, iv viḷiyāṭṭi āṭuvar. kōlāl kuccit taṭṭi yeḻuppi aṭikkamuṭiyāta iḷañciṟārkkeṉa iv āṭṭu eḻuntatākak kūṟuvar. cēranāṭākiya maliyāḷa nāṭṭil koṭṭippuḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkum āṭṭu, cillāṅkuccic cērntatē.
4. pantu
tuṇiyālum kayiṟṟālum iṟukak kaṭṭappaṭṭa uruṇṭiyi eṟintāṭum āṭṭu, pantu eṉappaṭum. (pantu = uruṇṭiyāṉatu).
I. pēyppantu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pēyttaṉamāka oruvaṉmēloruvaṉ eṟintāṭum pantu, pēyppantu eṉap peyar peṟṟirukkalām.
āṭuvār toki : perumpālum nālvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar āṭuvatē iv viḷiyāṭṭiṟkēṟṟatām. āṭuvār toki perukapperuka iv āṭṭuc ciṟakkum.
āṭukaruvi : āṭuvār ettuṇiyarāyiṉum oru pantē iv āṭṭiṟkuriyatām.
āṭiṭam : poṭṭalum veḷinilamum iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : palar iṭiyiṭṭu niṉṟukoṇṭirukka avaruḷ oruvaṉ panti mēlē pōṭṭup piṭittukkoṇṭu, pantē pantu eṉṟu urakkak kattuvāṉ. piṟar eṉṉa pantu? eṉṟu kēṭpar. avaṉ pēyppantu eṉpāṉ. yār mēlē eṉṟu oruvaṉ kēṭka, avaṉ uṉmēlē eṉṟu collik koṇṭu avaṉmēl vaṉmiyāy eṟivāṉ. atu avaṉmēl paṭṭālum paṭum; paṭātupōṉālum pōm. pantu yār kippaṭṭatō avaṉ ati ōṅki yārmēlum eṟivāṉ. iṅṅaṉam viruppamuḷḷavari māṟi māṟi aṭittu āṭikkoṇṭēyiruppar. yār eṟiyiṉum avaṉuṭiya valimikkut takkavāṟu vaṉmiyāy eṟivatē vaḻakkam.
āṭṭuttōṟṟam : kuraṅkeṟi viḷaṅkāyiṉiṉṟō, pēyc ceyalākak karutappaṭṭa oru pantu vīḻcciyiṉiṉṟō, iv āṭṭut tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
viḷāmarattilirukkum kuraṅkik kallāleṟiya atu viḷaṅkāy koṇṭeṟivatu, kuraṅkeṟi viḷaṅkāyām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : caṟṟut tolivil niṟkum allatu iyaṅkum irutiṇip poruḷkaḷmēlun tappātu eṟitaṟkēṟṟa payiṟciyi, iv viḷiyāṭṭu aḷikkum. vēṭṭiyāṭal, pōr ceytal, tiruṭaṉik kallāl aṭittal mutaliya viṉikaṭku ip payiṟci ēṟṟatām.
II. piḷḷiyār pantu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : piḷḷiyārik kuṟikkum oru kalliṉ mēṟ panti eṟintāṭum āṭṭup piḷḷiyār pantu. itu tiruccirāppaḷḷi vaṭṭārattiṟ piḷḷiyār viḷiyāṭṭu eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
āṭuvār toki : potuvāka, eṇmarkkuk kuṟiyāta palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : ēṟattāḻa āṟaṅkula nīḷamuḷḷa oru kallum, oru pantum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām.
āṭiṭam : cuvaraṭiyum atiyaṭutta veḷinilamum iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : āṭuvōrellārum camat tokiyiṉavāṉa      iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoḷvar. oru ceṅkalli allatu ciṟu        nīḷak kallip piḷḷiyārākap pāvittu oru cuvaraṭiyil ciṟitu maṇalik kuvittu atil ati naṭṭu, oru kaṭciyār aiṅkacat tolivil etirniṉṟu ovvoruvarāy ovvoru taṭavi piḷḷiyārip paṭukiṭiyākac cāyttaṟkup pantālaṭikka, iṉṉoru kaṭciyār iru pakkattilum piḷḷiyārkkum         avarukkum iṭiyil variciyāka niṉṟukoṇṭu, eṟiyappaṭṭa pantip piṭikka muyalvar. piḷḷiyārip paṭukiṭiyāyc cāytta panti yārēṉum antarattiṟ piṭittu viṭiṉum, eṟinta pantu piḷḷiyārmēṟ paṭāviṭiṉum, paṭṭum atip                  paṭukiṭiyāyc cāykkāviṭiṉum, eṟiyuṅ kaṭciyār āḷ māṟikkoṇṭēyiruntu aṉivarun  tīrntapiṉ, etirk kaṭciyār aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyārākavum aṭitta kaṭciyār piṭikkuṅ kaṭciyārākavum, māṟal vēṇṭum.
piḷḷiyārip paṭukiṭiyāyc cāytta pantu piṭikkap paṭāviṭiṉ, piṭikka niṉṟa kaṭciyar aṉivarum uṭaṉē ōṭippōyc caṟṟut tolivil iṭiyiṭṭu niṟpar. aṭitta kaṭciyāraṉivarum nerukkamākak kūṭi niṉṟu, avaruḷ oruvaṉ pantit taṉ aṭi vayiṟṟiṉmēl vittu atu veḷikkut teriyāmal iru kiyālum pottikkoṇṭum, piṟarum tām pantu vittiruppatāka etirk kaṭciyārukkut tōṉṟumāṟu taṉittaṉi naṭittukkoṇṭum, avariṭiyē pirintu celvar. etirkkaṭciyāruḷ yārēṉum oruvaṉ, uṇmiyāyp pantu vittiruppavaṉi aiyuṟātu avaṉukkup pakkamāka niṟpiṉ, pantu vittiruppavaṉ tiṭumeṉṟu avaṉmēl eṟintuviṭuvāṉ. atōṭu ōr āṭṭi muṭiyum. aṭutta āṭṭi āṭuvatu āṭakar viruppattip poṟuttatu.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : orukāl, piṟamatap pakimipaṟṟiyum koḷḷiyaṭittaṟ poruṭṭum, iṭik kālattiṟ cila aracarum koḷḷit talivarum teyvaccilikaḷi (vikkirakaṅkaḷi) uṭittatum kavarntatum, iv viḷiyāṭṭut tōṟṟattiṟkuk kāraṇamāyiruntirukkalām. kaciṉi makamatu, mālikkāpūr, tirumaṅkiyāḻvār mutaliyōr ceyalkaḷ, iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkaṉa.
5. marakkuraṅku
 (komparacaṉ kuḻiyaracaṉ)
āṭṭiṉ peyar : ciṟuvar marattiṟ kuraṅkupōl ēṟi viḷiyāṭum āṭṭu marakkuraṅku eṉpatām. itu pāṇṭi nāṭṭil komparacaṉ kuḻiyaracaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
āṭuvār toki : iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭumuṟi : āṭuvārellārum oru marattarukē oru vaṭṭak kōṭṭuḷ niṉṟukoṇṭu ovvoruvaṉāy iṭakkāli maṭakkit tūkki, ataṉ kavaṭṭūṭu oru kalliyāvatu kucciyāvatu eṟivar. kuṟinta tolivu pōkkiyavaṉ, piṟar marattilēṟip piṭikkac coṉṉa piṉ avarip piṭittal vēṇṭum. piṭikkiṟavaṉ marattilēṟumpōtu, cilar marattiṉiṉṟum kutittu vaṭṭattiṟkuṭ pōy niṉṟukoḷvar. cilar kiḷikkuk kiḷi tāvi āṭṭaṅ kāṭṭuvar. vaṭṭattiṟkuṭpōy niṟkumuṉ yārēṉum toṭappaṭṭuviṭṭāl, avaṉ aṭutta āṭṭiyil piṟari muṉ coṉṉavāṟu piṭittal vēṇṭum. marakkiḷikaḷi lirukkumpōtu komparacaṉ kuḻiyaracaṉ eṉṟu pāṇṭi nāṭṭuc ciṟuvar tammik kūṟikkoḷvar.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : kuraṅkukaḷiṉ ceyaliṉiṉṟu iv āṭṭut tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : maramēṟap payilutal iv vāṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
6. kāyā paḻamā?
āṭṭiṉ peyar : nīntat teriyum ciṟuvar nīril niṉṟu kāyā paḻamā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu āṭum āṭṭu, ak kēḷviyiyē peyarākak koṇṭatu.
āṭuvār toki : iruvar mutaṟ palar iti āṭuvar. iruvarkku mēṟpaṭiṉ iṉpañ ciṟakkum.
āṭiṭam : āṟuṅ kuḷamum pōlum nīrnilikaḷ iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : āṭuvārellārum nīril niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoruvaṉāyk kāyā, paḻamā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu nīruṭ cuṇṭuvar. cuṇṭum viral nīrmēlpaṭiṉ `taḷār eṉṉum ōci yeḻum; nīruḷ muṟṟum muḻukiṉ ōciyē kēḷātu; pāti muḻukiṉ `ṭapakku eṉṉum ōci piṟakkum. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉavi yiraṇṭum kāyām; piṉṉatoṉṟum paḻamām. kāyāyiṉ kāy eṉṟum, paḻamāyiṉ paḻam eṉṟum, piṟar kūṟuvar. palar kāyāyiṉ mīṇṭum cuṇṭuvar. oruvaṉē kāyāyiṉ, avaṉ piṟari nīruṭ piṭittal vēṇṭum. avar ciṟitu tolivu ceṉṟapiṉ, tammip piṭikkac colvār. avaṉ virintu ceṉṟu, naṭantu celpavari naṭantu ceṉṟum, nīntic celpavari nīntic ceṉṟum, muḻukic celpavari muḻukic ceṉṟum, piṭikkamuyalvāṉ. yārēṉum toṭappaṭiṉ, avaṉē piṉpu piṟarip piṭittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu viruppamuḷḷavarip piṭittu āṭappeṟum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : uḷḷāṉ eṉṉum nīrppaṟavi nīruḷ mūḻkic ceṉṟu mīṉ piṭippatiṉiṉṟu, iv āṭṭut tōṉṟi yirukkalām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : nīruḷ mūḻkiya poruḷi yeṭuttaṟkum muttuk kuḷittaṟkum ēṟṟa payiṟciyi, iv viḷiyāṭṭu 
aḷikkum.
7. pañcuveṭṭuṅ kampaṭō
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pañcuveṭṭuṅ kampaṭō eṉṟu colli āṭappeṟum āṭṭu, ac colliyē peyarākak koṇṭatu.
āṭuvār toki : iti āṭa iruvar vēṇṭum.
āṭiṭam : maṇaṟ kuviyalil maṭṭum itu āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟi : oruvaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉi maṇaliṟ paṭukka vittut tuṇiyāṟ pōrttu avaṉatu uṭaliṉ kīḻppakutiyi maṇalāl mūṭip pañcuveṭṭuṅ kampaṭō tōlē tōlē, paruttiveṭṭuṅ kampaṭō tōlē tōlē eṉṟu maṭakki maṭakkic colli mella mella avaṉ mutukil taṭṭik koṇṭēyiruppaṉ. ciṟitu nēram poṟuttup paṭuttavaṉ eḻuntuviṭuvāṉ.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : pōril tōṟṟōṭiṉavaruḷ oru cārār, tammit toṭarntu varum veṟṟi maṟavariṉiṉṟu tappikkoḷḷum poruṭṭu naṭitta naṭippukaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉ pōliyāka, iv āṭṭut tōṉṟiyatōveṉa aiyuṟa 
iṭamuṇṭu.
8. kuccu viḷiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oru kuccit  talikkumēṟ piṭittu atit taṭṭi viḷiyāṭuvatu kuccu viḷiyāṭṭu.
āṭuvār toki : itip potuvāy aivarkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : āṭakaṉ ovvoruvaṉuk kum ēṟattāḻa irumuḻa nīḷamuḷḷa oru kuccu vēṇṭum.
oru muḻac caturamāṉa ōr araṅku nilattiṟ kīṟi ataṉuḷ oru ciṟu vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ nāṉku kaṟkaḷ vikkappaṭum.
āṭiṭam : poṭṭalilum tiṟantaveḷi nilattilum iti āṭalām.
āṭumuṟi : mutalāvatu, āṭakarellārum ōr uttik kōṭṭiṉmēl ovvoruvaṉāy niṉṟukoṇṭu, avaṉavaṉ taṉtaṉ kucci iṭakkāṟ kavaṭṭiyūṭu iyaṉṟa tolivu eṟivaṉ. uttikkuk kiṭṭa eṟintavaṉ kuccup piṭittal vēṇṭum.
avaṉ catura araṅkiṟku veḷiyē pakkattiṟkoṉṟāka īretirppakkattil iru kāliyum vittu, kuccit talikkumēl iru muṉiyiyum irukiyāṟ paṟṟikkoṇṭu, niṟṟal vēṇṭum. yārēṉum oruvaṉ avaṉ kuccit taṉ kuccāl taṭṭiviṭuvāṉ. iṉṉoruvaṉ ak kuccic caṟṟut toli viṟkup pōkkuvāṉ. iṅṅaṉam piṟarellām atit tam kuccāl taḷḷit taḷḷi neṭuntoliviṟkuk koṇṭu pōvar.
kuccup piṭittavaṉ, taṉ kuccu viḻuntavuṭaṉ vaṭṭattuḷ  ḷirukkum nāṟkalliyum eṭuttuc caturattil mūlikkoṉṟāka vittuviṭṭu, piṟaruḷ oruvaṉip pōyt toṭal vēṇṭum. nāṟ kalliyum mūlikkoṉṟāka viyātu pōyt toṭiṉ, atu kūṭṭaṉṟu. kuccit taḷḷupavar, kuccup piṭittavaṉ tammit toṭumuṉ, araṅku mūlik kalliṉmēlēṉum āṅkāṅkuḷḷa piṟa karuṅkalliṉ mēlēṉum tam kucci vittukkoṇṭāl, avarit toṭal kūṭātu.
yārēṉum oruvaṉ toṭappaṭṭuviṭiṉ, avaṉ tāṉ toṭappaṭṭa viṭattiliruntu catura araṅku variyum, taṉ kucci vāyiṟ kuvikkoṇṭum, allatu valakkiyiṟ piṭittuk koṇṭum, iṭakkiyi maṭakki mutukil vittukkoṇṭum.
eṅkaḷ vīṭṭu nāyi elumpu kaṭikkap pōccu
 kallāl aṭittēṉ kāloṭintu pōccu
eṉṟu iṭiviṭātu pāṭikkoṇṭu, noṇṭiyaṭittuvaral vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam neṭukalum varat tavaṟiṉ, mīṇṭum puṟappaṭṭa iṭattiliruntu avvāṟu varalvēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam vanta piṉ, toṭappaṭṭavaṉ kuccup piṭippāṉ. ataṉ piṉ, muṉ pōṉṟē  tirumpavum  āṭappeṟum.
9. pamparam
I. ōyākkaṭṭi
ciṟuvar, tam pamparaṅkaḷi vaṭṭattuḷāyiṉum, varampilā nilattilāyiṉum oruṅkē āṭaviṭṭu, yāratu nīṇṭa nēram āṭukiṉṟateṉṟu pārkkum āṭṭu ōyākkaṭṭiyām. itu mīṇṭum mīṇṭum orē vakiyāy āṭappeṟum.
II. uṭitta kaṭṭi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : āṭṭattil tōṟṟavaṉatu pamparatti uṭikkum āṭṭu uṭitta kaṭṭi eṉappaṭum.
āṭumuṟi : iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa ciṟuvar palar, oru vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil oru māṅkoṭṭiyi vittu, ovvoru vaṉākap pamparatti ataṉmēlēṟṟi, ati veḷiyēṟṟuvar. atu veḷiyēṟiyavuṭaṉ, ellārum oruṅkē virivākat taṉ taṉ pamparatti āṭṭik kimēl ēṟṟuvar. mikap pinti ēṟṟiyavaṉ taṉ pamparatti vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil vittal vēṇṭum.
pamparatti yāṭṭik kimēlēṟṟumpōtu, apiṭkōcu allatu ciṅkōcu eṉṟu collikkoḷvatāl, aṅṅaṉam ēṟṟuvataṟku, apiṭkōceṭuttal allatu ciṅ kōceṭuttal eṉṟu peyar.
vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa pamparatti ēṉiyōrel                 lārum, muṉpu māṅkoṭṭiyi veḷiyēṟṟiyatu pōl veḷiyēṟṟi, ēṟattāḻa irupatu kacat toliviluḷḷa ellik kōṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭu pōvar. ovvoruvaṉāka avaṉavaṉ taṉtaṉ             pamparatti ataṉmēlēṟṟit taḷḷiyē, atik koṇṭu 
pōtal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam koṇṭu pōmpōtu, yārēṉum maṭṭi vīḻttiṉum caraṭṭi pōkkiṉum, kīḻēyirukkum pamparattiṟkup patilākat taṉ pamparatti vittuviṭal 
vēṇṭum.  kīḻēyirunta pamparakkāraṉ ati eṭuttup piṟarpōl āṭṭuvāṉ.
pamparam talikīḻāy viḻutaṟku maṭṭi eṉṟum, pakkamāka uruṇṭupōtaṟkuc cāṭṭi eṉṟum peyar.
yār pamparam ellik kōṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭatō, atu uṭaṉē piṟarāl uṭikkappaṭum.
III. pamparak kuttu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : tōṟṟavaṉatu pamparattik kutti yāṭum āṭṭup pamparakkuttu eṉappaṭum.
āṭumuṟi : iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa ciṟuvar palar, oru vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil māṅkoṭṭi vittu veḷiyēṟṟi, apiṭkōceṭuttu, atil mikap pintiyavaṉ taṉ pamparatti vaṭṭattuḷ vitta piṉ, ēṉiyōrellām ovvoruvaṉākat taṉ taṉ pamparattāṟ kutti ati veḷiyēṟṟuvar. atu veḷiyēṟṟappaṭiṉ, uṭaṉē mīṇṭum muṉpōl apiṭkō ceṭuttu, atiṟ pintiyavaṉ taṉ pamparatti vaṭṭattuḷ vittal vēṇṭum. ēṉiyarellām muṉpōṟ kutti ati veḷiyēṟṟuvar.
maṭṭi vīḻttiyavaṉ pamparamum, cāṭṭi pōkkiyavaṉ pamparamum vaṭṭattuḷ ēṟkeṉavē vittiruppatuṭaṉ cērttu vikkappaṭum.
vaṭṭattuḷ āṭum pamparaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu veḷiyēṟi yāṭumpōtu, ati vaṭṭattuḷ vittirukkum pamparakkāraṉ cāṭṭiyāl taṉ kiyilēṟṟi āṭṭiṉ, atuvum vaṭṭattuḷ vikkappaṭal vēṇṭum.
vaṭṭattuḷ āṭum pamparaṅkaḷuḷ etiyēṉum, vaṭṭattuḷ vikkappaṭṭirukkum pamparakkāraṉ, taṉ kiyālaḻuttip patittuviṭiṉ, ati eṭuttalkūṭātu.
uḷḷē vikkappaṭṭirukkum pamparam mēlum mēlum kuttappaṭṭuc cētamākātapaṭi, ataṉ contakkāraṉ āṭṭik koṇṭirukkum pamparakkāraruḷ oruvaṉiṭam, nī iṉṟu 
eṉ pamparatti veḷiyēṟṟiṉ, uṉ pamparam uḷḷirukkum 
pōtu nāṉ veḷiyēṟṟuvēṉ, eṉṟu oppantañ ceytu koḷvatuṇṭu.
pala pamparaṅkaḷ uḷḷē vikkappaṭṭuk kiṭappiṉ, aviyaṉittum veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭa piṉputāṉ apiṭkōcu eṭukkappaṭum.
iṅṅaṉam mīṇṭum mīṇṭum vēṇṭum aḷavu toṭarntu āṭappeṟum.
vaṭṭattuḷ vikkappaṭṭirukkum pamparaṅkaḷuḷ naṇpaṉati veḷiyēṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, piṟaṉati veḷiyēṟṟak kūṭāteṉṟum, iruvēṟu nōkkuk koṇṭu ataṟkēṟpap pamparatti āṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
IV. iruvaṭṭak kuttu
vaṭṭattuḷ vaṭṭamāka iruvaṭṭam kīṟi av viraṇṭuḷḷum pamparattik kuttuvatu, iruvaṭṭakkuttu. itu perumpālum pamparak kuttup pōṉṟatē.
itaṉ uḷvaṭṭattil māṅkoṭṭi vittu veḷiyēṟṟi apiṭkō ceṭuttup pintiyavaṉ pamparatti ataṉuḷ vittapiṉ, atik kutti veḷiyēṟṟuvar. maṭṭiyum cāṭṭiyum uḷ vikkappaṭum. uḷ vaṭṭat til āṭum pamparam veḷi vaṭṭattuḷḷum vantu āṭalām. āṭum pamparatti aḻuttuvatu uḷvaṭṭattiltāṉ nikaḻum; āyiṉ, atik kuttuvatu iruvaṭṭattilum uṇṭu. veḷivaṭṭattuḷ āṭum pamparam tāṉāy nakarntu veḷiyēṟiviṭiṉ, ati uṭiyavaṉ eṭuttu āṭṭalām; aṉṟi uḷḷēyē ōyntuviṭiṉ ati eṭuttal kūṭātu. atuvum maṭṭipōṟ pāvikkappaṭum.
V. taliyāri
āṭuvār toki : mūvarkkuk kuṟiyāta ciṟuvar palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : āḷukkoru pamparamum, ēṟattāḻa irupatu kacam iṭiyiṭṭa iru cama aḷavāṉa vaṭṭaṅkaḷum, orupōkāka (atāvatu camatūramāka) irupuṟamum nīṭṭappaṭṭa avaṟṟiṉ viṭṭaṅkaḷum, iv viḷiyāṭṭiṟkuriya karuvikaḷām.
āṭumuṟi : mutalāvatu oru vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa māṅkoṭṭiyip pamparattiṉ mūlamāy veḷiyēṟṟi, apiṭkōceṭuttu atiṟ pintiyavaṉ pamparatti uḷvittu, ati ēṉiyōrellām ovvoruvaṉāyp pamparamēṟṟi veḷiyēṟṟuvar. aṅṅaṉam veḷiyēṟṟumpōtu vaṭṭat tiṟku valappuṟamāy veḷiyēṟṟal vēṇṭum; illāviṭiṉ mīṇṭum apiṭkōceṭuttup pintiyavaṉ pamparatti uḷḷē vikka vēṇṭiyirukkum.
veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭa pamparatti nērē etiruḷḷa vaṭṭattiṟkup pamparattiṉ mūlamāy aṭittukkoṇṭu pōvar. oruvaṉ iṭiyil maṭṭi vīḻttiṉum cāṭṭi pōkkiṉum, kīḻirukkum pamparattiṟkup patilākat taṉati vittuviṭal vēṇṭum. muṉpu kīḻirunta pamparak kāraṉ piṉpu piṟaroṭu cērntu āṭuvāṉ.
aṭittukkoṇṭu pōkappaṭum pamparam etirvaṭṭattuḷ cērntavuṭaṉ, piṟa pamparakkārarellām tam cāṭṭi kaḷik kaḻuttiṟ cuṟṟikkoṇṭu, atip pampara āṇiyāl ovvoru taṭavi kuttuvar. kuttumpōtu cāṭṭi taḷarntu nilattit toṭiṉ, kuttappaṭum pamparakkāraṉ taṉ cāṭṭiyāl vaṉmiyāy aṭittuviṭuvāṉ.
piṉpu, iraṇṭām vaṭṭattiliruntu mutal vaṭṭattiṟku, muṉpōṉṟē ap pamparam aṭittukkoṇṭu pōkappaṭum. aṅku ati  ivviru taṭavi kuttuvar.
ataṉpiṉ, atu iraṇṭām vaṭṭattiṟku mīṇṭuṅ koṇṭupōkappaṭṭu, mummūṉṟu taṭavi kuttappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam iṅkumaṅkumāka iyakkappaṭṭu, ovvoru muṟikkum ovvoru taṭavi kūṭutalākak kuttappaṭum.
ivvakiyil itu virumpiya aḷavu toṭarntu āṭappeṟum.
10. paṭṭam
paṭṭam oruvaṉ āṭiṉāl poḻutupōkkām (Pastime); iruvar āṭiṉāl viḷiyāṭṭām.
oruvaṉ paṭṭattiṟkumēl oruvaṉ paṭṭattip paṟakkac ceyvatum, oṉṟiyoṉṟu tākki vīḻttac ceyvatumē iv viḷiyāṭṭām.
(2) iravāṭṭu
iṅku, iraveṉṟatu kaṇteriyum nilākkālattiravi. nilavoḷi yillātaviṭattut teru viḷakkoḷiyilum cila āṭṭukaḷ āṭappeṟum.
1. kutirikkuk kāṇaṅkaṭṭal
āṭṭiṉ peyar : kutirikkuk kāṇaṅkaṭṭi avaṟṟiṉ mēl ēṟuvatākap pāvittukkoṇṭu, oru kaṭciyār iṉṉoru kaṭciyārmēl ēṟi viḷiyāṭuvatu kutirikkuk kāṇaṅkaṭṭal. kāṇaṅkaṭṭutalāvatu koḷvittal.
āṭuvār toki : potuvāka, eṇmarkku mēṟpaṭṭa ciṟuvar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : ōr āṭittuṇṭi muṟukkip piṉṉiya tiri itaṟkuriya karuviyām. ēṟattāḻa aintu kacam iṭiyiṭṭa iru camatūrak kōṭukaḷik kīṟi, avaṟṟi etiretirāka niṉṟu āṭum iru kaṭciyārum tattam maṉiyelli eṉpar.
āṭiṭam : ūrp poṭṭalum akaṉṟa teruvum iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : āṭuvār ellārum mutalāvatu utti kaṭṭic camattokiyāṉa irukaṭciyākap pirintu koḷvar.
muntiyāṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu tuṇiyappaṭṭa kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉ, tiriyiṉ oru muṉiyit taṉ valakkāṟ peruviraṟkum   aṭutta viraṟkum iṭiyil iṭukki, iru kiyiyum nilattil         ūṉṟi, karaṇam pōṭuvatupōṟ kālittūkkit tiriyi etirkkaṭciyāriṉ maṉikkuḷ kālāl eṟivāṉ. ati etirkkaṭciyār antarattiṟ piṭittuviṭṭāl, eṟintavaṉ tolivāṉ. ataṉpiṉ etirkkaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉ avvāṟu āṭal vēṇṭum. tiri piṭikkappaṭāviṭiṉ, oruvaṉ ettaṉi muṟiyum āṭalām. tiri maṉikkuṭ pukāmal ellikku veḷiyē vīḻiṉum eṟintavaṉ tolivāṉ.
tavaṟāta ovvōr eṟivum, kāṇam viḷittuk kutirikkuk kaṭṭaṟkuriya ovvoru viṉiyāka muṟiyē toṭarntu kūṟappaṭum. mutal eṟivil, uḻutāyiṟṟu eṉṟum; iraṇṭāvatil, vitittāyiṟṟu eṉṟum; mūṉṟāvatil, muḷittuviṭṭatu eṉṟum; iṅṅaṉamē toṭarntu mēṟpaṭṭavaṟṟil muṟiyē, ōr ilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu, īr ilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu, mūvilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu, nālilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu, aiyilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu, āṟilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu, ēḻilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu, eṭṭilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu, oṉpatilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu, pattilakku viṭṭirukkiṟatu, koṭiyōṭi yirukkiṟatu, pūppūttirukkiṟatu, piñcuviṭṭirukkiṟatu, kāy kāyttuviṭṭatu, kāṇatti aṟuttāyiṟṟu, kaḷattil aṭittāyiṟṟu, vīṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭuvantāyiṟṟu, aṭuppil vēkiṟatu, kutirikku vittirukkiṟatu, eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. aṭutta eṟivil, etirkkaṭciyār kuṉiya, veṉṟa kaṭciyār avaṉavaṉ uttippaṭi avarmēl ēṟikkoḷvar. kuṟitta tolivāvatu, kuṟitta taṭavi ōriṭattic cuṟṟiyāvatu, tōṟṟa kaṭciyār veṉṟa kaṭciyāric camantu cellavēṇṭum. atōṭu ōr āṭṭi muṭiyum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : iv viḷiyāṭṭu orukāl uḻiñip pōriṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
oru nakar allatu talinakar nīṇṭakālamāka muṟṟuki yiṭappaṭṭirukkumpōtu, nocciyār (atāvatu aṭipaṭṭuḷḷa nakarattār) tamakkum tam kālnaṭikaṭkum vēṇṭum uṇavup poruḷkaḷi, nakarak kuṭiyiruppiṟkum puṟamatiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa nilattilēyē viḷittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭiyiruntirukkum.
nāṭukaṇ ṭaṉṉa kaṇituñcu vilaṅkal eṉṉum patiṟṟuppattut toṭarukku (16:2) neṭunāṭpaṭa aṭimatiṟ paṭṭa kālattē viḷittuk kōṭaṟku, vayalum kuḷamum uḷavākac camittu vittamiyāṟ kaṇṭārkku nāṭu kaṇṭāṟpōṉṟa..... iṭimatil eṉṟa an nūliṉ paḻiyavuriyāciriyar viḷakka vuri kūṟiyiruppatu, iṅkē kavaṉikkat takkatu.
kālil tiriyeṟital, uḻiñiyār (atāvatu muṟṟukiyiṭ ṭiruppavar) nakarattuḷ eṟiyum erivāṇattik kuṟikkalām. ippōtu vēṭikkikkāka viṭappaṭum erivāṇam (vāṇak kaṭṭu) paḻaṅkālattil nocci nakaruḷ eriyūṭṭuvataṟku viṭappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu.
viḷiyāṭṭiṟ kāṇaṅ kaṭṭiyapiṉ kutiriyēṟuvatu, nocci maṟavar kāṇam viḷittut tam kutirikaṭku vitta piṉ, avaṟṟiṉ mēlēṟi nakarukku veḷiyē pōrukkup puṟappaṭṭu varuvatik kuṟikkalām.
āṭṭiṉpayaṉ : kālāl oru poruḷip paṟṟuvatum karaṇampōṭṭut tāṇṭuvatumākiya viṉippayiṟciyi, iv viḷiyāṭṭu aḷikkum.
2. vaṇṇāṉ tāḻi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pāṇṭināṭṭil, vaṇṇārak kulatti yum tāḻttappaṭṭa vakuppukaḷiyum cērnta ciṟuvar, vaṇṇāṉ tuṟiyil āṭiyolippatu pōl naṭittāṭum āṭṭu vaṇṇāṉ tāḻi. tāḻi eṉpatu alacuvataṟkut tuṇikaḷ vikkappaṭṭirukkum perumpāṉi.
āṭuvār toki : potuvāka, nālvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : oru tuṇi mūṭṭi itaṟku vēṇṭuṅ karuviyām.
āṭiṭam : ūrp poṭṭalilum akaṉṟa teruvilum itu āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟi : āṭuvār aṉivarum ovvoru tuṇi pōṭa vēṇṭum. avi oru mūṭṭiyākak kaṭṭappaṭum. ati oruvaṉ eṭuttut taṉ piṭariyil vittukkoṇṭu, maruḷkoṇṭa tēvarāḷaṉpōl āṭi variciyāy niṟkum ēṉiyōruḷ oruvaṉmēl kaṇṇi mūṭikkoṇṭu eṟivāṉ. atu yārmēl viḻuntatō avaṉ atit taṉ piṭariyil vittu irukiyālum, piṭittukkoṇṭu kīḻē uṭkāra vēṇṭum. mūṭṭiyi eṟintavaṉ, atil iraṇṭōr aṭiyaṭittuviṭṭu, kūḻ kuṭikkap pōkiṟēṉ, eṉṟu collic cuṟṟut tolivupōy mīḷvāṉ. ataṟkuḷ ēṉiyōrellām am mūṭṭiyil topput toppeṉṟu aṭittu makiḻvar. pōy mīṇṭavaṉ avaruḷ oruvaṉit toṭa muyalvāṉ. avaṉ aṇukiyavuṭaṉ aṉivarum ōṭippōvar. avaṉ oruvaṉit toṭumuṉ iṉṉoruvaṉ mūṭṭiyil aṭittuviṭiṉ, at toṭuki kaṇakkilli. iṉṉoruvaṉ mūṭṭiyil aṭikkumuṉ oruvaṉit toṭṭuviṭiṉ toṭappaṭṭavaṉ mūṭṭiyi vāṅkit taṉ piṭarimēl vittukkoṇṭu kīḻē uṭkāra vēṇṭum. muṉpu kīḻē uṭkārntiruntavaṉ piṉpu piṟarit toṭupavaṉāvaṉ. muṉpu toṭṭavaṉ piṉpu piṟaroṭu cērntu mūṭṭiyil aṭittu viḷiyāṭuvāṉ.
iṅṅaṉamē, neṭukalum, toṭappaṭṭavaṉ mūṭṭi vittiruppavaṉākavum, mūṭṭi vittiruntavaṉ toṭupavaṉā kavum, toṭṭavaṉ  mūṭṭiyil aṭittu viḷiyāṭupavaṉākavum, māṟikkoṇṭē varuvar.
putitāyt toṭupavaṉākum ovvoruvaṉum, mutalāvatu mūṭṭiyil iraṇṭaṭi yaṭittuviṭṭuk kūḻ kuṭikkap pōvatum, piṉpu mīṇṭum piṟarit toṭuvatum marapām.
mūṭṭiyil aṭittu makiḻum ovvoruvaṉum piṉpu mūṭṭi tāṅki aṭivāṅkuvataṟku iṭamiruttalāl, muṉpu piṟaṉ mutukil mūṭṭiyiruntapōtu kaṇṇōṭṭamiṉṟi vaṉmiyāy aṭittavaṉ. piṉpu taṉ viṉiviḷivi mikutiyāy aṟukka nērum.
viḷiyāṭṭu muṭintapiṉ, avaṉavaṉ tuṇiyi avaṉavaṉ eṭuttukkoḷvāṉ.
āṭṭut tōṟṟa viḷakkam : vaṇṇāruḷ ovvoruva ṉukkum ovvoru tuṟiyuṇṭu. oruvaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉ tuṟiyil veḷuppatu, vacatik kuṟivumaṭṭumaṉṟi iḻappu muṇṭupaṇṇum. paḻaṅkālattil ovvoru vaṇṇāṉum vaṇṇārap pāṭṭam allatu vaṇṇārap pāṟi eṉṉum toḻil vari celutta vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ataṉāl oruvaṉ pāṟiyil iṉṉoruvaṉ veḷuppatu, mikak kaṇṭippāyt taṭukkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. āyiṉum, oruvaṉ pāṟi mika vacatiyuḷḷatākaviruppiṉ, avaṉ tāṉ vaḻakkamāka uṇṇuṅ kūḻuṇṇac ceṉṟirukkumpōtu, piṟar avaṉ pāṟiyip payaṉpaṭuttiyiruppar. avaṉ mīṇṭu varumpōtu, avar ōṭiyiruppar. avaṉ avarit  turatti aṭittirup pāṉ, allatu kaṭumiyāyt  tiṭṭiyiruppāṉ.
ic ceyaliyē iv viḷiyāṭṭu uṇarttukiṉṟatu. toṭupavaṉ vaṇṇāṉiyum, mūṭṭi pāṟiyiyum, atil aṭippavar avaṉ kūḻuṇṇac ceṉṟirukkumpōtu atip payaṉpaṭuttum piṟa vaṇṇāriyum, oruvaṉi ōṭit toṭuvatu tiruṭṭuttaṉamāyp pāṟiyip payaṉpaṭuttiya oruvaṉip piṭittu aṭippatiyum, kuṟippatākak koḷḷappaṭum.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : ōṭum oruvaṉip piṭippatum oruvaṉāṟ piṭipaṭāmal ōṭippōvatumāṉa viṉippayiṟci, iv viḷiyāṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
3. cū viḷiyāṭṭu
(1) pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu muṟi
āṭṭiṉ peyar: pāṇṭināṭṭil itu iṉṟu avuṭṭu (out) eṉṉum āṅkilappeyarāl vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ōṭit toṭum oruvaṉ, uṭkārntirukkum oruvaṉi avuṭṭu eṉṟu colli eḻuppum viḷiyāṭṭu, ac colliyē peyarākak koṇṭatu. itu mutalāvatu ucu viḷiyāṭṭu eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyirukkalām.
āṭuvār toki : iti āṭa aivar vēṇṭum.
āṭiṭam : ūrp poṭṭalilum akaṉṟa teruvilum iti āṭalām.
āṭu muṟi : mūvar ivviru kaca iṭiyiṭṭu, orē tici nōkki variciyāka nilattil uṭkārntukoḷvar. oruvaṉ avaric cuṟṟiyum avarukkiṭiyilum ōṭuvāṉ. iṉṉoru vaṉ avaṉit toṭavēṇṭum. toṭukiṟavaṉ variciyic cuṟṟiyaṉṟi iṭiyiṟ cellakkūṭātu. āyiṉ, iṭiyil varicik kōṭṭit tāṇṭātu eṭṭit toṭalām.
ōṭukiṟavaṉukku mika vacatiyiruttalil avaṉit toṭuvatu aritu. toṭa muyalkiṟavaṉ nīṇṭa nēram āṭiyōṭip pārttu viṭṭu, piṉpu ōṭukiṟavaṉukku aṇmiyākat taṉakku muṉṉāliruppavaṉi avuṭṭu eṉṟu colli eḻuppiviṭṭu avaṉiṭattil tāṉ uṭkārntukoḷvāṉ. uṭkārntiruppavar orē ticinōkki uṭkārntiruppatālum, muṉṉōkkiyaṉṟip piṉṉōkki ōṭit toṭutal kūṭāmiyālum, eḻuppukiṟavaṉ eṉṟum eḻuppappaṭukiṟavaṉukkup piṉṉāka niṉṟē eḻuppuvaṉ. eḻuppappaṭṭavaṉ uṭaṉē ōṭip pōy ōṭukiṟavaṉit toṭṭuviṭiṉ, toṭṭavaṉ ōṭukiṟavaṉākavum ōṭiṉavaṉ toṭukiṟavaṉākavum māṟal vēṇṭum. ōṭukiṟavaṉ tāṉ eppōtu virumpiṉum, uṭkārntirukkum oruvaṉi eḻuppi viṭṭu, tāṉ avaṉiṭattil uṭkārntu koḷḷalām.
ōṭukiṟavaṉ tāṉ kūṭiyamaṭṭum toṭappaṭātavāṟu toṭukiṟavaṉukkum uṭkārntirukkiṟavaṉukkum nēr muṉṉum aṇmiyilum nillātu mikat taḷḷiyē niṟpaṉ.
iṅṅaṉam vēṇṭumaḷavu toṭarntāṭappeṟum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : itu vēṭṭi viṉiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākat terikiṉṟatu. toṭukiṟavaṉ vēṭṭikkāraṉum, ōṭukiṟavaṉ vēṭṭi vilaṅkum, eḻuppappaṭukiṟavaṉ vēṭṭi nāyum pōṉṟavarāvar.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : ōṭum oruvaṉip piṭippatum, oruvaṉuk kup piṭikoṭāmal ōṭit tappuvatumākiya viṉippayiṟci, iv vāṭṭāṟ peṟappaṭum.
(2) cōḻanāṭṭu muṟi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : cū viḷiyāṭṭu eṉṉum taṉittamiḻp peyar vaḻaṅkuvatu cōḻanāṭṭiltāṉ. cū eṉṟu colli oruvaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉi eḻuppum viḷiyāṭṭu ac collāṟ peyarpeṟṟatu.
āṭuvār toki : aṟuvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭumuṟi : āṭuvār camattokiyavāṉa iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoṇṭu, kaṭcikkoruvaṉāka iruvar niṟka, ēṉiyarellām variciyāy iṭiyiṭṭu nilattil uṭkārntu koḷvar. uṭkārntiruppavaruḷ, ovvoru kaṭciyārum oruvaṉviṭ ṭoruvaṉāyiruppar. oru kaṭciyār kiḻakkunōkkiṉ maṟṟoru kaṭciyār mēṟku nōkkiyum, oru kaṭciyār teṟku nōkkiṉ maṟṟoru kaṭciyār vaṭakku nōkkiyum, iruppar.
niṟpavaruḷ, oruvaṉ ōṭa iṉṉoruvaṉ toṭal vēṇṭum. toṭukiṟavaṉ taṉṉāl toṭamuṭiyāteṉṟu kaṇṭāl, taṉ kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉic cū eṉṟu colli eḻuppuvāṉ. piṟa ceytikaḷellām pāṇṭināṭṭu muṟiyē.
iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoṇṭu etiretirtticiyi nōkkiyiruppatum, cū eṉṟu colluvatumē cōḻanāṭṭu vēṟupāṭām.
(3) irupoḻutāṭṭu
pakalum iravum āṭappaṭum āṭṭu irupoḻutāṭṭām.
1. kiḷittaṭṭu
mukavuri : tamiḻnāṭṭil peruvāriyāka viḷiyāṭap paṭum viḷiyāṭṭukaḷil iraṇṭāvatu kiḷittaṭṭu. itu kiḷiyantaṭṭu eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. iḵtu āṭavar viḷiyāṭṭu.
tōṉṟiya vaki: naṉcey puṉceykaḷilum pōr aṭikkum kaḷaṅkaḷilum aṟuvaṭik kālattil, cintic citaṟik kiṭakkum kūla (tāṉiya) maṇikaḷi eṟumpukaḷ īrttuk koṇṭu pōy, nilattiṉ kīḻum maṇ cuvaraṭiyilum taṅkaḷ vaḷikaḷil paṭikkaṇakkākavum marakkāṟ kaṇakkākavum maḻikkāla uṇavākac cērttuvippatu iyalpu. iṅṅaṉam cērttu vikkappaṭṭa kūlattiṟku aṭipul allatu aṭippul eṉṟu peyar. pañcakkālattilum uṇavillāta pōtum ēḻiyeḷiyavar inta aṭippullit tēṭi akaḻnteṭuppatu vaḻakkam.
aṭippul pōṉṟē ēḻi pāḻikaṭku vēṟoru varuvāyu muṇṭu. atu kiḷiyīṭu. atāvatu, kiḷikaḷ kaḻaṉikaḷilum kollikaḷilum payir paccikaḷ viḷinta piṉpu avaṟṟiṉ katirkaḷik kottuk kottāyk kottikkoṇṭupōy aṇmiyiluḷḷa marappontukaḷil iṭṭu vittiruppatu. itik kaṇṭavarkaḷ eṭuttukkoḷvārkaḷ.  itu arumiyāy vāyppateṉiṉum eḷitākavum ērāḷamākavum kiṭippatāl, tiṭumeṉa ōr iravalaṉ peṟṟa perum pariciṟkāvatu etirpārātu kiṭṭiya peruvarumāṉattiṟkāvatu iti uvami kūṟuvatu          vaḻakkam. itaṉāl kiḷiyīṭu vāyttāṟ pōla eṉṉum uvamip paḻamoḻiyum eḻuntatu. ip paḻamoḻik karutti yamittē,
oliyiruṅ katuppiṉ āyiḻi kaṇavaṉ
 kiḷimarīiya viyaṉpuṉattu
 maraṉaṇi peruṅkural aṉiyaṉ ātaliṉ
 niṉṉi varutal aṟintaṉar yārē 	(puṟam : 138)
eṉṟu maruta ṉiḷanākaṉār, nāñcil vaḷḷuvaṉip pāṭiṉār.
pāriyiṉ paṟampumali mūvēntarāl muṟṟukiyiṭap paṭṭiruntapōtu, kapilar pala kiḷikaḷip paḻakki avaṟṟāl maliyaṭivārattuk kaḻaṉikaḷiluḷḷa neṟkatirkaḷik koṇar vittu avaṟṟikkoṇṭu noccimakkaḷi uṇpittaṉar eṉṟoru ceyti vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu.
kiḷikaḷ cila camayaṅkaḷil veṭṭukkiḷikaḷ pōlap paṉ ṉūṟṟuk kaṇakkiṉavāyp paṭalam paṭalamāy viḷipulaṅkaḷil vantu viḻuntu katirkaḷi yellām koytu tāmē aṟuvaṭi ceytuviṭuvatuṇṭu. itaṉālēyē,
viṭṭil kiḷinālvāy taṉṉaracu vēṟṟaracu
 naṭṭam kaṭumpuṉalkāl eṭṭu
eṉṟu nāṭṭukkuk kēṭṭi viḷippavaṟṟuḷ kiḷiyiyum oṉṟākak kūṟiṉar muṉṉōr.
viḷipulaṅkaḷil vantu viḻum paṟavikaḷuḷ peruṅ kēṭṭi viḷippatu kiḷiyātaliṉ, viḷipulattiṟ paṟaviyōṭ ṭutalukkuk kiḷi kaṭital allatu kiḷiyōpputal eṉṟum, ataṉi ōṭṭuṅ karuvikkuk kiḷikaṭi karuvi allatu kiḷikaṭi kōl eṉṟum peyar.
kiḷikaḷ peruntokiyāy viḷipulaṅkaḷil vantu vīḻntapōtu, uḻavar ap pulaṅkaḷiṟ pukuntu taṭṭuttaṭṭāy niṉṟu ak kiḷikaḷi aṭittut turattiyum avi koytukoṇṭu pōkum katirkaḷik kavarntatum vantaṉar. iv vaḻakkattiṉiṉṟē kiḷittaṭṭu eṉṉum viḷiyāṭṭut tōṉṟiyatu. taṭṭu eṉpatu oru tavaṇiyiṉ uṟuppākum pātti varici.
viḷiyāṭu muṟi : ūrukkarukiluḷḷa oru poṭṭalil allatu veḷinilattil cumār muppatu aṭi akalamuḷḷa oru nīḷ nāṟkōṇa varappukkōṭukaḷ pātattāl iḻuttamikkappaṭum. ataṉ ōr akalap pakkattiṉiṉṟu maṟṟōr akalap pakkattiṟku naṇṇaṭuvil neṭukkāka oru tavaṇikkōṭu iḻukkappaṭum. ataṉpiṉ pāttikaḷ nāṟkōṇamāy amiyumāṟu at tavaṇik kōṭṭiliruntu kuṟukkākat taṭṭukkōṭukaḷ iḻukkappaṭum. taṭṭukkōṭṭiṉ toki viḷiyāṭuvāriṉ tokiyip poṟuttatu. atu oru kaṭciyiṉariṉ tokikku oṉṟu kuṟintirukkum. taṭṭiṉ tokikkut takkapaṭi kiḷittaṭṭiṉ nīḷ varappuk kōṭukaḷ nīṇṭirukkum.
kiḷittaṭṭuk kōṭu kīṟu muṉpāvatu kīṟiya piṉpāvatu, āṭuvār camattokiyāka iru kaṭciyāyp pirintu koḷvar. kaṭcit talivaṉukku iv viḷiyāṭṭil kiḷi eṉṟu peyar.
viḷiyāṭat toṭaṅkumuṉ, entak kaṭci taṭṭukkaṭṭuvatu eṉṟum, entak kaṭci taṭṭiṟkuḷ iṟaṅkuvatu eṉṟum, tīrmāṉikkap paṭum. taṭṭiṟkuḷ iṟaṅkuvatu taṭṭiṟkuḷ pukutal. taṭṭiṟkuḷ iṟaṅkiyavari mēṟcellaviṭātu taṭuttal taṭṭukkaṭṭutal; itu taṭṭu maṟittal eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭum. oru kāciyāvatu ōṭṭāñ calliyiyāvatu eṭuttu, ataṉ irupakkattiyum taṉittaṉi iṉṉa kaṭciyāratu eṉṟu variyaṟuttup piṉ mēleṟintu atu kīḻviḻuntatum, ataṉ mēṟpakkattiṟkuriya kaṭciyār iṟaṅkuvar; maṟṟak kaṭciyār maṟippar.
mutalāvatu, maṟikkiṉṟa kaṭciyār ovvoru taṭṭuk kōṭṭilum oruvarākap pōy niṉṟukoḷvar. kiḷittaṭṭiṉ iru kuṟumpakkaṅkaḷil oṉṟu pukum pakkamākavum iṉṉoṉṟu puṟappaṭum (veḷiyēṟum) pakkamākavum vacatikkēṟpak kuṟikkappaṭum. pukum pakkam ūr nōkkiyum puṟappaṭum pakkam ūrppuṟam nōkkiyum iruppatu perumpāṉmi. puṟappaṭum pakkattu varappuk kōṭṭil kiḷi niṟpāṉ. avaṉukkut taṉ kōṭṭilum maṟṟa varappuk kōṭukaḷilum cella urimiyuṇṭu. piṟarkku avaravar taṭṭuk kōṭṭilēyē cella muṭiyum. pukum pakkattu varappukkōṭu veṟumiyāy viṭappaṭum.
iyaṅkum kaṭciyār ovvoruvarāy allatu mottamāyt taṭṭiṟkuḷ pukuntu, taṭṭukkāraṉālāvatu kiḷiyiṉālāvatu toṭappaṭātapaṭi eṭṭi niṉṟu, irupuṟa neṭuvarappuk kōṭṭiṟkappāl kāl viyātu, mēṟ ceṉṟu veḷiyēṟa muyalvar. maṟikkum kaṭciyār ovvoruvarum ovvoruvari maṟittukkoḷvar. oruvar palari maṟikka muṭiyātātaliṉ, ovvoru taṭṭilum iyaṅkiyavaruḷ oruvarukku mēṟpaṭṭavar mēltaṭṭiṟku ēṟiviṭuvatu eḷitu. iṅṅaṉam mutal taṭṭukkāraṉ mutal kiḷivariyil ovvoruvarum ovvoruvari maṟittuk koṇṭirukka nērum. iṟaṅkiyavar ovvoruvarum tammi maṟippavari iṭamum valamumāka iṭiviṭātu maṟuk kāṭṭic ciṟiya iṭi nērntālum mēṟcellap pārppār. im muyaṟciyil yārāvatu toṭappaṭṭuviṭṭāl avar kaṭci tōṟṟuviṭum. uṭaṉē āṭṭam niṟuttappaṭṭu maṟupaṭiyum toṭaṅkum; veṉṟa kaṭciyār iṟaṅkuvar. iṟaṅkiṉa kaṭciyāruḷ yārāvatu oruvar kiḷikkum tappiyōṭik kuṟikkappaṭṭa elliyi mitittuviṭṭāl, avar kaṭciyār veṉṟavarāvar; aṭutta āṭṭiyilum avarē iṟaṅkuvar. kiḷikkut tappi oruvar ōṭiviṭṭatik kiḷi pōyviṭṭatu eṉṉum maraput toṭarāṟ kuṟippar.
cōḻanāṭṭu muṟi
cōḻanāṭṭil, iṟaṅkiṉa kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉ orumuṟi kiḷikkut tappi ōṭiṉavuṭaṉ āṭṭi muṭintuviṭātu. aṅṅaṉam ōṭiṉavaṉ kuṟitta elliyiṟ kuvittu vittirukkum maṇṇiṟ ciṟitu oru kiyil aḷḷikkoṇṭu, mīṇṭum araṅkiṟkuṭ pukuntu, muṉpiṉṉākat ticimāṟit taṭṭukkaṭṭi niṟkum etirkkaṭciyār ellārkkun tappittāṉ mutaliṟ pukunta vaḻiyāy veḷiyēṟiviṭavēṇṭum. allākkāl tōlviyām.
viḷiyāṭṭu viḷakkam : kiḷittaṭṭu viḷipulam; kiḷit taṭṭiṉ varappu tavaṇi taṭṭu pātti eṉpavi viḷipulattiṉ          varappu tavaṇi taṭṭu pātti eṉpaviyām. iṟaṅkupavar kiḷikaḷ; maṟippavar uḻavar. iṟaṅkupavar veḷiyēṟa muyalutal kiḷikaḷ tām koyta katirkaḷik kuvikkoṇṭu paṟantupōtal. maṟippavar taṭuttal ak kiḷikaḷit turattutalum avi kuvic                 cellum katirkaḷik kavartalum. kiḷi ōṭippōtal koyta katirik kuvikkoṇṭu kiḷikaḷ eṭṭāta uyarattil paṟantuviṭutal.
viḷiyāṭṭiṟkēṟpac cila ceytikaḷ māṟṟavum kūṭṭavum paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
viḷiyāṭṭiṉ payaṉ
1.	pakivarukkup piṭikoṭāmal tappap paḻakutal.
2.	pakivaṉiyum tiruṭaṉiyum ōṭumpōtu paṭik kalattāl tākkap payilutal.
3.	vēkamāy ōṭuntiṟaṉi aṭital.
4.	kiḷikaḷāl viḷiyuḷukkuk kēṭu varātavāṟu muṉviḻippāka iruttal.
kuṟippu	:	kiḷittaṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭiṉic cōḻanāṭṭār 	uppukkōṭu eṉpar.
2. pārik kōṭu
I. nālāḷam pāri
āṭukaruvi : ēṟattāḻa nāṟkacac caturamāṉa ōr araṅku kīṟappaṭum.
āṭuvār toki : iti āṭa eṇmar vēṇṭum.
āṭiṭam : itu poṭṭalilum akaṉṟa muṟṟattilum āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟi : nannāṉku pēruḷḷa irukaṭci amikkappaṭum. uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉpaṭiyō, tiruvuḷaccīṭṭiṉpaṭiyō, piṟavakit tērtaṟpaṭiyō, oru kaṭciyār araṅkiṟkuḷ niṟka, iṉṉoru kaṭciyār pakkattiṟkoruvarākak kōṭṭiṉmēl niṉṟu koḷvar. uḷniṟpār kōṭṭiṉmēl niṟpārāl toṭap paṭāmal veḷiyēṟa vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam oruvaṉ veḷiyēṟi viṭiṉum, uḷ niṟpārkku veṟṟiyāy āṭṭi muṭintuviṭum. mutalil veḷiyēṟupavaṉ kōṭṭiṉmēl niṟpāruḷ oruvaṉāl toṭappaṭṭuviṭiṉ, maṟippārkku (atāvatu kōṭṭiṉmēl niṟpārkku) veṟṟiyāy āṭṭi muṭiyum. ataṉ piṉ, maṟippār uḷniṟpārākavum, uḷniṟpār maṟippārākavum māṟavēṇṭum.
II. eṭṭāḷam pāri
itu eṇ kacac caturaṅ kīṟip patiṉaṟuvarāl āṭappaṭum. eṇmar uḷniṟka, eṇmar pakkattiṟ kiruvarākak kōṭṭiṉmēl niṉṟu maṟippar. nālāḷam pāriyum eṭṭāḷam pāriyum āṭumuṟi yoṉṟē.
3. aṇiṟpiḷḷi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oruvaṉ, oru peruñ caturattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa kuṟukki (ciluvi) pōṉṟa kōṭṭiṉmēl niṉṟukoṇṭu, aṇilpōl muṉṉum piṉṉum iyaṅkiyāṭum āṭṭu aṇiṟpiḷḷi eṉappaṭum.







āṭuvār toki : iti aivar āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : nāṟcaturamāka vakukkappaṭṭa oru peruñ caturamum nāṟkallum iti āṭukaruvikaḷām.
āṭiṭam : poṭṭalum, akaṉṟa teruvum iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : nāṟcaturamāka vakukkappaṭṭa oru peruñ caturattiṉ ovvoru caturattilum ovvoruvaṉāka, nālvar niṉṟukoḷvar. peruñ caturattiṉ naṭuvil nāṟkal vikkap       paṭum. oruvaṉ naṭuviluḷḷa kuṟukki pōṉṟa kōṭṭil             kuṟukkum maṟukkum muṉṉum piṉṉumāka muṉṉōkkiyē iyaṅkik koṇṭu, an nālvarum ak kaṟkaḷi yeṭātavāṟu taṭuppaṉ. avar avaṉāl toṭappaṭāmal āḷukkoru kalli eṭuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. oruvaṉē nāṟkalliyum eṭuttu āḷuk koṉṟākak kiyiṟ koṭukkavuñ ceyyalām. ovvoruvaṉum ovvoṉṟāka eṭukkumpōtēṉum, oruvaṉē nāṉkiyum eṭuttuk koṭukkumpōtēṉum, aṇiṟpiḷḷi toṭa muyalvāṉ : toṭṭuviṭiṉ, toṭappaṭṭavaṉ aṇiṟpiḷḷi yātal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamē toṭarum.
4. caṭukuṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : āṭuvāruḷ ovvoruvaṉum etirk kaṭciyiṉ ellikkuṭ ceṉṟu, caṭukuṭu eṉṟu colliyāṭum         āṭṭu, caṭukuṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. itu, pāṇṭināṭṭil kuṭaṭṭi eṉṟum, vaṭacōḻa nāṭṭil paliccappiḷāṉ allatu palīṉ caṭukuṭu eṉṟum peyar peṟum. caṭukuṭu eṉpatu teṉcōḻa nāṭṭilum koṅkunāṭṭilum itaṟku vaḻaṅkum peyarām.
āṭuvār toki : nālvarkkuk kuṟiyāta iraṭṭip paṭiyāṉa tokiyiṉar, iti āṭalām.
āṭiṭam : veḷinilattilum, poṭṭalilum akaṉṟa teruvilum itu āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟi : āṭuvārellārum, uttikaṭṭic camattoki yāṉa iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoḷvar. iru kaṭciyār iṭattiṟkum iṭiyil oru kuṟukkuk kōṭu kīṟappeṟum.
mutalāvatu, entak kaṭciyār pāṭivaruvatu eṉṟu          ētāvatoru vakiyil tīrmāṉikkappeṟum. aṅṅaṉam tīrmāṉikkappeṟṟa kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉ kuṟukkukkōṭṭit        tāṇṭi etirkkaṭci yellikkuṭ pukuntu, caṭukuṭu caṭukuṭu          eṉṟō, caṭukuṭu kuṭu kuṭu kuṭu eṉṟō, palīṉ caṭukuṭu kuṭukuṭu eṉṟō, iṭiviṭātu pāṭikkoṇṭu etirkkaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉiyō, palariyō toṭṭuviṭṭu avaṉiṭamēṉum avariṭamēṉum piṭikoṭātu taṉ kaṭci ellikkuḷ vantuviṭa vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam varumvari pāṭuvati niṟuttakkūṭātu. avaṉāl toṭappaṭṭavarellām tolintuviṭuvar. avar āṭṭiṟ kalavātu oru puṟamāyp pōyiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉ piṉ, toṭappaṭṭa kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉ iṅṅaṉamē etirkkaṭci yellikkuṭ pukuntu āṭic cellavēṇṭum.
oruvaṉ etirkkaṭci ellikkuḷ āṭumpōtu pāṭuvatit tāṉē niṟuttiviṭṭālum, etirkkaṭciyāriṭam piṭipaṭṭu ati niṟuttiviṭṭālum, avaṉ tolintavaṉāvaṉ. tolitalip paṭṭuppōtal eṉpar. oru kaṭciyār tam ellikkuḷ āṭum etiriyit toṭṭup piṭikka muyaṉṟu muṭiyāmaṟpōyiṉ, avaṉit toṭṭavarellām toṭappaṭṭarākavē karutap paṭṭut tolintuviṭuvar. oruvaṉ etirkkaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉi yō palariyō toṭṭuviṭṭu varumpōtu, taṉ kaṭciyellik kuṭ kālvikkumuṉ pāṭuvati niṟuttiviṭiṉ avaṉāl toṭappaṭṭavar paṭṭuppōkār.
taṉippaṭṭavarkku veṟṟiyō tōlviyō illāmal irukaṭciyārum camamākavuḷḷavari, kaṭcikkoruvaṉāka māṟimāṟip pāṭivaral vēṇṭum. oru kaṭciyār oruvaṉi iḻantuviṭiṉ (aḵtāvatu avaruḷ oruvaṉ paṭṭuppōṉāl) avarē aṭutta muṟiyum pāṭic cella vēṇṭum.
orucila āṭakar caṭukuṭu eṉṟu collāmal pāṭṭuppōl mōṉi yetuki aminta neṭumoḻit toṭarkaḷiyum colvatuṇṭu. (oruvaṉ taṉ veṟṟi vīram valimi mutaliyavaṟṟi etiriyiṭam mikuttuk kūṟuvatu neṭumoḻiyām.)
caṭukuṭu mutaliya toṭarkaḷi mūccuviṭāmaṟ colla vēṇṭum eṉpatu marapu. āyiṉum cilar mūccu viṭṭukkoṇṭē iṭiviṭātu pāṭikkoḷvar. entak kaṭciyil aṉivarum muṉpu paṭukiṉṟaṉarō, antak kaṭci tōṟṟatākum. tōṟṟavark kut tōlviyāl uṇṭākum ciṟṟavamāṉamēyaṉṟi vēṟoru taṇṭaṉiyumilli.
āṭṭuttōṟṟa viḷakkam : paṇṭit tamiḻaracar ceytuvanta pōrvakikaḷuḷ veṭci eṉpatu oṉṟu. atu niri kavartal allatu niri mīṭṭal. ivaṟṟuṭ piṉṉatu karanti yeṉappaṭum.
kuṟumparacar koḷḷittalivar ceyalākavāvatu, vēntar ēvalālāvatu nikaḻvatu niri kavartal. niri kavarappaṭṭa nāṭṭaracaṉ taṉ paṭikaḷikkoṇṭu nirimīṭṭal karanti.
niri kavarumpōtum niri mīṭkumpōtum pōr nikaḻum. ap pōril veṟṟi tarumāṟu koṟṟavi eṉṉum kāḷikkut tuṭikoṭṭip paliyiṭutal marapu. itu koṟṟavi nili yeṉappaṭum.
maṟaṅkaṭik kūṭṭiya tuṭinili ciṟanta
 koṟṟavi niliyum attiṇip puṟaṉē 	  (puṟat. 4)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
caṭukuṭu eṉṉum viḷiyāṭṭu, veṭcippōriṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākat terikiṉṟatu. iru kaṭciyārum veṭci maṟavarum karanti maṟavarum pōlvar. iṭikkōṭu, av virucārār nāṭṭiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa ellippuṟam pōṉṟatu. palicaṭukuṭu eṉṟu pāṭuvatu, kāḷikkup paliyiṭuvatiyum tuṭi koṭṭuvati yum kuṟippatu (tuṭippaṟiyiṟ ciṟiyavaki caṭukuṭukki allatu kuṭukuṭuppi eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.) iru kaṭci yārum tattam etirkkaṭciyārip piṭittu niṟuttuvatu, niri kavartaliyum niri mīṭṭaliyum nikarppatu. paṭṭup pōtal eṉṉum vaḻakku veṭcippōril vīḻntiṟattalik kuṟippatu.
āṭṭiṉ peyar : inta āṭṭiṉāl, nīṇṭa nēram mūccaṭak kavum, tiruṭaṉip piṭikkavum, etiriyiṉiṉṟu tappik koḷḷavum, payiṟci peṟalām.
koṅkunāṭṭu vēṟupāṭu : pāṇṭināṭṭār caṭukuṭu viṟku iṭikkōṭoṉṟē kīṟikkoḷvar. koṅku nāṭṭārō, irukaṭciyārkkum iru vēṟu caturak kaṭṭaṅkaḷi amittukkoḷvar. āṭumpōtu ovvoruvarum tattam kaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷēyē niṟṟal vēṇṭum. veḷiyēṟiṉ vilaki viṭal vēṇṭum.
5. kāl tūkkukiṟa kaṇakkap piḷḷi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oru mutalāḷiyiṉ kālit tūkkiya kaṇakkap piḷḷipōl, oruvaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉ kālit tūkki āṭum āṭṭu, kāl tūkkukiṟa kaṇakkap piḷḷiyām.
āṭuvār toki : iti āṭa nālvar vēṇṭum.
āṭiṭam : itu poṭṭalilum, teruvilum āṭap peṟum.
āṭumuṟi : oruvaṉ valakkiyum oruvaṉ iṭakkiyumāka iruvar kikōttuk kuṭaṅkiyākak kīḻē toṅkaviṭṭu muṉṉōkki niṟka, mūṉṟāmavaṉ av viruvaruḷ iṭavaṉ kaḻutti iṭakkiyālum valavaṉ kaḻutti valakkiyālum aṇittukkoṇṭu, taṉ vala muḻaṅkāli avaruṭiya kōtta kuṭaṅkiyil vittapiṉ, avaṉatu iṭa muḻaṅkāli nālāmavaṉ nilattil ūṉṟātavāṟu iru kiyālum tāṅkip piṭittuk koḷvāṉ. inta niliyil mūṉṟāmavaṉit tūkkikkoṇṭu, mutaliruvarum nālāmavaṉum aṅku miṅkum iyaṅkikkoṇṭirup par. aṅṅaṉam iyaṅkumpōtu, mutaliruvarum, kāl tūkkukiṟa kaṇakkap piḷḷikku mātam aintu rūpā, eṉṟu maṭakkik maṭakkic collikkoṇṭē celvar.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : nōyppaṭṭa allatu naṭakka viyalāta mutalāḷi yoruvar, vīṭṭiṟkuḷ iṭam peyara vēṇṭiya viṭattum, veḷiyē ceṉṟu vaṇṭiyēṟiya viṭattum, avarik kittāṅkalāka iruvar tāṅkic cella, avaruṭiya kaṇakkappiḷḷi avaratu kāloṉṟit tūkkic ceṉṟatākavum, at toṇṭupaṟṟi avarukku mātam aintu rūpā campaḷam koṭukkappaṭṭatākavum, terikiṉṟatu. ic ceytiyi naṭittuk kāṭṭuvatē iv viḷiyāṭṭu. paṇṭik kālattil aintu rūpā nalla campaḷam eṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum.
6. pūk kutiri
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oru pūp peyaric colli, oruvaṉmē loruvaṉ kutiriyēṟi viḷiyāṭuvatu pūkkutiri.
āṭuvār toki : potuvāka, aivarkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭiṭam : muṟṟattilum teruvilum piṟa veḷiyiṭaṅka ḷilum iti āṭalām.
āṭumuṟi : ētēṉumoru tērnteṭuppu vakiyil tavaṟi viṭṭa oruvaṉ, aṇṇāvipōl niṟkum oruvaṉiṭam maṟivāka oru pūp peyaric colliviṭṭuk kuṉintu niṟka vēṇṭum. piṟar ovvoruvaṉāy avaṉmēṟ kutiriyēṟumuṉ, ovvoru pūppeyari aṇṇāviyiṭam veḷippaṭiyāyc colla vēṇṭum. yārēṉum oruvaṉ colla, pū kuṉintavaṉ coṉṉatā yiruppiṉ piṉpu avaṉ aṇṇāviyiṭam oru pūp peyari maṟivākac colliviṭṭut tāṉ kuṉiya vēṇṭum. 
muṉpu kuṉintu niṉṟavaṉ, piṉpu piṟaroṭu cērntu muṉ coṉṉavāṟu viḷiyāṭuvāṉ. kuṉintavaṉ collāta vēṟu pūp peyar collik kutiriyēṟiṉavaṉ, oru nimiyattiṟkuḷ iṟaṅkiviṭal vēṇṭum. kuṉintavaṉ coṉṉa pū varumvariyum, ovvoruvaṉākavum māṟi māṟiyum ēṟiyiṟaṅkik koṇṭē yiruppar.
7. paccik kutiri1
ciṟuvar, kutiripōṟ kuṉintu viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟu paccik kutiri. oru ciṟuvaṉ kuṉintu niṟpatu oru ciṟu kutiripōl tōṉṟuvatāl, itu ip peyar peṟṟiruk kalām. itil iruvakiyuṇṭu.
I. oruvaki
palar variciyāka iṭiyiṭṭuk kuṉintukoṇṭu niṟpar. avaruḷ oru kōṭiyil iruppavaṉ, piṟariyellān tāṇṭit tāṇṭi maṟukōṭiyiṟpōyk kuṉintu niṟpāṉ. iṅṅaṉamē piṟarum varicippaṭi ovvoruvaṉāyt tāṇṭit tāṇṭi maṟukōṭiyiṟ pōy niṟpar. ivvāṟu āṭṭut toṭarat toṭara iṭam peyarntu koṇṭē yiruppar. inta āṭṭu, pala cuvarkaḷit toṭarntu tāṇṭutaṟkēṟṟa payiṟciyākum.
II. maṟṟoru vaki
ētēṉumoru tērnteṭuppil tavaṟiya ciṟuvaṉ oru kāli nīṭṭi uṭkārntiruppaṉ. avaṉatu nīṭṭiya kālip piṟa ciṟuvar ovvoruvarāyt tāṇṭit tāṇṭic celvar. aṭutta muṟi avaṉ kālmēṟ kālvittu nīṭṭiyiruppāṉ. atiyum avvāṟē tāṇṭuvar. ataṟkaṭutta muṟi oru ki ataṉmēl vittiruppaṉ. piṉpu, muṟiyē, uṭkārnta niliyil tāḻak kuṉintum, an niliyil caṟṟu nimirntum, eḻuntu niṉṟu tāḻak kuṉintum, anniliyil caṟṟu nimirntum, ivvāṟu paṭippaṭiyāka uyarntukoṇṭiruppaṉ. piṟarum uṭaṉuṭaṉ tāṇṭik koṇṭē varuvar. enniliyilēṉum oruvaṉ tāṇṭamuṭiyātu niṉṟāl, piṉpu avaṉ kīḻē uṭkārntu muṉcoṉṉavāṟu paṭippaṭiyāy uyarntukoṇṭirukka vēṇṭum. muṉpu kīḻēyiruntavaṉ piṉpu piṟaroṭu cērntu viḷiyāṭuvāṉ. iṅṅaṉam āṭṭut toṭarum. ciṟuvarkku iḵtoru paṭimuṟi yuyarat tāṇṭaṟ payiṟciyām.
