8. kutiric cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oruvaṉmē loruvaṉ kutiriyēṟic cilliyeṟintāṭum āṭṭu kutiric cilli.
āṭuvār toki : iti āṭa iruvar vēṇṭum.
āṭiṭam : muṟṟattilum, teruvilum piṟa veḷiyiṭaṅ kaḷilum itu āṭappeṟum.
āṭukaruvi : āḷukkoṉṟāka iru kaṟkaḷ allatu ōṭukaḷē itaṟkut tēvi. avi cilliyeṉappaṭum. 
āṭumuṟi : iruvar oru ciṟu kuḻi killi ataṉiṉṟu aintāṟu kacattoliviṟ kīṟappaṭṭa uttiyil niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoruvaṉāyt taṉtaṉ cilliyi eṟivar. kuḻikkup pakkamāka eṟintavaṉ muntiyāṭuvāṉ.
muntiyāṭukiṟavaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉ mutukiṉmēl ēṟikkoḷvāṉ. cumakkiṟavaṉ taṉ cilliyi muṉṉāl caṟṟut toliviṟku eṟivāṉ. ēṟiyiruppavaṉ atit taṉ cilliyāl aṭittal vēṇṭum. aṭittuviṭiṉ, mēliruntukoṇṭē aṭutta muṟiyum aṭikkalām. aṭiyāviṭiṉ, kīḻē iṟaṅkiviṭal vēṇṭum. atōṭu ōr āṭṭi muṭiyum. ovvōr āṭṭikkum cilli eṟintu, yār muntiyāṭuvateṉṟu tuṇiyappaṭum.
mēlē yirukkiṟavaṉ, tāṉē yaṭikkāmal, taṉṉic cumantukoṇṭirukkiṟavaṉiyum avaṉ eṟinta cilliyi          aṭikkac collām, aṉṟu nī aṭikkiṉṟāyā? nāṉē aṭikkaṭṭumā? eṉṟu kēṭpāṉ. cumakkiṟavaṉ nīyē aṭi eṉṟāl, mēlirukkiṟavaṉ aṭippāṉ; aṉṟi, nāṉ           aṭikkiṟēṉ eṉṟāl, mēlirukkiṟavaṉ cumakkiṟavaṉiṭam taṉ cilliyik koṭuttuviṭal vēṇṭum. cumakkiṟavaṉ piṉpu aṭippāṉ. aṭipaṭṭuviṭiṉ, mēlirukkiṟavaṉ kīḻē yiṟaṅkiviṭal vēṇṭum; paṭāviṭiṉ, tāṉ kelikkumvari allatu mēlirukkiṟavaṉ tōṟkumvari, cumakkiṟavaṉ cumantu koṇṭē yiruttal vēṇṭum. mēlirukkiṟavaṉ kīḻiṟaṅka āṭṭi muṭiyum.
2. peṇpāṟ pakuti
(1) pakalāṭṭu
1. taṭṭāṅkal
perumpāṉmi iruvarum ciṟupāṉmi palarumāṉa makaḷir ciṟu kaṟkaḷik kiyāl taṭṭippiṭikkum viḷiyāṭṭu, taṭṭāṅkal. itu paṇṭik kālattil kaḻaṅku koṇṭu āṭappaṭṭatiṉāl, kaḻaṅku eṉa vaḻaṅkiyatākat terikiṉṟatu. kaḻaṅkāvatu kaḻaṟkāy allatu kaḻaṟcikkāy. kaḻaṟcikkāy eṉpatu iṉṟu keccakkāy eṉa maruvi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
ceṟiyaric cilampiṟ kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir
 polañcey kaḻaṅkiṟ ṟeṟṟi yāṭum	(puṟam. 36)
 makaḷir.... muttavārmaṇaṟ
  poṟkaḻaṅ kāṭum.	(perumpāṇ. 327 - 35)
taṭṭāṅkal kīḻvarumāṟu palavakippaṭum. aviyaṉittum vīṭṭuḷḷum vīṭṭumuṟṟattilum viḷiyāṭap peṟum.
I. mūṉṟāṅkal
āṭṭiṉ peyar : mūṉṟu kaṟkaḷik koṇṭu āṭum āṭṭu mūṉṟāṅkal.
āṭumuṟi : mūṉṟu kaṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟik kīḻvittu ēṉiyiraṇṭiyum kiyil vittukkoṇṭu, avaṟṟuḷ           oṉṟi mēleṟintu iṉṉoṉṟik kīḻvittu muntik kīḻvittati eṭuttukkoṇṭu, mēleṟintatip piṭittal vēṇṭum. piṉpu mīṇṭum oṉṟi mēleṟintu, kiyiluḷ        ḷatik kīḻ vittuk kīḻiruntati eṭuttuk koṇṭu mēleṟintatip piṭittal vēṇṭum. ivvāṟē paṉṉirumuṟi toṭarntu tavaṟātu āṭiviṭiṉ, paḻamām. paṉṉirumuṟikkum kīḻvarumāṟu pāṭṭup pāṭappaṭum.
  (1)	oṉṟāvatu oṉṟāṅkāy.
  (2)	iraṇṭāvatu irattiṉakkiḷi (allatu īccaṅkāy.)
  (3)	mūṉṟāvatu muttuccaram.
  (4)	nālāvatu nāṟkāli.
  (5)	añcāvatu pañcavarṇam.
  (6)	āṟāvatu pālāṟu.
  (7)	ēḻāvatu eḻuttāṇi.
  (8)	eṭṭāvatu koṭṭāram.
  (9)	oṉpatāvatu ōlippū.
(10)	pattāvatu paṉaṅkoṭṭi.
(11)	patiṉoṉṟāvatu teṉṉam piḷḷi.
(12)	teṉṉimarat taṭiyilē tērōṭum piḷḷiyār.
orutti āṭumpōtu tavaṟiviṭiṉ, aṭuttavaḷ āṭal vēṇṭum. āṭiṉavaḷ maṟumuṟiyāṭumpōtu, mīṇṭum mutaliliruntē āṭal vēṇṭum.
II. aintāṅkal (oruvaki)
āṭṭiṉ peyar : aintu kaṟkaḷikkoṇṭu āṭum āṭṭu aintāṅkal.
āṭumuṟi : muntiyāṭupavaḷ, inta āṭṭiṟkuriya 
aintu kaṟkaḷiyum oruṅkē citaṟi, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi 
eṭuttu mēlē pōṭṭuk, kīḻiruppavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiyeṭuttuk koṇṭu piṭittalvēṇṭum. piṉpu, kiyiliruppavaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟi mēlē pōṭṭuppōṭṭu, ovvoru taṭaviyum kīḻiruppavaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟi yeṭuttukkoṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum.
piṉpu mīṇṭuñ citaṟi oru kalliyeṭuttu mēleṟintu, kīḻiruppavaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭi eṭuttup piṭittal vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, kiyiliruppavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi mēleṟintu, kīḻirukkum ēṉiyiraṇṭiyum eṭuttukkoṇṭu piṭittalvēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam aintumuṟi citaṟip piṭikkumpōtu, mūṉṟām muṟi oṉṟum mūṉṟumākavum, nālām muṟi nāliyum oruṅkēyum, aintām muṟi mūṉṟum oṉṟumākavum, kīḻirukkuṅ kāykaḷi eṭuttalvēṇṭum. im muṟikaḷ muṟiyē, oṉṟāṅkoṭṭi, iraṇṭāṅkoṭṭi, mūṉṟāṅkoṭṭi, nālāṅkoṭṭi, aintāṅkoṭṭi eṉa avvav viṟutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭum.
ataṉpiṉ nālu kaṟkaḷik kikkuḷ vittukkoṇṭu, oru kalli iru taṭavi mēlē pōṭṭup piṭittal vēṇṭum. mutaltaṭavi āṭkāṭṭi viralāl nilattil iḻuttuk I“nfhÊ kokkām eṉṟum, iraṇṭām taṭavi kuttukkiyāl nilattiṟ kuttik kuttuviḷakkām eṉṟum, mēleṟinta kallip piṭikku muṉ collavēṇṭum. piṉpum, avvāṟoru kalli iru taṭavi mēleṟintu piṭittalvēṇṭum. mutaltaṭavi piṭikkumuṉ ēṉi nālukaṟkaḷiyum kīḻē vittu vittu eṭuppām eṉṟum, maṟutaṭavi piṭikkumuṉ an nāṉkiyum vārikkoṇṭu vārikkoṇṭām eṉṟum, collavēṇṭum.
piṉpu, iru kikaḷiyum cērttuk kūṭṭukkiyāka vittukkoṇṭu, aiṅkallum kīḻē viḻātavāṟu, tappu tāḷam talivali mōḷam eṉṟu nāṟcīrpaṭac collik koṇṭu, valakkiyip puṟaṅkiyum akaṅkiyum puṟaṅkiyum akaṅkiyumāka irutaṭavi puraṭṭi vittal vēṇṭum. ataṉ piṉ, aiṅkalliyum mēleṟintu piṭittu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟic cokkal vēṇṭum. cokkutalāvatu cilupputal.
piṉpu aiṅkalliyum pōṭṭup puṟaṅkimēl tāṅki, avaṟṟuḷ etiri piṭikkaccoṉṉa kallip piṟavaṟṟuṭaṉ mēleṟintu piṭittukkāṭṭal vēṇṭum. kāṭṭiyapiṉ atit taṉiyāy eṭuttuvittuviṭṭu, ēṉi nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟi mēleṟintu mūṉṟik kīḻē vittuviṭṭup piṭittu, mīṇṭum 
I.  it toṭar cēlattil kōḻippīyām eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
ati mēleṟintu kīḻē vitta kalli vārikkoṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum; allatu, valak kuṭaṅkiyiluḷḷa nāṟkaṟkaḷiyum ivviraṇṭāka iru taṭavi caṟṟē mēleṟintu, avaṟṟip puṟammēṉōkkiya iṭakkuṭaṅkiyāl uṭaṉuṭaṉ piṭittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉañ ceyyiṉ paḻamām.
orutti āṭumpōtu, mēleṟintu piṭikkuṅ kal tavaṟiṉālum, kīḻirukkuṅ kalli yeṭukkumpōtu piṟa kallit toṭṭuviṭṭālum, avaḷ niṉṟuviṭavēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavaḷ āṭuvāḷ. orutti orē āṭṭiyil maṟumuṟi allatu vaḻimuṟi āṭumpōtu, muṉpu viṭṭatiliruntu āṭuvāḷ. ivi ellāvakikkum potuvām.
āṭṭi muṭintapiṉ, veṉṟavaḷ tōṟṟavaḷiṉ kikaṭ kiṭiyil oru  kalli vittu, mēṟkimēl mūṉṟu taṭavi kuttuvatu vaḻakkam. itu ellā vakikkum potu.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : kiyum kinarampum inta āṭṭāl urampeṟum.  ituvum potuvaram.
aintāṅkal (maṟṟoru vaki)
nālāṅ koṭṭikkuppiṉ, aiṅkalliyuṅ kīḻiṭṭu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi muṉṉatiṟpōl nāṉku taṭavi mēlē pōṭṭup pōṭṭu ati ovvoru taṭaviyum kīḻirukkuṅ kalli ovvoṉṟāy iṭappakkamākac caṟṟut taḷḷit taḷḷip piṭittal vēṇṭum.
piṉpu, maṟupaṭiyum ellāvaṟṟiyum kīḻiṭṭu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi muṉpōṟ palataṭavi mēleṟintu, ati muṟpaṭṭa ovvoru taṭaviyum kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷuḷ nīṅkiyiruppavaṟṟi ovvoṉṟāka neruṅka vittup piṭittu, iṟutiyil kīḻiruppa vaṟṟiyellām oruṅkē vārip piṭittal vēṇṭum.
ataṉpiṉ iru pātaṅkaḷiyum kūṭṭivittu, avaṟṟiṉ mēl mūlikkoṉṟāka nāṉmūlikkum nālu kalvittu, ēṉiyoṉṟi nāṉkutaṭavi mēleṟintu, ati ovvoru taṭaviyum pātaṅkaḷiṉ mēluḷḷa kalli ovvoṉṟāy irupāta iṭikkuḷ taḷḷit taḷḷip piṭittal vēṇṭum.
piṟaku mīṇṭum, aiṅkalliyum kīḻiṭṭu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi muṉpōl nāṉku taṭavi mēleṟintu, ati ovvoru taṭaviyum, kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷi ovvoṉṟāy iṭappuṟam nilattiṟ pottic caṟṟē tiṟantuvikkappaṭṭirukkum iṭak kuṭaṅ kikkuḷ taḷḷit taḷḷip piṭittal vēṇṭum.
piṉpu, aiṅkalliyum pōṭṭup puṟaṅkimēl tāṅki, avaṟṟuḷ etiri cuṭṭiyatip piṟavaṟṟuṭaṉ mēleṟintu piṭittuk kāṭṭi, atit taṉiyē eṭuttuvittuviṭṭu, ēṉi nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟi mēleṟintu, mūṉṟik kīḻē vittup piṭittu, mīṇṭum ati mēleṟintu kīḻuḷḷavaṟṟi vārip piṭittal vēṇṭum. atōṭu paḻam.
III. ēḻāṅkal (oruvaki)
ēḻāṅkalliṉ ivvaki ēṟattāḻa aintāṅkalliṉ mutalvaki pōṉṟatē.
ēḻu kalliyum uruṭṭi avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiyeṭuttu mēleṟintu oṉṟāṅkoṭṭi mutal ēḻāṅkoṭṭi vari yāṭal vēṇṭum. oṉṟāṅ koṭṭiyil ovvoṉṟākavum, iraṇṭāṅkoṭṭiyil ivviraṇṭākavum, mūṉṟāṅkoṭ ṭiyil mummūṉṟākavum, nālāṅ koṭṭiyil iraṇṭum nālumākavum, aintāṅkoṭṭiyil aintum oṉṟumākavum, āṟāṅkoṭṭiyil āṟum orēyaṭiyākavum, ēḻāṅkoṭ ṭiyil oṉṟum iraṇṭum mūṉṟumākavum, kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷ eṭukkappeṟum.
piṉpu, muṟiyē, iḻuttal kuttal vittal vāral nāṉkum tappu-tāḷam-talivali-mōḷam nāṉkum nikaḻum.
ataṉpiṉ, ellāk kaṟkaḷiyum mēleṟintu puṟaṅkiyāl tāṅkavēṇṭum. mūṉṟu kalmaṭṭum puṟaṅkimēl niṟpiṉ, avi kaṭṭāṉ karuṅkal eṉak kīḻē pōṭappaṭum. ataṟku mēluṅ kīḻum niṟpiṉ cokka vēṇṭum.
mīṇṭum ellāvaṟṟiyum muṉpōṟ puṟaṅkiyil tāṅki, etiri cuṭṭiyatip piṭittukkāṭṭit taṉiyāka vittuviṭṭu, eñciyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi mēleṟintu aintik kīḻvittup piṭittu, mīṇṭum ati mēleṟintu kīḻuḷḷavaṟṟi vārip piṭittalvēṇṭum; piṭittuviṭiṉ paḻam.
ēḻāṅkal (maṟṟoru vaki)
oru kalli vittukkoṇṭu eñciya āṟu kalliyum uruṭṭi oṉṟāṅkoṭṭi mutal ēḻāṅkoṭṭi vari āṭal vēṇṭum. oṉṟāṅkoṭṭiyil ovvoṉṟākavum, iraṇṭāṅkoṭṭiyil ivviraṇṭākavum, mūṉṟāṅkoṭ ṭiyil mummūṉṟākavum, nālāṅkoṭṭiyil nālum iraṇṭumākavum, aintāṅkoṭṭiyil aintum oṉṟumākavum, āṟāṅkoṭṭiyil āṟum orēyaṭiyākavum, ēḻāṅkoṭ ṭiyil oṉṟum iraṇṭum mūṉṟumākavum, kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷ eṭukkappaṭum.
kīḻirunteṭukkuṅ kaṟkaḷi orē kikkuḷ aṭakka iyalātār, irukiyiyum payaṉpaṭuttik koḷvatumuṇṭu. āyiṉ, itu attuṇic ciṟappiṉataṉṟu; aṉivarālum oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭatumaṉṟu :
kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷ tūrattūra iruntāl, kikkallip pakkattil vittuviṭṭut toliviluḷḷa kalli eṭuttuk koḷḷalām.
ēḻukoṭṭikkum piṉvarumāṟu pāṭṭup pāṭappaṭum:
(1)	poṟukki ciṟukki pō(ki)ṟāḷām taṇṇīrkkut taṇṇīrk kuṭameṭuttu.
(2)	(allatu, vēṟu ētēṉumoṉṟu.) iraṇṭu irumpu, ēḻaṭik karumpu.
(3)	mūṉṟu mukkōṭu, murukaṉ ceṅkōṭu.
(4)	nāṉku naṭalam, tēṅkāyp puṭalam.
(5)	aivar arikkum mañcaḷ tēvar kuḷikkum mañcaḷ.
(6)	ākkūr aṭivāḻi, aṇṇaṉ tampi peruvāḻi.
(7)	ēḻaṇṇaṉ kāṭṭilē, eṅkaḷaṇṇaṉ rōṭṭilē, mañcaḷ cāraṭṭilē.
iṉṉoru pāṭṭup piṉvarumāṟu :
(1)	tūppoṟukki tūtuḷaṅkāy
	 māppoṟukki mātuḷaṅkāy
	 kal poṟukki kaṭāraṅkāy.
(2)	īr īrttikkoḷ
	 pūppaṟittukkoḷ
	 peṭṭiyil vittukkoḷ.
 (3)	mukkōṇa vācalilē
	 muttuttaṭṭup pantalilē.
(4)	nāṉku ṭōṅku ṭammāram
	 nāṅkaḷāṭum pamparam.

	 nāluveṟṟili vāṅku
(5)	``aivar arikkum mañcaḷ 
	 tēvar kuḷikkum mañcaḷ.
(6)	kūṟu kūṟu cittappā
	 kuṟukkē vanta periyappā.
(7)	ēḻi eṇṇik koḷ
	 eṇṇey maram cērttukkoḷ
	 peṇṇi aḻittuk koḷ.  1
1.  ip pāṭṭiluḷḷa cila coṟkaḷiṉ kocci vaṭivam tiruttappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
ēḻāṅkoṭṭikkup piṉ, oru kiyil mukkallum iṉṉoru kiyil nāṟkallumāka vittukkoṇṭu, nāṟkalluḷ oṉṟi mēleṟintu eñciya irumūṉṟiyuṅ kīḻvittu mēleṟintu kallip piṭittu, piṉpu mīṇṭum ati mēleṟintu atik kīḻvitta irumūṉṟiyum irukiyālum vārikkoṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum.
itu ciṟuputi eṉappaṭum. iti āṭumpōtu pāṭum pāṭṭu puti putikkiṟa pamparam, ceṭṭi citamparam eṉpatākum.
itaṉpiṉ, irukiyilum mummūṉṟu kalli vittuk koṇṭu, ēṉiyoṉṟi mēleṟintu, āṭkāṭṭi viralāl nilattil iḻuttup piṭittalvēṇṭum.
piṉpu, oru kalli mēleṟintu āṟukallik kīḻ vittup piṭittapiṉ, mīṇṭum oṉṟi mēleṟintu ēṉi āṟiyum oruṅkē vārip piṭittal vēṇṭum. itu perumputi eṉappaṭum.
itiyaṭuttut tappu - tāḷam - talivali - mēḷam nāṉkum nikaḻum. piṉṉar oru kallik kīḻiṭa, ati etiri eṭuttuk koṭuttal vēṇṭum.  itu paḻattiṉ oppakkuṟiyām.
IV. pala nāloru kal
oṉpatum patiṉmūṉṟum patiṉēḻum irupattoṉṟum pōl pala nāloṭu oṉṟucērnta kaṟkaḷi mēleṟintu, puṟaṅkiyil tāṅkip, piṭikkumaḷavu vittukkoṇṭu mikutiyik kīḻiṭṭuviṭṭu, puṟaṅkiyiluḷḷavaṟṟi mēlē pōṭṭu akaṅkiyiṟ piṭittu, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu nannāṉkāy iṭakkiyāṟ piṭittuk kīḻē nannāṉkāy vittal vēṇṭum. ivvakiyiṟ perumpālum oru nāṉkittāṉ piṭittal kūṭum.
piṉpu, valakkiyiluḷḷavaṟṟuḷ oru kalli mēlēṟintu atik kīḻēyuḷḷavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiyō palavaṟṟiyō eṭuttukkoṇṭu piṭittalvēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam kīḻē kalluḷḷa vari (allatu tavaṟum vari) tirumpat tirumpa āṭavēṇṭum.           kiyiṟ palakaṟkaḷ cērntuviṭṭāl, uṭaṉē iṭakkiyāl oru nāṉki allatu pala nāṉkip piṭittu nannāṉkāyk kīḻē vittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. ivvāṟu ellāk kaṟkaḷiyum         piṭittu nannāṉkāyk kīḻē vittapiṉ, iṟutiyil eñciyuḷḷa oṟṟikkalli mēlēpōṭṭup puṟaṅkiyil tāṅki, ati mīṇṭum mēleṟintu nilattit toṭṭu, atu kīḻēviḻumuṉ atik kiyālaḻutti nērē vīḻtti mūṭiviṭal vēṇṭum. 
itu  amukkutal allatu mūṭutal eṉappaṭum. itōṭu oru paḻamām.
mutalāvatu puṟaṅkiyāl tāṅkumpōtu ellāk kaṟkaḷiyum kīḻē viṭṭuviṭṭālum, piṭikkumpōtu kal tavaṟiṉālum, nāṉkāy allatu nannāṉkāyp piṭikkumpōtu kūṭak kuṟiyap piṭipaṭṭālum, kīḻuḷḷa kalli eṭukkumpōtu maṟṟak kal aluṅkiṉālum, āṭṭam niṉṟuviṭum. piṉpu aṭuttavaḷ āṭavēṇṭum.
orē āṭṭiyil, aṭuttavaḷ āṭiṉālum, āṭiṉavaḷē maṟumuṟi āṭiṉālum, nannāṉkāyp piṭittu vikkappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷi viṭṭuviṭṭu maṟṟak kaṟkaḷik koṇṭutāṉ āṭavēṇṭum. orutti kaṭicik kalli amukkumpōtu tavaṟippōy aṭuttavaḷ atic cariyāy amukkiviṭṭāl, avaḷukkuttāṉ paḻam.
V. paṉṉiru kal
paṉṉiru kaṟkaḷi mēleṟintu avaṟṟip puṟaṅkiyil         tāṅki, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟimaṭṭum iruviraṟ kiṭiyil iṭukkikkoṇṭu ēṉiyavaṟṟik kīḻē viṭṭuviṭṭu,          avaṟṟi ovvoṉṟākavō ivviraṇṭākavō mummūṉṟā kavō, oṉṟum palavumākavō, vēṟiru viralāl iṭukkip           piṭittuk kīḻē vittu ellāvaṟṟiyum piṭittapiṉ puṟaṅkiyi luḷḷati amukki, atiyum maṟṟavaṟṟoṭu cērttu           mummūṉṟāka nāṟkūṟiṭṭu, ovvoṉṟiṉiṉṟum ovvoru            kalli eṭuttuviṭavēṇṭum.  ik kūṟukaṭku uṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu peyar. nāluṭṭiyiṉiṉṟum ovvoru kalli nīkkiyapiṉ,           eṭṭuk kal eñci niṟkum. av veṭṭiyum muṉpōṉṟē āṭi, mīṇṭum mummūṉṟāka uṭṭi vittu ovvoru kalli          nīkkiyapiṉ, āṟu kal eñciniṟkum. ivvāṟē toṭarntu āṭiṉ, iṟutiyil irukal eñcum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi mēlēyeṟintu iṉṉoṉṟik kīḻē vittuppiṭittu, piṉpu mīṇṭum ati mēlēyeṟintu kīḻēvittati eṭuttup piṭittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam mummuṟi ceytapiṉ, mēleṟinta kalli, mūṉṟu taṭavi ciluppiyum, mūṉṟu taṭavi mēlēṟintu nilantoṭṭup piṭittum, piṉṉum mūṉṟu taṭavi mēleṟintu nilamum mārpun toṭṭup piṭittum, muṭippiṉ paḻamām.
cilupputalāvatu, akaṅkiyiluḷḷatip puṟaṅkiyiliṭṭu veṭṭippiṭittal.
āṭumpōtu tavaṟum vakiyum, ataṉpiṉ nikaḻuñ ceytiyum, muṟkūṟiyaviyē.
VI. pala kal
oṉpatu mutal irupattintuvari oṟṟippaṭiyāṉa ētēṉum oru tokik kaṟkaḷi, mēlē pōṭṭup puṟaṅkiyāl tāṅki orukal tavira maṟṟavaṟṟi yellāṅ kīḻē pōṭṭuviṭṭu, av voru kalli mēlēyeṟintu uḷḷaṅkiyāṟ piṭittu, ati mīṇṭum mēlēyeṟintu, kīḻē kiṭakkuṅ kaṟkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu nāṉku āṟu eṭṭu eṉa iraṭṭippaṭiyāka eṭuttukkoṇṭu, mēlēyeṟinta kalliyum piṭittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ovvōr eṭuppiṟkum, muṉṉum piṉṉum, oru kalli mēleṟitalum atip piṭittalum muṟiyē nikaḻum.
irukal eṭuppiṉ kāy; nāṉku āṟu eṭṭu āyiṉ paḻam. paḻakkaṟkaḷellāvaṟṟiyum taṉ paṅkil vittukkoṇṭu, kāykkaṟkaḷiṟ pātiyi viḷiyāṭṭiṟ pōṭṭuviṭalvēṇṭum. āṭṭi muṭintapiṉ, kūṭutalāṉa kaṟkaḷip piṭittiruppavaḷ kelittavaḷāvaḷ.
piṟa iyalpukaḷum ceytikaḷum muṟkūṟiyaviyē.
VII. patiṉāṟāṅ kal
patiṉāṟu kaṟkaḷikkoṇṭu āṭuvatu patiṉāṟāṅkal. itu palavakiyāy āṭappeṟum. vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaṭṭārattār iti āṭuvar.
2. kiccuk kiccut tampalam
āṭṭiṉ peyar : tiriyi maṇṇuḷ vittu maṟittuk kiccuk kiccut tampalam eṉṟu colliyāṭum āṭṭu, ac colliyē peyarākakkoṇṭatu. itu pāṇṭināṭṭil tirittiri pommutiri eṉa vaḻaṅkum. 
āṭuvār toki : iru ciṟumiyar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : oru muḻa nīḷamum nālviral uyaramumuḷḷa oru ciṟumaṇ allatu maṇaṟkariyum, oruviral akalamum iruviral nīḷamumuḷḷa oru tuṇittiriyum, iti āṭukaruviyām. tirikkup patilākac cilaviṭattuk kucciyum vittukkoḷvatuṇṭu. potuvāka, pāṇṭināṭṭil tiriyum cōḻakoṅku nāṭṭil kuccum vittuk koḷḷappaṭum.
āṭiṭam : maṇṇum maṇalum uḷḷa iṭamellām iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : orutti, tiriyip paṟṟikkoṇṭirukkum taṉ valakkip peruviral āṭkāṭṭiviralkaḷi maṇkariyiṉ valappakkattilum, veṟumaṉē poruttiyirukkum iṭakkip peruviral āṭkāṭṭi viralkaḷi ataṉ iṭappakkattilum,           vittu uṭpukutti ataṉ oru kōṭiyiṉiṉṟu maṟu kōṭivari muṉṉum piṉṉumākap palamuṟi nakartti yiyakki, tiriyi           maṟivāka ōriṭattil vittuviṭṭup piṟitōriṭattil vittatāka naṭittukkāṭṭi, tiriyuḷḷa viṭattiṟ kivikkumpaṭi taṉ         etiriyik kēṭpāḷ. etiri taṉ irukikaḷiyum kōttut tāṉ aiyuṟṟaviṭattiṟ kariyiṉ kuṟukkē potti vippāḷ. avaḷ cariyāṉa iṭattilum vittirukkalām; tavaṟāṉa iṭattilum vittirukkalām. cariyāṉa iṭattil vittiruntāl pottiṉa          vaḷum, tavaṟāṉa iṭattil vittiruntāl tiriyi vittavaḷum, veṉṟavarāvar. ovvoru veṟṟikkum aṭiyāḷamāka         ovvoru ciṟumaṇ kuviyal vikkappaṭum. yārāyiṉum, veṉṟavaḷē aṭutta muṟi āṭavēṇṭum.
tiriyi maṇṇukkuḷ vittu maṟikkumpōtu, kiccuk kiccut tampalam kīyāk kīyāt tampalam, maccu maccut tampalam māyā māyāt tampalam eṉṟu pāṭuvatuṇṭu. pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟ kīḻvaru māṟu pāṭappaṭum :
tirittiri pommutiri
 tiri kālaṭi pommutiri
 kācu koṇṭu pommutiri
 kaṭiyilē koṇṭum pommutiri
 nālu karaṇṭi nalleṇṇey
 nāṟpat toru tīvaṭṭi
 kaḷḷaṉ varukiṟāṉ kataviyaṭi
 veḷḷacci varukiṟāḷ viḷakkēṟṟu
 varukiṟār aiyā cuppiyā
 vaḻivi ṭammā mīṉāṭci.
itil, varukiṟāṉ varukiṟāḷ varukiṟār eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ, muṟiyē, vāṟāṉ, vāṟāḷ, vāṟār eṉak kocci vaṭiviṟ pāṭappaṭum.
pattumuṟi munti veṉṟavaḷ, etiriyiṉ kūṭṭukki nirampa maṇṇaḷḷi vittu ataṉuḷ tiriyi (allatu kucci) vittu maṟittu, maṇ naṭuvil eccit tuppi atil oru ciṟṟeṟumpip piṭittup pōṭṭu, etiriyiṉ kaṇpaṭṭimayir allatu talimayir oṉṟi avaḷip piṭuṅkaccolli atiyum av         vecciṉmēl iṭṭu, avaḷ kaṇṇi iṟukappotti aimpatu kacattolivu koṇṭu ceṉṟu maṇṇik kīḻē          koṭṭuvittu, piṉpu puṟappaṭṭa iṭattiṟkuk koṇṭuvantu, kaṇṇip pottiṉa kiyi eṭuttuviṭṭu, maṇ koṭṭiṉa iṭattiṟkup pōyt tiriyi (allatu kucci) eṭuttukkoṇaṭu varaccolvāḷ. etiri eṭuttukkoṇṭu vantuviṭṭāl taṇṭaṉiyilli; illāviṭiṉ avaḷ taliyil oru kuṭṭuk kuṭṭappaṭum. etiri maṇ koṭṭiya iṭatti eḷitāyk kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyātapaṭi, avaḷik kaṇpottik koṇṭupōmpōtum koṇṭu varumpōtum, nērākac ceṉṟu nērāka varāmal vaḷintu vaḷintu pala ticiyil pōyvaruvatu vaḻakkam. veṉṟavaḷ tōṟṟavaḷik kuṭṭumpōtu, pakkattilirukkum piḷḷikaḷum āḷukkoṉṟu kuṭṭuvatu cilaviṭattuṇṭu.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : orukāl itu koḷḷit toḻili ṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : niṉivāṟṟalum ticiyaṟiyun tiṟaṉum itaṉāl vaḷarkkap peṟalām.
3. kuṟiñci (kuñci)
potuvāka aivarkkuk kuṟiyāta pala piḷḷikaḷ variciyākavō vaṭṭamākavō kūṭṭamākavō niṉṟu koṇṭu, avaruḷ orutti ēṉi ovvoruttiyiyum varicip paṭi cuṭṭi, oṇṇari, ṭūvari, ṭikkari, āvaṉ, cāvaṉ, iṅkilīṣ, meṉ, piḷuṉ, pōṭiṅ, ciṭ eṉṟu colluvāḷ. ciṭ eṉṟa collappaṭṭa piḷḷi nīṅkiviṭa vēṇṭum. piṉpu, mīṇṭum mīṇṭum pala taṭavi ic coṟkaḷ collappaṭum. ovvoru taṭaviyum ciṭ eṉṟu collappaṭṭa piḷḷi nīṅkiviṭa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu ceyyiṉ iṟutiyil oru piḷḷi eñciniṟkum. avaḷ maṟṟavari ōṭit toṭavēṇṭum.
āṭṭan toṭaṅkumuṉ kālāl oru vaṭṭam pōṭappaṭum. toṭavēṇṭiyavaḷ piṟarellām ōṭippōy oḷintu koḷḷumvari, taṉ kaṇṇi mūṭikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. ellārum oḷintukoṇṭapiṉ varalāmā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, avar varalām eṉṟu coṉṉapiṉ, toṭavēṇṭiyavaḷ aṅkumiṅkum ōṭippōyp palaviṭaṅkaḷilum tēṭippārppāḷ. oḷintiruntavar ōṭivantu vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ niṉṟuviṭiṉ, avarit toṭal kūṭātu. vaṭṭam nōkki ōṭivarumpōtu, āṭi aviḻntuviṭṭālum kīḻē viḻuntuviṭṭālum, aṉṟum avarit toṭalkūṭātu. cilar, oḷintirāmalē nīṇṭanēram āṭṭamkāṭṭi vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ vantu niṉṟukoḷvar.
vaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē toṭappaṭṭavaḷ, aṭutta muṟi muṉ coṉṉavāṟu piṟarit toṭalvēṇṭum.
vaṭṭattiṟkup patilāka ōr ellipporuḷ kuṟikkappaṭu vatumuṇṭu. av vellip poruḷit toṭṭuviṭṭālum, ataṉpiṉ toṭutal kūṭātu.
(2) iravāṭṭu
1. pākkuveṭṭiyik kāṇōmē
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pākkuveṭṭiyik kāṇōmē eṉṟu collit toṭaṅkum viḷiyāṭṭu, ac colliyē peyarākak koṇṭatu; itu vaṭakoṅku nāṭṭil paruppuccaṭṭi eṉappaṭum.
āṭuvār toki : potuvāka aṟuvarkkuk kuṟiyāta palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭiṭam : ūrppoṭṭal iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : talimiyāṉa iru petumpiyār1 aṇṇāviyarpōl etiretir niṉṟukoḷvar. avaruḷ oruttiyiṉ piṉṉāl, ēṉic ciṟumiyarellārum orutti ariyāṭiyi iṉṉorutti  paṟṟikkoṇṭu variciyāy niṟpar. iṉṉorutti, av varicikku etir niṉṟu maṟukkāṭṭi valamum iṭamum cuṟṟicceṉṟu, variciyāy niṟkum ciṟumiyaruḷ aṇṇāviyoḻinta piṟaruḷ oruttiyi allatu palarit toṭamuyalvāḷ. avaḷ valañ cellum pōtu iṭamum, iṭañ cellum pōtu valamumāka, variciyāy niṟkuñ ciṟumiyar vaḷintu vaḷintu iyaṅkuvar. toṭappaṭṭa peṇ nīṅkiviṭa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam (aṇṇāviyoḻinta) ellāp peṇkaḷum toṭappaṭumvari, āṭṭut toṭaralām.
ōr āṭṭi muṭintapiṉ maṟumuṟiyum muṉ pōṉṟē āṭappeṟum.
āṭṭu nikaḻumpōtu, taṉittu niṟpavaḷum varici mutalviyumāṉa aṇṇāviyar iruvarum, piṉ varumāṟu
1.  eṭṭu akavi mutal 11 akavi variyuḷḷa peṇ petumpi.
pāṭṭuppāṭi neṭukalum uṟaḻnturippar. pāṭṭu muṭintavuṭaṉ tiruppappaṭum.
pāṇṭināṭṭup pāṭṭu
(1)	ta :	pākkuveṭṭiyik kāṇōmē.
	va :	tēṭi ōṭip piṭittukkoḷ.
(2)	ta :	veṟṟilip peṭṭiyik kāṇōmē.
	va :	tēṭi ōṭip piṭittukkoḷ.
(3)	ta :	āṭukiṭakkiṟa kiṭiyip pār.
	va :	āṭṭup piḻukkiyit tūrttup pār.
(4)	ta :	kuṭṭi kiṭakkiṟa kiṭiyip pār.
	va :	kuṭṭip piḻukkiyit tūrttup pār.
(5)	ta :	pallē valikkutē
	va :	nellik koṟittukkoḷ.
koṅkunāṭṭup pāṭṭu
(1)	ta :	paruppuc caṭṭi.
	va :	tiruppi nakku.1
(2)	ta :	vāḻi yili.
	va :	vaḻittu nakku.2
(3)	ta :	ūciyālē kuttuvēṉ.
	va :	vīṭṭumēlē ēṟuvēṉ.
(4)	ta :	kiṇaṟṟilē kutippēṉ.
	va :	kalleṭuttup pōṭuvēṉ.
(5)	ta :	taliyē nōkutē.
	va :	taliyaṇi pōṭṭukkoḷ.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : inta āṭṭu, nari āṭṭukkuṭṭi kaḷiyō, paruntu kōḻik kuñcukaḷiyō, piṭippatiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākat terikiṉṟatu.
1,2.	`nakku eṉṉuñ coṟkup patilāka `nokku eṉṟu vittukkoḷḷalām. nokkutal = uṇṭu kuṟiyac ceytal, mikutiyāka vuṇṇutal.
2. nilāk kuppal
āṭuvārellārum 1ām kaṭci 2ām kaṭci eṉa iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoḷvar.
piṉpu, iru kaṭciyārum vevvēṟu pakkamākac ceṉṟu, kuṟippiṭṭa ellikkuḷ, maraniḻalum teruccantum poṭṭalum pōṉṟa pala iṭaṅkaḷil āḷukkoṉṟākac ciṟuciṟu 
maṇ kuppalkaḷi vittuviṭṭu vantuviṭuvar. maṇ 
kuppalukku aṭiyāḷamāka mēlē kuccu naṭṭuvippatumuṇṭu. cila camayaṅkaḷil maṇ kuppalukkup patilākat teṉṉimaram varivar.
iru kaṭciyārum kuppal vittuviṭṭuvanta piṉ, ovvoru kaṭciyārum oruṅkē ceṉṟu, etirk kaṭciyār vitta kuppalkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittu aḻittuviṭuvar. mutalāvatu, oṉṟāṅ kaṭciyār iraṇṭāṅ kaṭciyāriṉ kuppalkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭippar. ellāk kuppalkaḷiyum kaṇṭupiṭippatu perumpālum aritākum. kaṇṭupiṭittavaṟṟi aḻittapiṉ, vayiṟu nirampiviṭṭata  eṉṟu   2ām kaṭciyār kēṭpar. mēluṅ kaṇṭupiṭikka viruppamiruppiṉ, iṉṉum vayiṟu nirampavilli eṉṟum, viruppamillāviṭiṉ,  vayiṟu nirampiviṭṭatu eṉṟum, oṉṟāṅ kaṭciyār patilurippar. piṉpu, 2ām kaṭciyār 1ām kaṭciyār kaṇṭupiṭiyāta kuppal          kaḷik kāṭṭi avaṟṟi eṇṇikkoḷvar. ivvāṟē 2ām kaṭciyār 1ām kaṭciyāriṉ kuppalkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭikkumpōtum nikapam. iṅṅaṉam iru kaṭciyārum kaṇṭupiṭittu muṭintapiṉ, ekkaṭciyār mikutiyāṉa kuppalkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittiruk            kiṉṟaṉarō, ak kaṭciyār tam kaṭciyi aracaṉ (rājā) eṉṟu collik koḷvar. iṅṅaṉam palamuṟi āṭi, 50 allatu 
100 eṉak kuṟitta eṇṇikkiyi muntiyaṭinta kaṭciyār veṉṟavarāvar. tōṟṟa kaṭciyār tōppukkaraṇam pōṭal         vēṇṭum. kuppalukkup patilākat teṉṉimaram variyappaṭiṉ, ataṉ ovvoru kōṭṭiṟkum eṇṇikkiyuṇṭu.
maṟikkappaṭṭatik kaṇṭupiṭikkum payiṟci iv viḷi yāṭṭiluṇṭu eṉalām. inta āṭṭuc ciṟumiyarukkuriyatāyiṉum, aintāṇṭiṟkum pattāṇṭiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa ciṟuvarum itiṟ kalantukoḷvar.
3. paṉṉīrkkuḷattil muḻukutal
patiṉoru peṇpiḷḷikaḷ kūṭit teruvil ōriṭattil vaṭṭamāy  uṭkārntapiṉ, avaruḷ talimiyāṉavaḷ ovvoruttiyiṉ muṭṭikkālkaḷiyum ovvoṉṟāka varicippaṭi cuṟṟic cuṟṟit toṭṭukkoṇṭu.
	1	2	3	4
	oruppuṭṭam	tiruppuṭṭam	ōṭivā	maṅkaḷam
	5	6	7	8
	cekkit	tiruppic	cevveṇṇey	vārttu
	9	10	11	12
	māṭuṅ	kaṉṟum	varukiṟa	vēḷi
	13	14	15	16
	mañcaḷ	taṇṇīr	teḷikkiṟa	vēḷi
	17	18	19
	kāli	maṭakkaṭi	kāmāṭci
eṉṉum maraput toṭari, toṭukikkoṉṟākap pattoṉpatu cīrpaṭac colvāḷ. kāmāṭci eṉṟu muṭikiṟa peṇ uṭaṉē oru kāli maṭakki uṭkāra vēṇṭum (atāvatu maṇṭiyiṭṭuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum). iṅṅaṉamē mīṇṭum mīṇṭum am maraput toṭar collappaṭṭu, ovvoru taṭaviyum kāmāṭci eṉṟu muṭikiṟa peṇ taṉ kāli maṭakkikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. iraṇṭān taṭaviyākak kāmāṭci eṉṟu muṭiyum peṇ taṉ maṟu kāliyum maṭakkiṉavuṭaṉ eḻuntu pōyviṭa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ēṉip patiṉmarum eḻuntupōy ōriṭattiṟ kūṭṭamāyiruppar.
piṉpu, talimiyāṉavaḷ ōriṭattil taṉimiyāka iruntukoṇṭu, mutalil pōṉa kāmāṭci ōṭivā eṉpāḷ. avaḷ vantavuṭaṉ uṉ kuḻantiyi eṉṉa ceytāy? eṉṟu kēṭpāḷ. avaḷ, eṉ kuḻantiyik kiṇaṟṟil muṟittup pōṭṭuviṭṭuk kuḷattil muḻukiviṭṭu vantēṉ eṉṟu colvāḷ. nī entak kuḷattil muḻukiṉāy? eṉṟu talimi yāṉavaḷ kēṭpāḷ. ataṟku avaḷ, tayirkkuḷattil muḻuki ṉēṉ eṉṟu patilurippāḷ. uṭaṉē, talimiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām veḷḷiyāyp pōy viṭṭatu eṉṟu colli, avaḷit turattiviṭuvāḷ.
piṉpu, iraṇṭāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci ōṭivā, mūṉṟāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci ōṭivā, eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam eñciyōruḷ ovvoruttiyiyum muṟiyē aḻittu muṉpōṟ kēṭpāḷ. ovvoru taṭaviyum muṉ coṉṉavāṟē nikaḻum. kuḷattil muḻukiṉatippaṟṟic collumpōtu, iraṇṭāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci paruppukkuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ eṉpāḷ. aṉṟu, talimiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām mañcaḷāyp pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟu collit turatti viṭuvāḷ. mūṉṟāvatupōṉa kāmāṭci neyk kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ eṉpāḷ. uṭaṉē, talimiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām eṇṇeyāyp pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟu, collit turattiviṭuvāḷ. nālāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci pavvīk1 kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ eṉpāḷ. uṭaṉē, talimiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām pavvīyāyp pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟu collit turattiviṭuvāḷ. iṅṅaṉam oṉpatu peṇkaḷ ēṟkāta ovvoṉṟic collit turattappaṭṭapiṉ, pattāvatu peṇmaṭṭum nāṉ paṉṉīrk kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ eṉpāḷ. uṭaṉē talimiyāṉavaḷ vā vā vā eṉṟu colli avaḷic cērttu aṇittuk koḷvāḷ. atōṭu āṭṭam muṭiyum.
talimiyāṉavaḷiṭam ovvoruttiyum ōṭi varum pōtu, ōr iṭakkarāṉa marapuri kūṟikkoṇṭu varuvatu vaḻakkam.
1.  iṭakkarāṉa col iṭakkaraṭakki acciṭappeṟṟatu.
(3) irupoḻutāṭṭu
1. orukuṭam taṇṇīr ūṟṟi
 āṭṭiṉ peyar : orukuṭam taṇṇīr ūṟṟi eṉṟu toṭaṅkum pāṭṭip pāṭi āṭum viḷiyāṭṭu, am mutaṟ kuṟippiyē peyarākak koṇṭatu.
āṭuvār toki : iti āṭa nālvarkkuk kuṟiyātu vēṇṭum.
āṭiṭam : itu teruvil āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟi : iruvar kikōttu uyartti vittuk koṇṭirukka avarukkiṭiyē vēṟiruvar allatu palar oruttipiṉ oruttiyāka orutti yariyāṭiyi iṉṉorutti paṟṟikkoṇṭu nuḻintu ceṉṟu, oru kuṭam taṇṇīr ūṟṟi oru pūp pūttatām, iraṇṭu kuṭam taṇṇīr ūṟṟi iraṇṭu pūp pūttatām eṉṟu pattukkuṭam taṇṇīr variyum pāṭik koṇṭu kikōttu niṟkum iruvariyum māṟi māṟic cuṟṟik koṇṭēyiruppar.
pattukkuṭam taṇṇīr ūṟṟi eṉṟa aṭimuṭintavuṭaṉ, allatu ataṟkuc caṟṟu muṉpē, kikōttu niṟkum iruvarum kiyit tāḻtti iraṇṭāvatu allatu kaṭicip piḷḷiyip piṭittukkoḷvar allatu ciṟiceyvar. appōtu varici mutalvikkum kikōttu niṟpavarukkum piṉvarumāṟu uṟaḻuri yāṭṭu nikaḻum.
va :	viṭaṭā tulukkā!
ki :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va :	(kaṇikkāliṟ kivittu) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ1, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
ki :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va : 	(muḻaṅkālil kivittu) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
ki :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va :	(iṭuppiṟ kivittu) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
ki :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va :	(tōḷiṟ kivittu) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
ki :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va :	(taliyiṟ ki vittu) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
ki :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va :	(talikkumēṟ kitūkki) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
ki :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va :	(talikkumēṟ kitūkkik kutittu) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
ki :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
iṅṅaṉam uṟaḻnturiyāṭiyapiṉ, āṭṭuk kaṟiyum cōṟum tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā! eṉṟu varici mutalvi kūṟiyavuṭaṉ, ciṟiceyyappaṭṭa piḷḷi viṭutali ceyyap paṭum. atōṭu viḷiyāṭṭu muṭiyum. viṭaṭā tulukkā! eṉṉum ēvalum, viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā eṉṉum maṟuppum, orumi kuṟittaṉavēṉum, kikōttu niṟpavar iruvarum oruṅkē ēvappaṭuvatum oruṅkē maṟutturippatumē marapām.
1.	`poṉ tarukiṟēṉ eṉpatu `poṉṉu tāṟēṉ eṉṟu kocci vaṭiviṟ collappaṭum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : araṇmaṉip pūntōṭṭattiṟkuk kāvalāyirunta oru makamatiyaṉ, taṉ kāval tōṭṭattil pūppaṟittatoru piḷḷiyip piṭittukkoṇṭu, avaḷ peṟṟōr evvaḷavu paṇantantum ēṟkāmal kaṭiciyil pulavu allatu kaṟiccōṟu taruvatākac coṉṉavuṭaṉ, ap piḷḷiyi viṭṭuviṭṭa ceytiyi, naṭittuk kāṭṭuvatupōl uḷḷatu iv viḷiyāṭṭu.
2. eṉ ulakki kuttuk kuttu
āṭu muṟi : irupatu ciṟumiyarpōl oru vīṭṭu muṟṟattil vaṭṭamāka neruṅki yiruntu, naṭuvaṇ (miyam) nōkkik kālnīṭṭi orutti pātattōṭu orutti pātattic cērttuvittu, ovvorut tiyum iru muḻaṅkiyālum iru vilāviyum iṭiviṭātu aṭittukkoṇṭum, eṉ ulakki kuttuk kuttu, akkā ulakki cantikkup pō eṉṟu maṭakki maṭakkip pāṭikkoṇṭu, mella mellap peyarntu cuṟṟic cuṟṟi iyaṅkikkoṇṭē yiruppar.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : tamakkik koṉṟum taṅkik koṉṟumākak kuṟikkappaṭṭa īrulakkikaḷuḷ, tamakkiyiṉatu cantikku viṟpaṉikkākap pōka, taṅkiyiṉatu taṅkippōṉa tiṉāl, avaḷ makiḻcciyōṭu ataṉāl kuttiṉa ceyali, iv viḷiyāṭṭu niṉivu kūrvikkiṉṟatu pōlum!
3. ūtā maṇi
pala piḷḷikaḷuḷ mūvar kikōttuc cāṭ pūṭtiri eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu mūṉṟutaram ki acittut tiri eṉṟu coṉṉavuṭaṉ kiyi yuruvik kimēṟki vippar. mēṟki, akaṅki mēṉōkkiyiruntāl veḷḷi eṉṟum, puṟaṅki mēṉōkkiyiruntāl karuppu eṉṟum, collappaṭum. iv viraṇṭil evvakiyilēṉum, taṉiyāka vitta piḷḷi vilakiviṭa vēṇṭum. piṉpu, ēṉiyiruvarum mutaliṟ cērāta vēṟoru piḷḷiyuṭaṉ kikōttu, muṉpōṉṟē oru piḷḷiyip pirikkavēṇṭum. mūvarum orē vakiyāyk kivittiruntāl, mīṇṭuṅ kōkkavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam mummūvarāyk kikōttu ovvoruttiyip pirittapiṉ, iṟutiyil iruvar iruppar. av viruvaruṭaṉ, mutaṉmutaṟ pirinta piḷḷi oppukku eṉṟu collik kikōkkum. muṉpu piriyāta iruvaruḷ yārēṉum orutti taṉivakiyiṟ kivippiṉ, avaḷ vilakiviṭa, aṭuttavaḷ akappaṭṭukkoḷvāḷ.
akappaṭṭuk koṇṭavaḷ caṟṟut toliviṟpōy niṟka, avaḷukku muṉṉamē kāṭṭappaṭṭa oru maṇimāli allatu vēṟōr aṇi, oru piḷḷiyiṭam oḷittu vikkappaṭum. tolivil niṟpavaḷ, varalāmā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, varalām eṉṟu coṉṉapiṉ vantu, maṇimāli vittirukkiṟavaḷ eṉṟu oru piḷḷiyi ūkippāyc cuṭṭikkāṭṭum. illātavaḷ eḻuntu niṟpāḷ. aiyattiṟkiṭamāyiruppiṉ, avaḷ maṭi mukaliyavaṟṟip piṭittup pārkkalām. orumuṟi kaṇṭupiṭikkat tavaṟiṉavaḷ maṟumuṟiyuṅ kaṇṭupiṭittal vēṇṭum. maṇimāli vittiruppavaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittuviṭiṉ, maṟṟellāp piḷḷikaḷuñ cērntu, ūtāmaṇiyik kaṇṭupiṭittāl kitaṭṭuvōm eṉṟu pāṭik kitaṭṭi makiḻvar. itu teruvilum ūrppoṭṭalilum viḷiyāṭappeṟum.
4. pūppaṟikka varukiṟōm
potuvākap patiṉmarukkuk kuṟiyāta pala piḷḷikaḷ uttikaṭṭic camamāṉa irukaṭciyākap pirintu, ōr akaṉṟa teruvilēṉum ūrppoṭṭalilēṉum iru kaṭcikkum potuvāka oru naṭukkē ṭu kīṟi, ataṟku ippālum appālum niṉṟukoḷvar. piṉpu irukaṭciyārum kaṭcivāriyākak kikōttu muṉpiṉṉākap piṉ varumāṟu pāṭikkoṇṭu, māṟimāṟi naṭukkōṭu variceṉṟu mīḷvar.
mutaṟ kaṭciyār : pūppaṟikka varukiṟōm,
	pūppaṟikka varukiṟōm, inta nāḷilē.
iraṇṭāṅ kaṭciyār : yāraṉuppap pōkiṟīr?
	yāraṉuppap pōkiṟīr? inta nāḷilē.
mu :	kamalā aṉuppap pōkiṟōm,
	  kamalā aṉuppap pōkiṟōm, intanāḷilē.
i :	entappū vēṇṭum?
	  entappū vēṇṭum? inta nāḷilē.
mu :	mallikippū vēṇṭum,
	  mallikippū vēṇṭum, inta nāḷilē.
iṉṉapū vēṇṭumeṉṟu colli mutaṟkaṭciyār pāṭi muṭintatum , irukaṭciyiṉiṉṟum ovvoru piḷḷi muṉceṉṟu naṭukkōṭṭi yaṭuttavuṭaṉ, iruvarum oruttiyi yorutti piṭittiḻuppar. kōṭṭiṟkappāl iḻuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭa piḷḷi etirkkaṭciyic cērum. piṉpu muṉpōṟ pāṭi, vēṟiruvar iḻuppar. iṅṅaṉam ivviruvarāy ellārum iḻukkappaṭṭapiṉ, mikutiyākap piḷḷikaḷ cērntirukkiṟa kaṭciyār veṉṟavarāvar.
5. taṇṇīr cēntukiṟatu
nāṉku piḷḷikaḷ, caturamāka neruṅki oṟṟik kālāl niṉṟu maṟṟik kālik kālmēṟpōṭṭu, naṭuviṟ kiṇaṟu pōṉṟa paḷḷam tōṟṟi,
taṇṇīr cēntit taṇṇīr cēntik kuṭattilē ūṟṟu, 
 pūppaṟittup pūppaṟittuk kūṭiyilē pōṭu, 
 viṟṟuviṟṟup paṇattiyeṭuttup peṭṭiyilē pōṭu,
eṉṟu pāṭikkoṇṭu, taṇṇīr cēntik kuṭattilē ūṟṟuvatupōṉṟum, pūppaṟittuk kūṭiyilē pōṭuvatu pōṉṟum, pū viṟṟa paṇattip peṭṭiyilē pōṭuvatu pōṉṟum, avvav vaṭikkēṟpa avinayañ ceyvar.
oṟṟik kālil niṟkum payiṟciyi iv viḷiyāṭṭāṟ peṟalām.
irupāṟ pakuti
ciṟuvarum ciṟumiyarum pāl vēṟupāṭiṉṟik kalantum, piyaṉkaḷum petumpiyarum pāl vēṟupāṭṭāṟ pirintum, āṭum āṭṭut tokuti irupāṟ pakutiyām.
(1) pakalāṭṭu
1. paṇṇāṅkuḻi
I. potuvaki
āṭṭiṉ peyar : neṟkuttum paṇṇipōl vaṭṭamāṉa paḷḷam allatu kuḻitōṇṭi, atiṟ kaṟkaḷiyiṭṭu āṭum āṭṭu paṇṇāṅkuḻi eṉappaṭum. paṇṇiyeṉpatu paḷḷam. paṇṇi paṟittal kuḻitōṇṭutal.
paṇṇāṅkuḻi eṉṉum peyar, avvav viṭattip poṟuttup paṉṉāṅkuḻi, pallāṅkuḻi, paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉa vevvēṟu vaṭivil vaḻaṅkum. perumpālum patiṉāṉku kuḻivittu iv viḷiyāṭṭu āṭappeṟuvatāl, patiṉāṉku kuḻi eṉpatu muṟiyē patiṉāṅkuḻi paṉṉāṅkuḻi eṉat tirintatākac cilar koḷvar. āyiṉ, patiṉāṅkuḻi eṉa eṅkēṉum vaḻaṅkāmiyāṉum, paṉṉāṉku eṉpatu ilakkiya vaḻakkātalā ṉum, paṇṇāṅkuḻi, paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷē peruvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, patiṉāṉkiṟkuk kuṟintum kūṭiyum kuḻikaḷ vittuk koḷḷappaṭutalāṉum, paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉpataṟkup paḷḷamāṉa kuḻi eṉṟē potumakkaḷāṟ poruḷ koḷḷappaṭutalāṉum, paṇṇāṅkuḻi allatu paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉpatē tiruntiya vaṭivamām.
āṭuvār toki : iti iruvar āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : nilattiṟ camamāṉa irupaṭukki variciyākak killappaṭṭa 10 allatu 14 allatu 16 kuḻikaḷum, avaṟṟuḷ avvintāy iṭuvataṟku vēṇṭiya kaḻaṟcikkāy (kaccakkāy) allatu puḷiyaṅkoṭṭi allatu kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷum, itaṟku vēṇṭuṅ karuvikaḷām.
cilar, eṉṟum eṅkum vacatiyāy āṭutaṟporuṭṭu, vēṇṭiya aḷavu paḷḷañ cetukkappeṟṟa marakkaṭṭikaḷi vittiruppar.
āṭiṭam : itu vīṭṭuḷḷum vīṭṭu allatu maraniḻalilum āṭappeṟum.  itu ēṉi vakikaṭkum okkum.
āṭumuṟi : kuḻi varicikkoruvarāka iruvar varici yaṭuttu etiretir uṭkārntu, kuḻikkintāka ellāk kuḻikaḷilum kaṟkaḷip pōṭuvar. muntiyāṭupavar, tam variciyil ētēṉumoru kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷintiyum eṭuttu, valappuṟamākac cuṟṟik kuḻikkoṉṟākap pōṭṭukkoṇṭē pōtal vēṇṭum. kaṟkaḷip pōṭṭu muṭintapiṉ, kaṭicikkal pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷintiyum eṭuttu, ataṟkappāluḷḷa kuḻiyil ovvoṉṟākap pōṭṭuk koṇṭu pōtalvēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam pōṭumpōtu, tam variciyil iṭavalamākavum, etiri variciyil vala iṭamākavum pōṭṭuc cellavēṇṭum. kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻi, veṟumiyāka iruntāl ataṟkaṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷaṉittiyum eṭuttut tammiṭam vittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, etiriyār tam variciyiluḷḷa oru kuḻiyiliruntu toṭaṅki, muṉ coṉṉavāṟē āṭuvar. kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta iru kuḻiyilum kal illāviṭiṉ, āṭupavar oṉṟum eṭukkāmalē niṉṟuviṭa vēṇṭum. ovvoruvarum tattam variciyiluḷḷa kuḻiyoṉṟil 4 kaṟkaḷ cērntuviṭiṉ, avaṟṟip pacu eṉṟu colli eṭuttukkoḷvar. cilaviṭaṅkaḷil, 8 kal cērntuviṭiṉ avaṟṟip paḻam eṉṟu colli eṭuttukkoḷvar.
iṅṅaṉam iruvarum māṟi māṟi āṭivarumpōtu, eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭātu eñciyuḷḷa kaṟkaḷellām orē variciyiṟ pōyc cērntuviṭiṉ, āṭṭam niṉṟuviṭum. iruvarum tāntām eṭuttuvittirukkum kaṟkaḷi eṇṇuvar. kaṭiciyil orē variciyiṟ pōyc cērnta kaṟkaḷellām, av variciyāric cērum.  mikutiyāṉa kaṟkaḷi eṭuttavar veṉṟavarāvar.
cilaviṭaṅkaḷil, orē āṭṭiyil veṟṟiyit tīrmāṉiyāmal, aintāṭṭiyiṉ piṉ allatu pattāṭṭiyiṉ piṉ tīrmāṉippar. ammuṟippaṭi āṭumpōtu, muṉ āṭṭikaḷil tōṟṟavar veṉṟavariṭam tamakku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷik kaṭaṉ vāṅkik koḷvar. veṉṟavar kaṭaṉ koṭāviṭiṉ, irukkiṉṟa kaṟkaḷi avvintākak kuḻikaḷil pōṭṭuviṭṭu, kal illāta allatu aintu kal illāta kuḻikaḷi veṟumiyāka viṭṭuviṭavēṇṭum. av veṟum kuḻikaṭkup pavvīk kuḻikaḷ eṉṟu peyar. avaṟṟil ovvoru kuccu iṭappaṭum.  oruvarum avaṟṟil āṭal kūṭātu.
tōṟṟavarkku orukuḻiyum nirampātapōtu (atāvatu aintu kaṟkum kuṟivāka irukkumpōtu) irukkiṉṟa kaṟkaḷi ovvoṉṟākak kuḻikaḷiṟ pōṭṭuviṭṭu, kal illāta kuḻikaḷip pavvīk kuḻikaḷāka viṭṭuviṭal vēṇṭum. aṉṟu etiriyārum tam variciyiluḷḷa ellāk kuḻikaṭkum ovvoru kallē pōṭavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ovvoru kallē pōṭṭu āṭum muṟikkuk kañci kāyccutal eṉṟu peyar. avvintu kal pōṭṭu āṭit tōṟṟavar, kañcikāycci āṭumpōtu vella iṭamuṇṭu. oru muṟi veṉṟavar maṟumuṟi muntiyāṭal 
vēṇṭum.
ōr āṭṭi muṭintapiṉ, veṟṟiyum tōlviyumiṉṟi iruvarum camamākak kaṟkaḷ vittiruppiṉ, aṭutta āṭṭiyil caripāṇṭi ā ṭal vēṇṭum. mutalāvatu avvintākavum piṉpu pappattākavum kaṟkaḷiyeṭuttu, attaṉiyē pōṭṭu āṭuvatu, caripāṇṭiyāṭalākum. itil virintu viḷivu kāṇalām.
II. kaṭṭuk kaṭṭal
āṭṭiṉ peyar : nāṉku mūlikkuṅ kaṭṭukkaṭṭi āṭum paṇṇāṅkuḻi viḷiyāṭṭuvaki, kaṭṭukkaṭṭal eṉappaṭum.
āṭuvār toki : iruvar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : iru camavariciyākavuḷḷa patiṉāṉku kuḻikaḷum, kuḻikkintāka avaṟṟiṟku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām.
āṭumuṟi : āṭakar ovvoruvarum tattam variciyiṉ iṭakkōṭi (atāvatu iṭappuṟak kaṭici)k kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki āṭalvēṇṭum. eṭutta kaṟkaḷiyellām ovvoṉṟāyp pōṭṭapiṉ, kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻiyiṉiṉṟē kaṟkaḷiyeṭuttu āṭal vēṇṭum. kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻiyil ēṟkeṉavē oru kallēṉum iruntāltāṉ, atiṉiṉṟu eṭukka muṭiyum; illāviṭṭāl (atāvatu pōṭṭapiṉ oru kallēyiruntāl) eṭukka muṭiyātu. veṟuṅkuḻiyit tuṭittu aṭutta kuḻiyiliruntēṉum, piṟa vakiyilēṉum, kaṟkaḷi eṭuttu vittukkoḷḷutal iv viḷiyāṭṭil illi. oru mūlik kuḻiyil ēṟkeṉavē mūṉṟu kalliruppiṉ, nālāvatu kallip pōṭumpōtu, atik kuḻikkuḷ pōṭāmal kuḻikkup pakkamāka vittuviṭa vēṇṭum. ituvē kaṭṭukkaṭṭal eṉappaṭuvatu. kaṭṭuk kaṭṭiya kuḻiyil, kaṭṭiṉavarē mēṟkoṇṭu kalpōṭa muṭiyum; aṭuttavar pōṭamuṭiyātu. iṅṅaṉam oruvarēyō iruvarumā kavō nāṉku mūlikkum kaṭṭuk kaṭṭik koḷḷalām. kaṭṭiya kuḻiyil kal pōṭalāmēyaṉṟi eṭukka muṭiyātu. ātalāl, kaṭṭukkaṭṭiya kuḻikaḷil mēlum mēlum kal cērntu koṇṭē yirukkum. ellāk kaṟkaḷum nāṉku mūlikkum pōyc cērntapiṉ avaravar kaṭṭiya kuḻikkaṟkaḷi yeṭuttu eṇṇal vēṇṭum. mikutiyāṉa kaṟkaḷiyuṭiyavar veṉṟa varāvar.
III. aracaṉum mantiriyum cēvakaṉum
āṭṭiṉ peyar : aracaṉum mantiriyum cēvakaṉum eṉa mūvar āṭum paṇṇāṅkuḻiyāṭṭuvaki, avar puṉipatavip peyariyē peyarākak koṇṭatu.
āṭukaruvi : kaṭṭukkaṭṭaṟkup pōṉṟē, iru camavarici yākavuḷḷa patiṉāṉku kuḻikaḷum, kuḻikkintāka avaṟṟiṟku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷum, iti āṭukaruviyām.
āṭumuṟi : iru varicik kuḻikaḷuḷḷum, naṭu iru mūṉṟu aracaṉuṭiyaṉaveṉṟum, iṭappuṟa īriraṇṭu mantiriyuṭiyaṉa veṉṟum, valappuṟa īriraṇṭu cēvakaṉuṭiyaṉaveṉṟum koṇṭu, aracaṉ mantiri cēvakaṉ eṉṟa variciyoḻuṅkil, āṭakar mūvarum āṭal vēṇṭum.
ovvoruvarum, tāntām āṭum ovvoru taṭaviyum,        tattam kuḻiyiliruntē toṭaṅki āṭalvēṇṭum. āyiṉ, ellār kuḻikaḷilum varicippaṭi kaṟkaḷip pōṭalām. ovvoṉ        ṟākak kaṟkaḷip pōṭṭu muṭinta piṉ, kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa  kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷiyeṭuttu muṉpōl āṭavēṇṭum. kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻi veṟumiyāka iruppiṉ atit tuṭittu ataṟkaṭutta         kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷiyellām eṭuttu vittukkoḷḷalām. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavar āṭalvēṇṭum. kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta iru kuḻikaḷum veṟumiyāka iruppiṉ, āṭupavar oṉṟum eṭukkāmalē niṉṟukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. veṟuṅkuḻikku aṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷi eṭukkumpōtu, yārum yār kāyiyum eṭukkalām.
avaravar kuḻikaḷiluḷḷa pacuvi avaravar eṭuttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.
ellāk kāyum eṭuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭa piṉ, aracaṉ ēṉiyiruvarkkum ovvoṉṟu koṭuttuviṭa vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, avaṉukku 6 kuḻi nirampiṉāl (atāvatu 30 kāy iruntāl), avaṉukku veṟṟiyām. eṉṟum, yār mikunta kāy eṭukkiṉṟaṉarō avarukkē veṟṟiyām. veṉṟavar tamakkaṭutta mēṟpataviyum tōṟṟavar tamakkaṭutta kīḻppataviyum aṭital vēṇṭum. aracaṉ velliṉ taṉ pataviyik kāttuk koḷvāṉ. āyiṉ, aracaṉum mantiriyum mūṉṟumuṟi toṭarntu paḻamāyiṉ, kīḻppatavi yaṭitalvēṇṭum. ulaka vāḻkkiyi luḷḷa ēṟṟiṟakkam iv viḷiyāṭṭāl uṇarttappaṭuvatu, kavaṉikkattakkatu.
IV. acōkavaṉat tāṭṭam
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oruvar tamittirukkumpōtu tāmāy āṭikkoḷḷam paṇṇāṅkuḻiyāṭṭuvaki, acōka vaṉattil taṉittiruntatākac collappaṭum cītiyoṭu toṭarpupaṭutti, acōkavaṉat tāṭṭam eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu.
āṭukaruvi : kaṭṭuk kaṭṭaṟkuriyaviyē itaṟkum.
āṭumuṟi : irukuḻi variciyuḷḷum, mēlvariciyil, muṟiyē oṉṟum iraṇṭum mūṉṟum nāṉkum aintum āṟum ēḻumāka mēṉmē lēṟṟa muṟiyilum, kīḻvariciyil, muṟiyē ēḻum āṟum aintum nāṉkum mūṉṟum iraṇṭum oṉṟumāka mēṉmē liṟakka muṟiyilum, iṭavalamākak kāykaḷip pōṭṭukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamaṉṟi, mēl variciyil mēṉmē liṟakka muṟiyilum kīḻvariciyil mēṉmē lēṟṟa muṟiyilum pōṭṭukkoḷḷalām. eṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, iruvarici yilum ēṟṟiṟakka muṟi māṟiyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatē vitiyām.
toṭaṅkumpōtu kīḻvariciyiluḷḷa peruntokik kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu (atāvatu 7 kāykaḷ uḷḷa kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu) toṭaṅ kal vēṇṭum. kāykaḷik kuḻikkoṉṟākap pōṭṭuc cellumpōtu, piṟavaki yāṭṭukaḷiṟ pōṉṟē kīḻvarici yil iṭavalamākavum mēl variciyil valiṭamākavum pōṭṭuc cellavēṇṭum. kāykaḷip pōṭṭu muṭintapiṉ, kaṭici yākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta peruntokik kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu kāykaḷi eṭuttāṭavēṇṭum. atu kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭuttē yirukkum.
veṟuṅkuḻi amivatum, atit tuṭittu aṭutta kuḻi
yiluḷḷa kāykaḷi eṭuttukkoḷvatum, iv viḷiyāṭṭil illi.
mēṟcoṉṉavāṟu (atāvatu eṉṟum oru variciyi luḷḷa peruntokik kuḻiyiṉiṉṟē kāyeṭuttu) oṉpatu muṟi toṭarntu āṭiṉ, āṭṭan toṭaṅkumuṉ pōṭṭirunta vāṟē, avvak kuḻikaḷiṟ kāykaḷ amintuviṭum. aṅṅaṉam irāviṭiṉ āṭiṉatu tavaṟeṉṟu aṟintukoḷḷalām.
oruvar tamittāṭutaṟkēṟṟa viruppatti iv viḷiyāṭṭu uṇṭupaṇṇa vallatu.
kuṟippu :	paṇṇāṅkuḻi peṇpiḷḷikaṭkē ēṟṟatāyiṉum, piyaṉ kaḷālum āṭappaṭuvatupaṟṟi irupāṟ pakutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭateṉka.
2. pāṇṭi
(1) pāṇṭināṭṭu muṟi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pala kaṭṭaṅkaḷuḷḷatāy nilattiṟ kīṟap paṭṭa araṅkiṉuḷ vaṭṭeṟintu, ati noṇṭiyaṭittuk kālāl taḷḷiyāṭum āṭṭu, pāṇṭi eṉappaṭum.
iv viḷiyāṭṭu, ēṉiyiru tamiḻnāṭukaḷilum, vaṭṭu eṉṟum cilli eṉṟum cillākku eṉṟum eṟikaruviyāṟ peyar peṟṟiruppatāl, orukāl pāṇṭi eṉpatum eṟikaruvip peyarāy irukkalām.
pāṇṭil eṉṉuñ col vaṭṭam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatāl, ataṉ kaṭikkuṟiyāṉa pāṇṭi eṉpatum ap poruḷ paṭalām. vaṭṭu cilli (cillākku) eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, vaṭṭam eṉṉum poruḷiyē moḻip poruṭ kāraṇamākak koṇṭiruppatu pōl, pāṇṭi eṉpatum koṇ ṭirukkalām.
pāṇṭi viḷiyāṭṭiṟku vaṭṭu allatu vaṭṭāṭṭu eṉpatu paḻam peyar. iṉṟum paḻañcēra nāṭākiya maliyāḷattil ap peyarē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
āṭuvār toki : iti iruvar āṭuvar. itu ēṉi vakikaṭkum okkum.
āṭukaruvi : varicikku mūṉṟu āka iru naṭṭu variciyāka āṟu kaṭṭaṅkoṇṭa oru nīḷcatura araṅkum, āṭakar ovvoruvarkkum ariyaṅkulak kaṉamum īraṅkula viṭṭamumuḷḷa oru vaṭṭamāṉa kal allatu ōṭṭāñcalliyum, iti āṭu karuviyām. araṅku, potuvāka, āṟaṭi nīḷamum nālaṭi akalamum uḷḷatāyum, mēṟkuṟumpakkattil, camuttiram eṉṉum ōr arivaṭṭaṅ koṇṭatāyum, irukkum. av vari vaṭṭattiṉ ucciyil ciṟupāṉmi amikkappaṭum oru ciṟu vaḷivu, kumpakkuṭam eṉappaṭum. kaṭṭaṅkaṭkut taṭṭukaḷ eṉṟu peyar. kalliṟku allatu ōṭṭāñ callikku vaṭṭu eṉṟu peyar.
āṭiṭam : akaṉṟa teruvum peru muṟṟamum poṭṭalum iti āṭiṭamām. itu ēṉi vakikaṭkum okkum.
āṭumuṟi : uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉpaṭiyō tiruvuḷac cīṭṭiṉ paṭiyō yār munti yāṭuvateṉṟu tuṇintukoṇṭu iruvaruḷ oruvar āṭat toṭaṅkuvar, tiruvuḷaccīṭṭut tīrppu, kācu cuṇṭi allatu vaṭṭeṟintu aṟiyappeṟum.
āṭupavar, araṅkiṟku muṉ niṉṟu, iṭavarici mutaṟkaṭṭat til tam vaṭṭiyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittu orēyeṭṭil av vaṭṭi mitittu, atik kālāl muṉpuṟamāka veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum noṇṭiyaṭittu orēyeṭṭil ati mitittal vēṇṭum.
eṟiyappaṭṭa vaṭṭu kaṭṭattiṟkappāl vīḻiṉum kaṭṭattiṟ kuḷ viḻātu kōṭṭiṉmēl vīḻiṉum, noṇṭiyaṭittuk kaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷum veḷiyum orēyeṭṭil vaṭṭi mitikkat tavaṟiṉum, kōṭṭiṉmēl mitikkiṉum tūkkiya kāli āṭumpōtu kīḻē ūṉṟiṉum, kālāl veḷiyē taḷḷappaṭṭa vaṭṭu mutaṟkaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu orē yeṭṭil mitikka iyalātavāṟu toliviṟ ceṉṟuviṭiṉum, āṭupavar tavaṟiyavarāvar. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum. aṭuttavarum tavaṟiṉ muṉṉavar mīṇṭum āṭavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam māṟi māṟik kaṭipōka āṭuvar.
mutaṟkaṭṭa āṭṭut tavaṟātu muṭiyiṉ, aṭutta iru kaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, piṉpu camuttirattilum, ataṉpiṉ valappuṟa mukkaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, muṟiyē muṉpōl āṭavēṇṭum. 2ām, 3ām, 5ām, 6ām kaṭṭaṅkaḷil āṭumpōtu, kaṭṭantoṟum mitittu noṇṭiyaṭippatum, oru kaṭṭam iṭiyiṭṭu noṇṭiyaṭippatumāka, iruvēṟu muṟiyuṇṭu. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉatē perumpāṉmi. vaṭṭik kālāl taḷḷuvatilum, mitikkak kūṭiya kaṭṭaṅkaṭṭamāyt taḷḷuvatum, orēyaṭiyāy veḷiyē taḷḷuvatum, eṉa irumuṟi yuṇṭu.
eṉṟum iṭavaricik kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyākavē araṅkiṟkuṭ pukavēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, iṭavaricik kaṭṭaṅkaḷum camuttiramum muṟiyē kīḻiruntu mēlākavum, valavaricik kaṭṭaṅkaḷ muṟiyē mēliruntu kīḻākavum, āṭappeṟum eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
iruvaricik kaṭṭaṅkaḷum āṭappaṭṭapiṉ, araṅkiṉ muṉniṉṟu, āyiṉ araṅkup pakkam puṟaṅkāṭṭit talikku mēlāka, camuttirattil viḻumāṟu vaṭṭiyeṟintu, muṉpōl noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittuk kālāl muṉpuṟamāka veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum mitittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam iṭiyil oṉṟum, iṟutiyil oṉṟumāka, irutaṭavi camuttirat til vaṭṭeṟintu āṭappaṭum. mutal taṭavi etirnōkkiyum, iraṇṭām taṭavi puṟaṅkāṭṭiyum, vaṭṭeṟivatē vēṟupāṭām.
yārēṉum oruvar iraṇṭāntaṭavi camuttirattil tavaṟātu āṭiyapiṉ, āṭṭi muṭiyum. veṟṟipeṟṟavar, araṅkiṟku veḷiyē muṉpuṟa mūliyoṉṟi yaṭuttu, kutiṅkāli naṭuvāka vūṉṟip peruviral allatu pātattāl oru vaṭṭam varivar. ataṟku uppu eṉṟu peyar. ati varital uppu vittal eṉappaṭum. ovvoru veṟṟikkum ovvōr uppu muṉṉēr variciyāka vikkappeṟum. munti veṉṟavar araṅkiṉ iṭappuṟa mūliyaṭuttum, pinti veṉṟavar ataṉ valappuṟa mūli yaṭuttum uppuvittal marapu. araṅkiṟku muṉṉāl taḷḷappaṭṭa vaṭṭu, araṅkiṉ mutaṟkaṭṭattiliruntu orē yeṭṭil mitikka muṭiyātavāṟu eṭṭattiliruppavaṉ, āṭakar tām vitta uppu vaḻiyāka naṭantēṉum noṇṭiyaṭittēṉum ceṉṟu mitikkalām. itu uppu vittavarukkum ēṟpaṭum vacati. iv vacati uppu mikutikkut takkavāṟu mikum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : orukāl, iv viḷiyāṭṭu, oru noṇṭi vaṇikaṉ palanāṭu kaṭantu arumporuḷ tēṭi vantatik kuṟittatāka irukkalām. 
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : oṟṟikkāṟ celavu, kuṟitta iṭattiṟku oru poruḷi eṟital, kālāl oru poruḷik kuṟitta iṭattiṟkut taḷḷutal. piṉpuṟamākak kuṟitta iṭattiṟku oṉṟi yeṟital mutaliya payiṟcikaḷ iv viḷiyāṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
(2) cōḻa koṅkunāṭṭu muṟi
I. oṟṟic cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟ pāṇṭiyeṉa vaḻaṅkum vaḷiyāṭṭu, cōḻa koṅku nāṭukaḷiṟ cilli eṉṟum cillākku eṉṟum, peyar peṟum. oṟṟikkaṭṭa araṅku kīṟi āṭappaṭum cilli oṟṟic cilli eṉappaṭum.
cillikkup pāṇṭi eṉṉum peyarum cilaviṭattu aruki vaḻaṅkum.
 āṭuvār toki : iruvar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : nāṉku camakaṭṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭatum, ēṟattāḻa 6 aṭi nīḷamum 2 aṭi akalamum uḷḷatumāṉa, oru nīḷ catura araṅkum, āḷukkoru cilli (cillākku) eṉappaṭum vaṭṭum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām. araṅkiṉ mēṟpakutiyāṉa arivaṭṭat tiṟkuc cōḻanāṭṭil mali eṉṟum koṅkunāṭṭil karakam eṉṟum peyar. cēlam vaṭṭārattiṟ karakaṅ kīṟāmalum āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟi : mutalāvatu mutaṟ kaṭṭattiṟ cilliyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittu ak kaṭṭattit tāṇṭi aṭutta kaṭṭaṅkaḷi ovvōr eṭṭil mitittuc ceṉṟu karakattil allatu kaṭicik kaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē kālūṉṟi, mīṇṭum muṉpōl noṇṭiyaṭittuk kīḻ vantu cilliyi mitittu veḷiyē taḷḷi, ati ōr eṭṭil mitittal vēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉam piṟa kaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, toṭarntu cilliyeṟintu āṭal vēṇṭum.
mēṟ kaṭṭaṅkaḷil eṟinta cilliyi ovvoru kaṭṭamāyk kīḻē taḷḷikkoṇṭu vantu, aṭik kaṭṭattiliruntu veḷiyē taḷḷi orē yeṭṭil mitittal vēṇṭum.
kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉūṭu cellumpōtum mīḷumpōtum, noṇṭiyaṭikka vēṇṭiyapōtellām, noṇṭiyaṭittē ceṉṟu mīḷavēṇṭum.
eṟiyappaṭṭa cilli kaṭṭattiṟkappāl vīḻiṉum, kaṭṭattiṟ kuḷ vīḻātu kōṭṭiṉmēl vīḻiṉum noṇṭiyaṭittuk kaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷum veḷiyum orēyeṭṭil cilliyi mitikkat tavaṟiṉum, kōṭṭiṉmēl mitikkiṉum, noṇṭiyaṭikkumpōtu tūkkiya kālik kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ ūṉṟiṉum, kālāl veḷiyē taḷḷappaṭṭa cilli mutaṟ kaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu orēyeṭṭil mitikka muṭiyātavāṟu neṭuntoliviṟkuc celliṉum āṭupavar tavaṟiyavarāvar. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum. aṭuttavarum tavaṟiṉ muṉṉavar mīṇṭum āṭalvēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam, āṭṭi muṭiyumvari iruvarum māṟimāṟi yāṭuvar.
nālu kaṭṭattilum cilliyeṟintāṭiyapiṉ, akaṅkiyil orumuṟiyum puṟaṅkiyil orumuṟiyum kuttukkimēl orumuṟi yum cilliyi vittukkoṇṭu, karakamvari noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mīḷavēṇṭum. karakattil kālūṉṟik koḷḷalām.
ataṉpiṉ, talimēl orumuṟiyum valappātattiṉmēl orumuṟiyum cilliyi vittukkoṇṭu, ovvoru kaṭṭatti yum ovvōr eṭṭil mitittuk karakam vari naṭantu ceṉṟu naṭantu mīḷavēṇṭum.
piṉpu, araṅkiṟku muṉ niṉṟu, karakattil allatu mēṟpuṟa veḷiyil cilliyeṟintu, muṉpōl noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu ati mitittal vēṇṭum. piṉpu aṅku niṉṟu araṅkiṟku muṉpākac cilliyeṟintu, aṅkiruntu noṇṭiyaṭittu vantu ati mitittal vēṇṭum.
piṉṉar, mīṇṭum orumuṟi karakattil allatu mēṟpuṟa veḷiyil cilliyeṟintu, kaṇṇi mūṭikkoṇṭāvatu mēṉōkkik koṇṭāvatu ovvoru kaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷum ovvōr eṭṭuvittu naṭantuceṉṟu, cilliyi mitittal vēṇṭum. ovvōr eṭṭuvikkum pōtum amarēcā eṉṟu collavēṇṭum. kāl kōṭṭil paṭātu eṭṭuc cariyāyirup piṉ, etiriyār uṭaṉ uṭaṉ rēcu (amarēcu) eṉṟu vaḻimoḻi var; cariyāyillāviṭiṉ illi eṉpar. ataṉpiṉ etiriyār āṭalvēṇṭum.
amarēcāp pakuti tavaṟātu muṭiyiṉ; etiriyāri yāṉiyā, pūṉiyā? eṉṟu viṉavi, yāṉi eṉṟu colliṉ nimirntu niṉṟu talikku mēlākavum, pūṉi eṉṟu colliṉ kuṉintu irukāṟ kavaṭṭūṭum cilliyi oru kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ eṟiyavēṇṭum. atu ek kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ viḻukiṟatō akkaṭṭattil, mūlik kuṟukkuk kōṭukaḷ kīṟalvēṇṭum. atu paḻam eṉappaṭum. paḻam pōṭṭa kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ etiriyār kāl vittal kūṭātu; eṉṟum atit  tāṇṭiyē cella vēṇṭum. āyiṉ, etiriyāriṉ vacatikkāka, ak kaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē valappuṟattil allatu iṭappuṟattil, kaṭṭatti yoṭṭi ōr arivaṭṭam amittuk koṭukkappaṭum . ataṟku `yāṉikkāl eṉṟu peyar. iṅṅaṉam ovvōr āṭakarum, tattam paḻakkaṭṭattiṉ pakkamāka etiriyārkku yāṉikkāl amittuk koṭuppar.
orumuṟi paḻam pōṭṭavar maṟumuṟi muntiyāṭuvar. maṟumuṟi paḻattiṟkuc cilliyeṟiyumpōtu, atu paḻak kaṭṭattil viḻuntuviṭiṉ tavaṟām.
oruvarēyō iruvarumō ellāk kaṭṭattilum paḻam pōṭṭapiṉ, araṅku kalikkappaṭṭu mīṇṭum kīṟappaṭum.
II. iraṭṭic cilli
mutalām vaki
āṭṭiṉ peyar : iraṭṭik kaṭṭa araṅku kīṟi āṭappaṭum cilli iraṭṭic cilliyām.
āṭuvār toki : iruvar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : varicikku nāṉkāka iruvaricikku eṭṭuc caturak kaṭṭaṅkaḷum avaṟṟōṭu maliyuṅkoṇṭa, ōr araṅkum; āḷukkoru cilliyum; iti āṭukaruviyām.
āṭumuṟi : itu perumpālum pāṇṭināṭṭu muṟippaṭi āṭappeṟum.
iraṇṭām vaki
āṭumuṟi : itu ēṟattāḻa oṟṟic cilli pōṉṟē āṭappeṟum. oṟṟic cilliyil, ovvoru kaṭṭattilum cilliyeṟintapiṉ, karakam allatu mēṟpuṟa veḷivari noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mīḷavēṇṭum. itilō, valappuṟak kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyākac ceṉṟu iṭappuṟak kaṭṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa cilliyi mitittut taḷḷa vēṇṭum. valappuṟa uccik kaṭṭattil kālūṉṟik koḷḷalām. itik kuṟittaṟku atil mūlik kuṟukkuk kōṭukaḷ kīṟappaṭṭirukkum.
naṭantu cellavēṇṭiya pakutikaḷil, orē camayattil iru kāliyum irupuṟak kaṭṭattilum vittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.
mūṉṟām vaki
āṭṭiṉ peyar : itu carikip pāṇṭi eṉappaṭum. 
āṭumuṟi : ituvum oṟṟic cilli pōṉṟatē. āyiṉ iruvaricik kaṭṭaṅ kaḷilum āṭappeṟum. iṭavariciyil cilli yeṟiyumpōtu valavarici vaḻiyākavum, valavariciyiṟ cilli yeṟiyumpōtu iṭa varici vaḻiyākavum cellavēṇṭum. iṭa varici yuccikkaṭṭattil kālūṉṟik koḷḷa lām.
naṭantu cellavēṇṭiya pakutikaḷ mēṟkūṟiya iraṇṭām vakiyippōl āṭappeṟum.
III. vāṉūrtic cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : vāṉūrtipōl araṅku kīṟi āṭappaṭum cilli vāṉūrtic cilliyām. iti ērōppiḷēṉ (Aeroplane) cilli eṉa āṅkilac coṟkoṇṭē aḻippar.
mēṉāṭṭu vāṉūrti tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku varumuṉṉarē inta āṭṭu āṭappaṭṭiruppiṉ, aṉṟu itaṟku vēṟoru tamiḻp peyar vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. 
āṭuvār toki : iruvar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : mēlē kāṭṭappaṭṭapaṭi ōr araṅkum, āḷukkoru cilliyum, iti āṭukaruviyākum.
āṭumuṟi : mutalāvatu, mutaṟ kaṭṭattil cilliyeṟintu noṇṭiyaṭittu ak kaṭṭattit tāṇṭi, mēṟkaṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ  oṟṟik kaṭṭaṅkaḷilellām ovvōr eṭṭuvittu noṇṭiyaṭittum, iraṭṭik kaṭṭaṅkaḷil kaṭṭattiṟkoṉṟāka orēcamayattil irukālum ūṉṟiyum, mēliraṭṭik kaṭṭam vari ceṉṟu, piṉpu aṅkiruntu iṅṅaṉamē mīṇṭuvantu mutaṟ kaṭṭattiluḷḷa cilliyi mitittu muṉpuṟamāka veḷiyētaḷḷi, ati orē eṭṭil noṇṭiyaṭittu mitittal vēṇṭum.
āṉāl, kaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu kutittu mēliraṭṭik kaṭṭattiṟ kālūṉṟumpōtu, malinōkki ūṉṟāmal, ataṟkup puṟaṅkāṭṭik kutittu muṉpuṟam nōkki ūṉṟavēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉam eṭṭāṅ kaṭṭamvari āṭal vēṇṭum. cilli yirukkiṟa kaṭṭam etuvāyiṉum, mēlē cellumpōtu ati mitittal kūṭā tu; kīḻē varumpōtutāṉ ati mitittal vēṇṭum. ākavē kīḻiraṭṭiyilāyiṉum mēliraṭṭiyilāyiṉum cilli irukkumpōtu, atiṟ piṟacamayampōl irukālum ūṉṟa muṭiyātu. cilliyuḷḷa kaṭṭatti viṭṭuviṭṭuc cilliyillāta kaṭṭattit tāṉ oṟṟik kaṭṭampōl noṇṭiyaṭittu mitittuc ceṉṟu mīḷavēṇṭum. mēṟcellum pōtum kīḻvarumpōtum, kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ eṇmuṟippaṭiyē ceṉṟu varavēṇṭum. kaṭṭatti luḷḷa cilliyi mitittu muṉpuṟamākat taḷḷumpōtu, kaṭṭaṅ kaṭṭamākavum taḷḷalām; orēyaṭiyākavun taḷḷalām.
eṭṭuk kaṭṭaṅkaḷum iṅṅaṉam āṭappaṭṭapiṉ, oṟṟic cilliyiṟpōl, veḷḷikkiyum karuppukkiyum kuttukkiyum taliyum kālum, muṟiyē āṭappeṟum. taliyuṅkālum āṭumpōtu, iraṭṭik kaṭṭattil orē camayattil irukālum vittal vēṇṭum.
ataṉpiṉ, malikkuc cilliyeṟintu noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittu, piṉpu aṅkiruntu muṉpuṟamākat taḷḷi avvāṟē vantu mitittal vēṇṭum. ip pakuti noṇṭi eṉac ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭum. itilum, iraṭṭik kaṭṭattil irukālum ūṉṟavēṇṭum.
piṉpu, malikku mīṇṭu cilli yeṟintu amarēcā āṭicceṉṟu mitittal vēṇṭum. iraṭṭik kaṭṭattil orē camiyattil irukāl vittal vēṇṭum.
amarēcā muṭintapiṉ, maliyil niṉṟukoṇṭu  etiriyāri yāṉiyā, pūṉiyā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu,            patilukkēṟpac cilliyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittuvantu ati       mitittu, cilli viḻunta kaṭṭattil oru kālāl niṉṟukoṇṭu, paḻam pōṭṭuviṭṭuc cellavēṇṭumā? vantu paḻam pōṭa vēṇṭumā? eṉṟu etiriyāri viṉavi, muṉṉatu kuṟippiṉ pōṭṭuviṭṭu noṇṭiyaṭittu muṉvaravēṇṭum; piṉṉatu kuṟippiṉ noṇṭiyaṭittu araṅkiṟku muṉpuṟamāka vantapiṉ ceṉṟu pōṭavēṇṭum; pōṭṭapiṉ āṭṭi muṭiyum.
noṇṭiyilum amarēcāvilum tavira, piṟa pakuti
kaḷil malikkuc celvatilli.
āṭṭattil tavaṟum vakiyum, ataṉpiṉ nikaḻum ceyalum, piṟa cillikaṭkuk kūṟiyaviyē.
ōr āṭṭiyil veṉṟavar aṭutta āṭṭiyil muntiyāṭuvar.
IV. vaṭṭac cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : vaṭṭamāṉa araṅku kīṟi āṭum cilli vaṭṭac cilliyām.
āṭukaruvi : cakkaravaṭimāṉa ōr araṅkum āḷukkoru cilliyum iti āṭu karuviyām.
cakkarattiṉ kuṟaṭṭil oru kuṟippiṭṭa vaṭṭat tokiyum, ataṉ ārikaṭkiṭiyil muṟiyē at tokikku varici yoḻuṅkākak kīḻppaṭṭa ciṟutokikaḷum, kuṟikkappaṭum.
āṭumuṟi : mutalāvatu kīḻttokiyuḷḷa kaṭṭattiṟ cilliyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittu ati orēyeṭṭil mitittu veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum orēyeṭṭil ati mitittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu mēṉmēluyarnta tokiyuḷḷa cuṟṟuk kaṭṭaṅkaḷilellām āṭiyapiṉ, naṭukkaṭṭattil āṭal vēṇṭum. naṭukkaṭṭattuḷ eṟinta cilliyi, atu aṅkirukkumpōtum ati veḷiyē taḷḷiya piṉpum, nērē orēyeṭṭil mitittal vēṇṭumēyaṉṟic cuṟṟuk kaṭṭattiṉ vaḻiyāyc ceṉṟu mitittal kūṭātu.
oruvar tavaṟiyapiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum.
kuṟitta vaṭṭat tokiyi munti yeṭuttavar (atāvatu ellāk kaṭṭaṅkaḷiyum tavaṟātu muntiyāṭiyavar), kelittava rāvar. kelippataṟkup paḻam eṉṟu peyar. ovvoru paḻattiṟkum kālāl ovvōr uppu vikkappaṭum.  uppukkuḷ, vaṭṭiyi ṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki oṉṟiyoṉṟu toṭṭukkoṇṭu variciyāka irukkum. veḷiyē taḷḷappaṭṭa cilli oreyeṭ ṭil mitikka muṭiyātavāṟu toliviliruppiṉ, āṭupavar tām vitta uppu varicimēl naṭantuceṉṟu ati mitikkalām.
V. kālippaṭṭac cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : kālippaṭṭam pōl araṅku kīṟi āṭum cilli kālip paṭṭac cilliyām.
kāṟṟiṟ paṟakkaviṭum paṭṭattik kālippaṭṭam eṉpar vaṭārkkāṭṭu māvaṭṭattār.
āṭukaruvi : mūlikkuṟukkuk kōṭiṭṭa oru caturamum, atiyoṭṭiya paṭukkiyāṉa oru ciṟu nīḷ caturamum koṇṭa oru peru nīḷcatura araṅkum; āḷukkoru cilliyum; iti āṭu karuviyām. mūlik kuṟukkuk kōṭiṭṭa caturam nāṉku kaṭṭamāka amiyum. mēluḷḷa nīḷcaturam aintāṅ kaṭṭamākum.
āṭumuṟi : mutalāvatu mutaṟkaṭṭattiṟ cilliyeṟintu, ati orēyeṭṭil noṇ ṭiyaṭittu mitittu veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum ati muṉpōl mitittal vēṇṭum. piṉpu mēṟkaṭṭaṅkaḷilum cilli yeṟintu, eṇ muṟippaṭi kaṭṭaṅkaṭṭamāy noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittu, nēraṭiyākavō kaṭṭaṅkaṭṭamākavō veḷiyē taḷḷi, muṉ pōṉṟē noṇṭiyaṭittu vantu mitittal vēṇṭum.
mali vēṇṭuvāruḷ cilar, aintāṅ kaṭṭatti maliyākak koḷvar. aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷātār, ataṉ mēṟpuṟa veḷiyi aṅṅaṉam payaṉpaṭuttuvar. malikkuc cilli yeṟiyumpōtu etirnōkkiyum, maliyiliruntu cilli yeṟiyumpōtu puṟaṅkāṭṭit talikku mēlākavum, eṟivatu vaḻakkam. paḻamāṉavar uppu vippar. iṅṅaṉam eḷiyamuṟiyil iti āṭuvatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakkamām.
cēlam vaṭṭārattil, mēṟkūṟiya pakutikaḷōṭu veḷḷikki karuppukki kuttukki tali kāl amarēcā eṉpavaṟṟiyum cērttukkoḷvar.
iṉi, uttiyil niṉṟu cilliyeṟintu, 4ām allatu 5ām kaṭṭattil vīḻiṉ uṭaṉē paḻamāvatum, piṟakaṭṭaṅkaḷil vīḻiṉ noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittu, uttivari noṇṭiyaṭittut taḷḷikkoṇṭupōyp paḻamāvatum; cēlam vaṭṭārattil maṟṟoru vakiyāy iti āṭum muṟiyām.
VI. kiccilli
taṉiyāyirukkum ciṟuvaṉ allatu ciṟumi, kīḻē uṭkārntu oṟṟic cilliyaraṅku ciṟiyatāy varintu, āṭkāṭṭi viralik kālpōṟ pāvittuk kaṭṭaṅ kaṭṭamāy vittuc ceṉṟu, āṭik koḷḷum āṭṭu kiccilliyām. itu cēlam vaṭṭārattil āṭappeṟum.
3. kampa viḷiyāṭṭu
nālvar, oru maṇṭapattiṉ allatu catura iṭattiṉ nāṉku mūlik kampattilum, kampattiṟkoruvarāka niṉṟukoṇṭu, etirum valamum allatu etirum iṭamumāka, māṟi māṟi allatu cuṟṟiccuṟṟi vēṟuvēṟu kampattiṟku iyaṅkikkoṇṭirukka, iṉṉoruvar avarit toṭalvēṇṭum. muṉṉarē ētēṉu moruvakiyil tōṟṟavar allatu tavaṟiyavar toṭavēṇ ṭiyavarāvar.
oruvar kampattiviṭṭu vilakiyirukkumpōtum, ēṟkeṉavē āḷuḷḷa iṉṉoru kampattic cērntirukkum pōtum, avarit toṭalām. toṭappaṭṭavar toṭṭavar viṉiyiyum, toṭṭavar toṭappaṭṭavar viṉiyiyum, mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
toṭukiṟavar, pāl mōr tayir viṟpavarpōl, pālō 
pāl! allatu mōrō mōr! allatu tayirō tayir! eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu tirivatu, cōḻa koṅkunāṭṭu vaḻakkum, uccantaliyik kiyāl taṭṭikkoṇṭu toṇṭāṉ 
toṇṭāṉ toṭupiṭi toṇṭāṉ eṉṟu collit tirivatu, pāṇṭināṭṭu vaḻakkum ākum. pāṇṭināṭṭil iv viḷiyāṭ ṭiṟkut toṇṭāṉ toṇṭāṉ toṭupiṭi toṇṭāṉ eṉṟē  peyar.
tīṭṭuḷḷa allatu tīṇṭappaṭāta oruvar tammiṉiṉṟu vilakiyōṭiya piṟari, viḷiyāṭṭiṟkō kuṟumpiṟkō toṭṭa ceyali, naṭittuk kāṭṭuvatākavuḷḷatu iv viḷiyāṭṭu.
4. kaccakkāyc cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : kaccakkāyic cilliyāl aṭittu āṭum āṭṭuk kaccakkāyc cilli.
āṭuvār toki : palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : āḷukkup pattiṟkuk kuṟiyāta pala kaccakkāy kaḷum, akaṉṟa cilliyum, ōraṭi viṭṭamuḷḷa oru vaṭṭamum, iti āṭukaruviyām. cillikku vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaṭṭattil cappātti eṉṟu peyar.
āṭiṭam : poṭṭalil itu āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟi : āṭakar nāṟkacat toliviluḷḷa uttiyil niṉṟukoṇṭu, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ tattam cilliyi eṟivar. yāruṭiyatu vaṭṭattiṟku allatu vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviṟku mika neruṅki yirukkiṉṟatō, avar muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum. ēṉiyōrellām tattam aṇmi muṟippaṭi muṉ piṉṉāka āṭuvar.
āṭakarellārum tattam kaccakkāykaḷi vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ iṭṭapiṉ, ovvoruvarum uttiyil niṉṟukoṇṭu, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ parappi allatu kuvittu vikkappaṭṭirukkum kaccakkāykaḷit tattam cilliyāl aṭittalvēṇṭum. vaṭṭattiṟku veḷicceṉṟa kāykaḷiyellām āṭuvōr eṭuttukkoḷḷalām.
cilli vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ cellāviṭiṉum, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ ceṉṟum kāykaḷi aṭittu veḷiyēṟṟāviṭiṉum, kāykaḷi veḷiyēṟṟiyakkālum cilliyum uṭaṉ veḷiyēṟāviṭiṉum, tavaṟām. tavaṟiviṭiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum; tavaṟāviṭiṉ toṭarntu āṭalām. iṟutiyil, mikutiyāṉa kāykaḷi vittiruppavar kelittavarāvar.
cēlam vaṭṭārattil kaccakkāykkup patilākac ciṟu curuṭṭup peṭṭiyiyum vittu āṭuvatuṇṭu. atu cikareṭ pākuc cilli eṉappaṭum.
5. kuñcu
āṭuvār iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoṇṭu, oru kaṭciyār pañcāram allatu āṭṭuk kūṇṭaḷavuḷḷa oru vaṭṭakkōṭṭiṉ arukum, iṉṉoru kaṭciyār caṟṟut tolivilum niṟpar. tolivil niṟpavar, tammuḷ oruvarik kuñcu eṉa viḷampi, etirk kaṭciyil tattam uttiyip piṭikkac celvar. etirk kaṭciyār kuñciṉip piṭikka muyalvar. kuñcu kūṭuvantu cērntuviṭiṉ (atāvatu piṭipaṭātu vaṭṭak kōṭṭiṟkuḷ pukuntuviṭiṉ), atē kaṭciyār mīṇṭum kuñcuvittu āṭuvar; piṭipaṭṭuviṭiṉ, kūṭṭiṉaruku niṉṟavar kuñcuvittu āṭal vēṇṭum.
kōḻikkuñcu paruntiṟkut tappik kūṭṭiṟkuḷ allatu vīṭṭiṟkuḷ pukuvati, iv viḷiyāṭṭuk kuṟippatupōlum!
(2) iravāṭṭu
1. kaṇṇāmpotti
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oruvar oru piḷḷiyiṉ kaṇṇip pottik koṇṭirukkumpōtu, piṟa piḷḷikaḷ ōṭi oḷiyum viḷiyāṭṭuk kaṇṇāmpotti eṉappaṭum.
āṭumuṟi : mutiyār oruvar, āṭa virumpum piḷḷikaḷi yellām oruṅkē iruttikkoṇṭu, ovvoruvariyum cuṭṭi oru maraput toṭaric colli, at toṭariṉ iṟutic collāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum piḷḷiyiṉ kaṇṇip pottuvar. iṉi, mutiyār oruvarmītu ellāp piḷḷikaḷum paṭapaṭaveṉṟu kiyālaṭikkumpōtu, am mutiyāriṉ kiyil akappaṭṭuk koṇṭa piḷḷiyiṉ kaṇṇip pottuvatumuṇṭu. pottum pōtu, maṟṟap piḷḷikaḷellām ōṭi oḷintu koḷvar.
mutiyār akappaṭṭukkoṇṭa piḷḷiyiṉ kaṇṇip pottikkoṇṭirukkumpōtu, av viruvarukkum piṉvarumāṟu uriyāṭṭu nikaḻum.
mutiyavar :	kaṇṇām pottiyārē kaṇṇām pottiyārē! 1
piḷḷi :	eṉṉa?
mu :	ettaṉi muṭṭiyiṭṭāy? 2
pi :	mūṉṟu muṭṭiyiṭṭēṉ.
1.	`kaṇṇām pottiyārē eṉpatu kocci vaṭivil `kaṇṇāmpūcciyārē eṉṟu tiriyum.
2.	viḷi uyarvup paṉmiyiliruppiṉum, payaṉili orumiyākavē yirukkum. 
itu vaḻuvē.
mu :	avaṟṟuḷ oru muṭṭiyip porittut tiṉṟu viṭṭu, oru muṭṭiyip puḷitta taṇṇīrukkuḷ pōṭṭuviṭṭu, oru muṭṭiyip piṭittuk koṇṭuvā.
pulā luṇṇātārāyiṉ, iv vuriyāṭṭil viḷikkup piṟpaṭṭa viṉā, viṭi, ēval piṉvarumāṟirukkum.
mu :	ettaṉi paḻam paṟittāy?
pi :	mūṉṟu paḻam paṟittēṉ
mu :	avaṟṟuḷ oru paḻattip piḷḷiyārukkup paṭittuviṭṭu, oru paḻatti aṟuttut tiṉṟu viṭṭu, oru paḻattip piṭittuk koṇṭuvā.
iṉi, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu uriyāṭṭiṉṟi,
kaṇṇāṅ kaṇṇām pūcci!
kāṭṭuttali mūcci
ūḷi muṭṭiyit tiṉṟuviṭṭu
nalla muṭṭi koṇṭuvā
eṉṟu ēvuṅ koṅkunāṭṭu vaḻakkum uḷatu.
iṅṅaṉam ēvappaṭṭa piḷḷi, uṭaṉē ōṭippōy aṅkumiṅkum pārttu oḷintukoṇṭirukkum piḷḷikaḷuḷ oruvarit toṭamuyalum. mutalāvatu toṭappaṭṭa piḷḷi aṭuttamuṟi kaṇ pottappaṭum. toṭappaṭumuṉ ōṭivantu mutiyārit toṭṭuviṭṭāl, piṉpu toṭutal kūṭātu. oruvarum toṭappaṭāviṭiṉ, muṉpu kaṇpottiya piḷḷiyē maṟumuṟiyuṅ kaṇpottappaṭum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : paḷḷikkuc cellātu oḷintu tiriyum tiṇṇippaḷḷi māṇavaric caṭṭanampip piḷḷi piṭittu varuvatiṉiṉṟō, ciṟikkut tappi oḷintu tiriyum kuṟṟavāḷi kaḷi ūrkāvalar piṭittuvaruvatiṉiṉṟō, iv viḷiyāṭṭut tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
2. pukiyilik kaṭṭi yuruṭṭal
itu oruvāṟu kaṇṇāmpotti pōṉṟatē. āyiṉ itaṟku oru vaṭṭam pōṭappaṭum; atōṭu muntit toṭuvatu yār eṉṟu tīrmāṉittaṟku, ellāriyum variciyāyk kuṉiya vittu, avarkaḷ niḻalil pukiyilik kaṭṭiyi oruvar 
uruṭṭuvar. atu yār niḻaliṟpōy niṟkiṉṟatō, avar ēṉiyōrit toṭutal vēṇṭum.
piṟarellām ōṭi oḷintukoḷḷutaṟku iraṇṭoru nimiyaṅ koṭukkappaṭum. toṭum piḷḷi toṭavarumpōtu, ellārum ōṭippōy vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ niṉṟukoḷvar. vaṭṭattiṟ kuṭ ceṉṟapiṉ toṭutal kūṭātu. vaṭṭattiṟkuṭ cellumuṉ toṭappaṭṭa piḷḷi aṭutta muṟi toṭutal vēṇṭum. oruvarum toṭappaṭāviṭiṉ, muṉpu toṭamuyaṉṟa piḷḷiyē mīṇṭum toṭutal vēṇṭum.
oruvar toṭarntu mūvāṭṭi oruvariyun toṭāviṭiṉ, avarmītu ēṉiyōr ciṟitu ciṟitu kutiriyēṟuvatuṇṭu. ataṉpiṉ, mīṇṭum pukiyilik kaṭṭi uruṭṭappaṭum.
3. pukiyilik kaṭṭi yeṭuttal
ituvum oḷintu viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭē.
āṭuvārellām uttikaṭṭi irukaṭciyākap pirintu koṇṭapiṉ, oru kaṭciyār ōṭi oḷintukoḷvar. iṉṉoru kaṭciyār, kaṭikaṭkuc ceṉṟu pukiyilik kaṭṭi (allatu veṟṟilikkāmpu) eṭuttuvantu, oḷintavarit tēṭippiṭippar. yāriyēṉum kaṇṭupiṭittapōtu, kaṭikkuc ceṉṟu vantamikku aṭiyāḷamākap pukiyilik kaṭṭiyik kāṭṭal vēṇṭum. kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭavar muṉpu ellārum niṉṟa iṭattiṟku vantuviṭuvar. ellāruṅ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭapiṉ viṉimāṟi viḷiyāṭuvar.
oḷintiruntavarik kaṇṭupiṭikkumpōtu pukiyilik kaṭṭiyik kāṭṭāviṭiṉum, yāriyēṉum kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyāviṭiṉum, muṉpu kaṇṭupiṭittavarē mīṇṭuṅ kaṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum. itu pāṇṭināṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭu. itaṉ cōḻanāṭṭu vaki varumāṟu :
āṭuvārellārum kipōṭṭu ovvoruvarākap         pirittu, iṟutiyilakappaṭṭuk koṇṭavar ēṉiyārip piṭittalvēṇṭum. piṭikkavēṇṭiyavar caṟṟut tolivi         luḷḷa oru kuṟippiṭṭa taḻiyik koṇṭuvanta piṉputāṉ           piṭittal vēṇṭum. ataṟkuḷ ēṉiyarellām maṟivāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil oḷintukoḷvar. kaṇṭupiṭippavar oḷintiruppa         varik kiyiṉāṟ piṭittukkoḷḷalām, allatu avar peyarimaṭṭum piṟarkkuk kēṭkumāṟu urakkac collalām. iv viraṇṭil etu ceyvateṉṟu muṉṉarē tīrmāṉikkap         paṭṭirukkum. kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭavar taḻiyik kāṭṭac collumpōtu, kaṇṭupiṭittavar kāṭṭal vēṇṭum; illāviṭiṉ, maṟumuṟiyum avarē kaṇṭupiṭittal vēṇṭum. taḻi kāṭṭappaṭiṉ, kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭavar maṟumuṟi kaṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum. iv viḷiyāṭṭu kulīmtār eṉṉum urutup peyarāl vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. itit taṉittamiḻil taḻipaṟittal eṉalām.
4. pūcci
pūcci eṉpatu āḷniḻal. nilavoḷiyiṟ pūcci teriyum pōtu oruvarit toṭum viḷiyāṭṭu, pūcci allatu pūcci viḷiyāṭṭu. āṭuvāruḷ ētēṉumoru vakiyil akappaṭṭuk koṇṭa oruvar, nilavoḷiyiṭattil niṟka, ēṉiyarellām arukēyuḷḷa ōr iruṇṭa iṭattil niṉṟukoḷvar. iruṇṭa iṭattil niṟpavar oḷiyiṭattiṟku variṉ, avarit toṭalām; illāviṭiṉ toṭal kūṭātu. oḷiyiṭattil toṭappaṭṭavar piṉpu piṟarit toṭutal vēṇṭum.
5. aracaṉun tōṭṭamum
aracaṉum cēvakaṉum tōṭṭakkāraṉum tōṭṭamum (atāvatu pūcaṇikkoṭikaḷum) eṉa āṭuvār mutalāvatu pirintukoḷvar.
aracaṉ caṟṟu eṭṭattiliruntu koṇṭu, pūcaṇit tōṭṭam pōṭumpaṭi cēvakaṉvaḻit tōṭṭakkāraṉi ēvuvaṉ. tōṭṭak kāraṉ vēli toṭaṅkuvaṉ.
aracaṉ aṭikkaṭi taṉ cēvakaṉi ēvit tōṭṭattiṉ nilimiyip pārttuviṭṭu varac colvaṉ. cēvakaṉ vantu kēṭkum ovvoru taṭaviyum, muṟiyē, ovvoru payirttoḻil viṉi nikaḻntuḷḷatākat tōṭṭakkāraṉ colvāṉ. iṅṅaṉam uḻutal, vitittal, nīr pāyccal. muḷittal, ōrili mutaṟ pala ilivari viṭal, koṭiyōṭal, kaḷiyeṭuttal, pūppūttal, piñcuviṭal, kāy ātal, muṟṟutal ākiya pala viṉikaḷum collappaṭum. uḻutal mutaṟ kāy ātal vari ovvoru viṉiyic collumpōtum, tōṭṭatti nikarkkum piḷḷikaḷ avvav viṉiyik kiyāl naṭittuk kāṭṭuvar.
kāykaḷ muṟṟiyatic collumuṉ, tōṭṭakkāraṉ         kāykaḷit taṭṭippārppatupōl ovvoru piḷḷiyiṉ taliyiyum kuṭṭi muṟṟiviṭṭatā? eṉṟu taṉṉittāṉē kēṭṭu, cila talikaḷi muṟṟiviṭṭatu eṉṟum, cila talikaḷi muṟṟavilli eṉṟum, colli; muṟṟiviṭṭateṉṟu coṉṉa piḷḷikaḷi vēṟāka vippāṉ. kāy koṇṭuvarumpaṭi aracaṉāl ēvappaṭṭa cēvakaṉ cila kāykaḷik koṇṭupōyt taṉ vīṭṭil vittiruppatupōl, cila piḷḷikaḷik koṇṭu pōyc caṟṟut tolivil vittiruppāṉ. ak kāykaḷ kaḷavu pōvatupōl, ap piḷḷikaḷ tām muṉpirunta iṭattiṟku vantuviṭuvar. iṅṅaṉam iraṇṭorumuṟi nikaḻntapiṉ, cēvakaṉ aracaṉiyē aḻittu vantu, ellāk kāykaḷiyuṅ koṇṭu pōvatupōl ellāp piḷḷikaḷiyuṅ koṇṭupōvaṉ. itōṭu āṭṭam muṭiyum. iv viḷiyāṭṭiṉ tōṟṟam veḷippaṭi.
6. kulikuliyāy muntirikkāy
pala piḷḷikaḷ vaṭṭamāy uḷnōkki uṭkārntirukka, oru piḷḷi tiripōl muṟukkiya tuṇiyoṉṟik kiyil vittukkoṇṭu, kulikuliyāy muntirikkāy eṉṟu viṭṭu viṭṭu urakkac colli, vaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē piḷḷi kaṭku arukil valamākac cuṟṟic cuṟṟivarum. ap piḷḷi kuli.... kāy eṉṟu colluntoṟum, uṭkārntirukkum piḷḷika ḷellām oruṅkē nariyē, nariyē, ōṭivā eṉṟu kattic colvar.
iṅṅaṉam nikaḻntu koṇṭirukkumpōtē, cuṟṟi varum piḷḷi tiṭumeṉṟu tiriyi oru piḷḷiyiṉ piṉṉāl vittuviṭum; tirivikkappaṭṭa piḷḷi uṭaṉē kaṇṭu ati eṭukkāviṭiṉ, vittapiḷḷi oru cuṟṟuc cuṟṟi vantu, vikkap paṭṭa piḷḷiyiṉ mutukil at tiriyāl ōraṭi ōṅkivittu eḻuppi, ati ap piḷḷiyiṭam koṭuttuviṭṭu, ap piḷḷi yiṉ iṭattil tāṉ uṭkārntukoḷḷum. tiri vittavuṭaṉ vikkappaṭṭa piḷḷi kaṇṭu eṭuttukkoṇṭu eḻuntuviṭiṉ, vitta piḷḷi virintu oru cuṟṟuc cuṟṟi vantu veṟṟiṭattil uṭkārntu aṭikkut  tappikkoḷḷum.
tiriyeṭutta allatu tirivāṅkiṉa piḷḷi, muṉpōl kuli... kāy, eṉṟu muṉcolla, maṟṟap piḷḷikaḷellām, nariyē.. ...th” eṉṟu piṉ colvar. piṉpu tirivippatumpiṟavu«nikaḻum. iṅṅaṉamtoṭarnJāṭappeṟum.
iv viḷiyāṭṭiṟkut tirittiri pantam eṉṟu peyar.
pāṇṭināṭṭil, piḷḷikaḷ vaṭṭamāy uṭkārātu variciyāy uṭkārntu, iv viḷiyāṭṭi āṭuvatuṇṭu. atu yāṉittiri eṉṟu peyar peṟum.
(3) irupoḻutāṭṭu
1. noṇṭi
āṭiṭamum poḻutum āṭuvārum : noṇṭi eṉpatu, poṭṭalilum tiṟanta veḷinilattilum, perumpālum ciṟuvarum ciṟupāṉmi ciṟumiyarum, pakalilum nilaviravilum viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭu.
āṭukaruvi : Mṭuvārbjkikkēṟpaniy¤tiṟkīṟa¥paṭṭoru peUvaṭlnmivtṭLkkaruviam.
āṭumuiw : iruvarukkuk kuṟiyāta cilarum palarum camat tokiyavāṉa irukaṭcikaḷāyp pirintu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭavarāyiṉ uttikaṭṭip pirintu koḷvar.
marapāṉa muṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉpaṭi tuṇintukoṇṭu xrufṭciyārtṭṭattiṟkuḷḷiṟaṅkuvar.kW kaṭciyārbvaḷiÃṉṟubkāṇṭu,x›voruvarāyo›bvārumuṟivaṭl¤tiṟkuḷno©ṭiyaṭittucceṉW,uḷÃṟpahruḷoruvirayō      gyiuyōellahriyumōtolmuyalvar.tol¥g£lt®  uṭaṉē bvaḷivantuviṭalnvaṇṭum.cŸÃ‰gh® miṉavarum,ta«mieṇṭiyaṭippavarjṭātavāṟu,va£ṭattiṟkuḷa§kumiṅkumōottirivar.a§‡d« ōṭumpōtu kōṭṭi Ätippiṉum,nkāṭṭiṟkubvaḷiyēflivappiṉum,cṭaṉēbvaḷivantuviṭalnvaṇṭum.beh©oao¥gh® kaḷittup pōyiṉ kōṭṭiṟku veḷiyē ceṉṟu kālūṉṟal vēṇṭum. orumuṟi veḷiyē ceṉṟapiṉ, kālūṉṟākkālum, mīṇṭum uḷḷē varal kūṭātu. noṇṭiyaṭippavar vaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷum kōṭṭiṉmēlum kālūṉṟiṉum, kōṭṭi mitikkiṉum, avar tolivatōṭu, avarālum (avar pintiṉavarāyiṉ) avarukku muntiṉavarālum toṭappaṭṭu veḷiyēṟiyuḷḷa attuṇip         pērum uṭaṉē uḷḷē vantuviṭuvar. mīṇṭum avarit toṭutal vēṇṭum. oruvar noṇṭiyaṭittu muṭinta piṉ, iṉṉoruvar noṇṭiyaṭippar. veḷiniṟkum kaṭciyāruḷ,  mutalilō iṭiyilō iṟutiyilō noṇṭiyaṭippavar oruvarē uḷniṟpār ellāriyum toṭṭuviṭuvatumuṇṭu; ovvoruvarum ovvoruvarit toṭuvatumuṇṭu; iṭiyiṭṭu orōvoruvar oruvariyō palariyō toṭuvatumuṇṭu; orēyoruvar orēyoruvarit toṭuvatu muṇṭu; oruvarum oruvariyum toṭātiruppatumuṇṭu. iṅṅaṉam, uḷ niṟkum kaṭciyāruḷ oruvarē veḷiyēṟiyiruttalum, cilarō palarō veḷiyēṟiyiruttalum, aṉivarum veḷiyēṟiyirut talum, aṉivarum veḷiyēṟātiruttalum, ākiya nālvaki nilimi ēṟpaṭakkūṭum.
veḷiniṟkum kaṭciyārum aṉivarum noṇṭiyaṭittu muṭintapiṉ, allatu uḷniṟkum kaṭciyāruḷ aṉivarum toṭappaṭṭapiṉ, ōr āṭṭam muṭiyum.
āṭṭa veṟṟi : uḷniṟpār aṉivarum toṭappaṭṭu viṭiṉ veḷiniṟpārkkum, aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi oruvar eñciyirup piṉum uḷ niṟpārkkum, veṟṟiyām.
āṭṭat toṭarcci : orumuṟi yāṭiyapiṉ, mīṇṭum orumuṟiyō pala muṟiyō āṭa nēramum viruppamumiruppiṉ, aṅṅaṉañ ceyvar. muntiya āṭṭattil veṉṟa kaṭciyārē pintiya āṭṭattilum uḷniṟpar. uḷniṟpatē iṉiyatākak karutappaṭum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : iv viḷiyāṭṭup pōrviṉi
yiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākum. pōrkkaḷattil oru kāl veṭṭuṇṭa maṟavaṉ maṟukālāl noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟē, tāṉ paṭṭu vīḻuntuṇiyum, pakivari veṭṭi vīḻttuvatuṇṭu. im maṟa viṉiyi naṭittukkāṭṭum mukamākavē iv viḷiyāṭṭut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
kuṟittalikaḷ kūttāṭuvatum taliyik kiyilēnti niṟpatum pōṉṟa maṟaviṉikaḷi nōkkumpōtu, oru kāṟ kuṟikaḷ noṇṭic ceṉṟu nūḻilāṭṭuvatu viyappaṉṟu.
kaḷvaṉoruvaṉ paṭiyiluḷḷa kutiriyoṉṟit tiruṭa muyaṉṟu kāl taṟiyuṇṭapiṉ nalvaḻippaṭṭa ceytiyic cintuc ceyyuḷāṟ puṉintu kūṟum noṇṭi nāṭakam eṉṉum nāṭaka nūlvakiyum uḷatu. cītakkāti noṇṭi nāṭakam itaṟkōr  eṭuttukkāṭṭām. in nāṭakac ceyti mēṟkūṟiya pōr viṉic ceytiyiṉ vēṟām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : oru kāl nōyppaṭṭum veṭṭuṇṭum naṭakkaviyalātapōtu maṟukālāl noṇṭiyaṭittu vēṇṭu miṭañ celvataṟkāṉa payiṟciyi, iv viḷiyāṭṭu aḷikkum.
2. niṉṟāl piṭittukkoḷ
ētēṉum oru vakiyil akappaṭṭukkoṇṭapiḷḷi piṟa piḷḷikaḷi niṉṟāl toṭavēṇṭum; uṭkārntu koṇṭāl toṭakkūṭātu. toṭappaṭṭa piḷḷi, piṉpu piṟarit toṭal vēṇṭum.
3. paruppuc caṭṭi
pala piḷḷikaḷ viṟaku viṟaku eṉṟu collik koṇṭu, vaṭṭamāyc cuṟṟi varavēṇṭum. oru periya piḷḷi avarkaḷi appaṭiyē uṭkārac colli, vaṭṭattuḷ niṉṟu uṅkaḷ vīṭṭil eṉṉa kuḻampu? eṉṟu varicippaṭi ovvoruvariyum kēṭkum. ovvoruvarum paruppallāta ovvoru kuḻampic colvar. piṉpu, iṟutiyil ellāp piḷḷikaḷuñ cērntu, ap periya piḷḷiyi avvāṟē kēṭpar. ap piḷḷi paruppukkuḻampu eṉṉum. uṭaṉē ellārum eḻuntiruntu, ap piḷḷiyip paruppuccaṭṭi eṉa aḻittu nakiyāṭi makiḻvar.
nāḷtōṟum paruppuk kuḻampiyē virumpiyuṇṇum oruvarip paḻippatupōl uḷḷatu,  iv viḷiyāṭṭu.
4. mōtiram vittal
āṭuvārellārum irukaṭciyākap pirintukoṇṭu, kaṭcikkoruvarāka iruvaroḻiya ēṉiyarellām, kaṭci vāriyāy iru variciyāka etiretir uṭkārntu koḷvar. uṭkārāta iruvarum tattam kaṭci variciyiṉ piṉṉāl niṉṟukoṇṭiruppar. avaruḷ oruvar oru mōtirattik kiyil vittukkoṇṭu kuṉintu, tam variciyil ovvoruvar piṉṉālum ati vippatāka naṭittu, yārēṉum oruvar piṉṉāl vittuviṭṭu, varici neṭukalum ceṉṟapiṉ nimirntu niṟpar. etir varicikkup piṉṉāl niṟpavar, mōtiram vikkappaṭṭa iṭatti iṉṉārukkup piṉ eṉṟu cuṭṭikkūṟa vēṇṭum. cariyāyc colliviṭiṉ, aṭutta muṟi etir variciyāḷ mōtiram vittal vēṇṭum; illāviṭiṉ muṉvittavarē vittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu āṭappeṟum. kīḻē yiruppavar āṭṭu neṭukalum uṭkārntu koṇṭē yiruppar.
5. puliyum āṭum
 pala piḷḷikaḷ vaṭṭamāyk kikōttu niṟka, oru piḷḷi uḷḷum maṟṟoru piḷḷi veḷiyum niṟpar. uḷ niṟkum piḷḷi āṭākavum, veḷi niṟkum piḷḷi puliyākavum, pāvikkap peṟuvar. pulikkum vaṭṭamāy niṟkum piḷḷikaṭkum piṉ varumāṟu uriyāṭṭu nikaḻum:
puli	:	caṅkili puṅkili katavit tiṟa.
piḷḷikaḷ	:	nāṉmāṭṭēṉ vēṅkippuli.
puli	:	varalāmā? varakkūṭātā?
piḷḷikaḷ	:	varakkūṭātu.
piḷḷikaḷ vaḻimaṟuttapiṉ, puli yārēṉum irupiḷḷikaṭ kiṭiyil nuḻiyap pārkkum. piḷḷikaḷ iṭam viṭuvatilli. palamuṟi aṅkumiṅkum cuṟṟip pārttapiṉ, puli tiṭumeṉṟu ōriṭattil valintu pukum. uṭaṉē āṭu veḷiyē viṭappeṟum. puli āṭṭip piṭikka veḷiyēṟum. āṭu mīṇṭum uḷḷē viṭappeṟum. iṅṅaṉam māṟimāṟi iraṇṭorumuṟi nikaḻntu piṉ, iṟutiyil puli āṭṭip piṭittukkoḷḷum.
iv viḷiyāṭṭiṉ tōṟṟam veḷippaṭi. vaṭṭamāy niṟkum piḷḷikaḷ āṭṭut toḻuvatti nikarppar.
6. ‘ïbj‹d _£il?
pala piḷḷikaḷ kūṭiṉaviṭattu, oru periya piḷḷi piṟariyellām vaṭṭamāka irutti, avarkaḷ kikaḷi virittu nilattiṉmēṟ kuppuṟa vikkac ceytu, oru maraputtoṭarc coṟkaḷit taṉittaṉi colli ovvoru collālum ovvoru kiyic cuṭṭi, iṟutic collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa piḷḷiyi uṅkaḷ appaṉ pēr eṉṉa? eṉṟu kēṭkum. ataṟku ap piḷḷi muruṅkippū eṉṉum. piṉpu ap periya piḷḷi muruṅkippū tiṉṟavaṉē? (ḷē!) muḷḷān taṇṇīr kuṭittavaṉē! (ḷē!) pāmpukki paṭakkeṉṟu eṭuttuk koḷ eṉṟu collum. muruṅkippū....... kuṭittavaṉē! (ḷē) eṉṉum pakutiyāl, collukkoruvarāka nāṉku piḷḷikaḷ cuṭṭappeṟum. kuṭittavaṉē! (ḷē) eṉṟu muṭiyum piḷḷi, uṭaṉē oru kiyi eṭuttup piṉṉāl vittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. kuṭittavaṉē! (ḷē) eṉṟu iraṇṭām muṟi muṭiyum piḷḷi, iṉṉoru kiyiyum eṭuttup piṉṉāl vittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam tirumpat tirumpac ceyyiṉ, iṟutiyil oru piḷḷi akappaṭṭukkoḷḷum.
ap piḷḷi taṉ irukikaḷiyum maṭakki oṉṟaṉmē loṉṟāyk kīḻēvikka, maṟṟap piḷḷikaḷum tam kikaḷi avvāṟē avaṟṟiṉmēl aṭukki vippar.
periya piḷḷi kīḻē cāṇipōṭṭu meḻukalāmā? maṇpōṭṭu meḻukalāmā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, piṟar cāṇipōṭṭu meḻuku eṉṟāl, taṉ akaṅkiyāl aṭikkiyiṉ kīḻp pūcuvatupōl taṭavavēṇṭum; maṇpōṭṭu meḻuku eṉṟāl, puṟaṅkiyāl avvāṟu ceyyavēṇṭum.
piṉpu mīṇṭum, tīṭṭiṉa kattiyil veṭṭalāmā? tīṭṭāta kattiyil veṭṭalāmā? eṉṟu periya piḷḷi kēṭṭu, tīṭṭiṉa kattiyil eṉṟāl aintuviralum nerukki nīṭṭip pakkavāṭ          ṭālum, tīṭṭāta kattiyil eṉṟāl maṇikkip pakkattālum veṭṭavēṇṭum.
 iṉi, veṭṭuñ ceyalpaṟṟi, muṟkūṟiya viṉāviṟkup patilāka, aṭiyil veṭṭaṭṭumā? nuṉiyil veṭṭaṭṭumā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, aṭiyil veṭṭu eṉṟāl kuttukkiyaṭukkiṉ aṭiyilum, nuṉiyil veṭṭu eṉṟāl ataṉ nuṉiyilum veṭṭuvatumuṇṭu.
piṉpu mīṇṭum muṉmuṟippaṭi kikaḷi aṭukki vikkavēṇṭum. periya piḷḷi ovvoru kiyāyt toṭṭu iteṉṉa mūṭṭi? iteṉṉa mūṭṭi? eṉṟu kēṭkum. piṟar arici mūṭṭi, paruppu mūṭṭi, puḷi mūṭṭi, uppu mūṭṭi eṉa ovvoru carakkiṉ peyarāṟ kūṟuvar. ovvoru mūṭṭiyilum koñcaṅ koñcan tāruṅkaḷ eṉṟu periya piḷḷi kēṭkum. piṟar taramāṭṭōm eṉpar. ataṉāṟ periya piḷḷi, ellāriyum kaṇṇi mūṭikkoṇṭu kuppuṟak kaviḻntukoḷḷac colvāḷ. avar aṅṅaṉañ ceytapiṉ, uṅkaḷ appā varukiṟārkaḷ, uṅkaḷ ammā varukiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu ēmāṟṟi yāriyēṉum eḻavittu, ap piḷḷikku oru kuṟippiṭṭa tokiyāṉa aṭi periyapiḷḷi koṭukkum. oru varum ēmāṟi eḻāviṭiṉ, viḷiyāṭṭup pōtum, eḻuntiruṅkaḷ eṉṟu colli, ellārum eḻuntapiṉ ellārkkum aṭikoṭukkum.  itōṭu āṭṭam muṭiyum.
7. kummi
pala pētiyarum1 petumpiyarum, vaṭṭamākac cuṟṟivantu pāṭik kikuvittu aṭikkum kūttu, kummi eṉappaṭum. vaṭārk kāṭṭu vaṭṭārattār itik koppi eṉpar.
kummutal kikuvittal allatu kikuvit taṭittal. kikuvit taṭikkum viḷiyāṭṭātalāl, itu kummiyeṉappaṭṭatu.
kummi yāṭṭattiṟkeṉṟu taṉivakip pāṭṭuṇṭu. atu kummiyaṭi eṉṟu toṭaṅkuvatōṭu, at toṭariyē ovvōr uruvilum (caraṇattilum) makuṭamākavuṅ koṇ ṭirukkum.
eṭuttukkāṭṭu
kummiyaṭi peṇṇē kummiyaṭi - nalla
koṉṟi malarcūṭik kummiyaṭi
 nammiyā ḷumtaṉi nāyakam nammiṭam
naṇṇiya teṉṟunī kummiyaṭi
 āṭcimoḻi yiṅkē āṅkilamāy - eṉṟum
āki viṭiṉatu kēṭākum
 māṭci mikuntamiḻ mānilat tāḷuki
mātara cēvarak kummiyaṭi.
ikkāli, oṟṟit tāḷattiṟkum aṭittāḷattiṟkum 
ēṟkum ellāp pāṭṭukaḷum kummikkum pāṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oṟṟi = ēkam. aṭi = āti.
1.	aintāṇṭu mutal ēḻāṇṭuvarippaṭṭa peṇ pētiyeṉappaṭuvāḷ.
2
kuḻantip pakkam
naṟṟāyarum cevilittāyarum piṟarum, aiyāṇṭiṟkuk kīḻppaṭṭa kuḻantikaḷik koṇṭu āṭum āṭṭattokuti, kuḻantip pakkam ākum.
irupoḻutāṭṭu
1 . cōṟu koṇṭupōkiṟa vaḻiyilē
mutiyār oruvar oru kuḻantiyiṉ kiyi akaṅki mēṉōkkap piṭittukkoṇṭu, ataṉ aintu viralkaḷiyum ovvoṉṟāka muṟiyē toṭṭu itu aiyāviṟku, itu ammāviṟku, itu aṇṇaṉukku, itu akkāviṟku, itu uṉakku, eṉṟu kūṟi, av vakaṅkiyiṟ paruppuk kaṭivatupōl tam kiyāl naṭittukkāṭṭi, piṉpu mīṇṭum paruppuk kaṭintu, cōṟu koṇṭupōkiṟa vaḻiyilē eṉṟu collik koṇṭu tam kiyi ak kuḻantiyiṉ akkuḷvari mella iḻuttucceṉṟu, kiccuk kiccu eṉṟu collik kiccaṅ kāṭṭuvar. itu kuḻantikku makiḻcciyum cirippiyum uṇṭu paṇṇum.
paruppuk kaṭivatupōṟ ceyyumpōtu, paruppuk kaṭintu paruppuk kaṭintu eṉṟum, paruppip pakirvatupōṟ ceyyum pōtu, intā uṉakku, intā uṉakku eṉṟum collappaṭum.
vaḻakkamāyk kūḻuṅ kañciyum uṇpavar, cōṟu koṇṭupōkiṟa vaḻiyilē eṉpatiṟkup patilāka, kañci koṇṭupōkiṟa vaḻiyilē eṉpar. ataṉāl avar at  toṭa rālēyē iv viḷiyāṭṭik kuṟippar.
tolivāṉa iṭattil vēli ceyyum aṇṇaṉukkup paruppum cōṟum koṇṭupōkum taṅkiyi, oruvar kiccuk kāṭṭiya viṉiyi iv viḷiyāṭṭuk kuṟikkumpōlum!
2. aṭṭalaṅkāy puṭṭalaṅkāy
cevilittāy allatu mūtāy (pāṭṭi) pala kuḻantikaḷi variciyākak kālnīṭṭi uṭkāravittu, avarkaḷ kālkaḷi iṭa valamākavum vala iṭamākavum taṭavikkoṇṭu,
aṭṭalaṅkāy puṭṭalaṅkāy aṭukkaṭukkāy mātuḷaṅkāy
 paccarici kuttip paraṇmēlē vittirukku
 tēṅkāy uṭittut tiṇṇimēlē vittirukku
 māṅkāy uṭittu maṭimēlē vittirukku
 uppuk kaṇṭañ cuṭṭu uṟimēlē vittirukku
 entap pūṉi tiṉṟatu - intap pūṉi tiṉṟatu
eṉṟu pāṭi, intap pūṉi eṉṟu collumpōtu oru kuḻanti yic cuṭṭik kāṭṭuvāḷ.
pūṉi uṟiyiluḷḷa uppukkaṇṭattit tiṉṟuviṭṭatik kuṟippatāka uḷḷatu iv viḷiyāṭṭu.
3
periyōr pakkam
iḷiñarukku mēṟpaṭṭa iṭiñarum mutiyōrum āṭum āṭṭut tokuti periyōr pakkamām.
1. āṇpāṟ pakuti
(1) pakalāṭṭu
tāyam
itu palavakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ peruvaḻakkāṉatu patiṉintu nāyum puliyum eṉpatām. itu tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē āṭappaṭṭateṉpatu,
valeṉ kiḷavi toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē	(tol. 373)
 nāyum palakiyum varūuṅ kāli
 āvayiṉ ukaraṅ keṭutalu murittē
 ukaraṅ keṭuvaḻi akaram niliyum	(. 374)
eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷāl (cūttiraṅkaḷāl) aṟiyalām.
(2) iravāṭṭu
kaḻiyal
oru kuṟippiṭṭa tokiyiṉar, kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil neruṅki niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoru kiyilum ovvoru kuṟuṅkaḻi ēnti avaṟṟip piṟar kaḻikaḷōṭu tākkiyaṭittu, piṉṉip piṉṉiyum cuṟṟic cuṟṟiyum varum āṭṭu, kaḻiyal eṉappaṭum.
itu pakalilum āṭaṟkuriyatāyiṉum, potuvāka iravilēyē āṭappeṟum. itaṟkum kummikkup pōl oru taṉivakip pāṭṭuṇṭu. atu,
 taṉṉaṉa taṉṉaṉa taṉṉāṉa - taṉa
taṉṉaṉa tāṉaṉa taṉṉāṉa
eṉṉum vaṇṇam paṟṟiyatākum.
(3) irupoḻutāṭṭu
mukkuḻiyāṭṭam
iruvarum avarkku mēṟpaṭṭavarum, periya elumiccaṅkā yaḷavāṉa iruppukkuṇṭu āḷukkoṉṟu vittukkoṇṭu, pappattuk kacam iṭippaṭṭa mukkuḻikaḷil eṟintāṭuvatu, mukkuḻiyāṭṭam eṉappaṭum.
itu perumpāṉmi pakalilum ciṟupāṉmi nilavirali lum āṭappeṟum. tiruccirāppaḷḷi vaṭṭārattār itic ciṟappāka āṭuvar.
peṇpāṟ pakuti
(1) pakalāṭṭu
1. paṇṇāṅkuḻi
itu iḷiñar pakkattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
2. tāyam
paramapatam, ciluvittāyam eṉa vaḻaṅkum kuṟukkuk kaṭṭat tāyam ākiya iraṇṭum, perumpāṉmiyāka vaḻaṅkum peṇṭir tāya viḷiyāṭṭukaḷām.
(2) irupoḻutāṭṭu
kummi
itu iḷiñar pakkattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
taliyāya kulattup peṇṭir potuvāka iti āṭuvatilli.
piṟkuṟippu : 54ām pakkattil variyappaṭṭuḷḷa pārikkōṭu (allatu pārikōṭu) eṉṉum viḷiyāṭṭu, pāriyiṉ paṟampumaliyi mūvēntarum muṟṟukiyiṭṭiruntatik kuṟikkalām.
piṉṉiṇippu
I. vaḻakkaṟṟa viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ
1. aṟiyappaṭṭavi
(1) āṇpāṟ pakuti
vaṭṭu
itu pakalil āṭappaṭum oruvakic cūtāṭṭākum.
(2) peṇpāṟ pakuti
1. pala pantu
orutti 5 pantu koṇṭāṭiyatu cintāmaṇiyuḷḷum, irumakaḷir 7 pantum 12 pantum koṇṭāṭiyatu peruṅkatiyuḷ ḷum, kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi pakalāṭṭu.
2. ammāṉi
mūvar makaḷir muṟiyē kūṟṟum viṉāvum viṭiyumāka mukkūṟuṭiyatum ammāṉi eṉṟiṟuvatumāṉa oruvakik koccakak kalippāvip pāṭikkoṇṭu, taṉittaṉi pala pantukaḷip pōṭṭup piṭittu āṭum āṭṭu ammāṉiyām. ituvum pakalāṭṭē.
uḻuviyuri yarikkacitta ulakamelā muṭiyaperu
muḻumutalē karuvinakar mukantiruntār ammāṉi
muḻumutalē karuvinakar mukantiruntā rāmāyiṉ
eḷiyavarpōṟ kaḷavāṇṭa temmuṟiyē ammāṉi
itaṉālaṉ ṟēmaṟivāy irukkiṉṟār ammāṉi
eṉpatu ōr ammāṉic ceyyuḷām.
3. kuravi
 eḻuvar allatu paṉṉiruvar makaḷir, vaṭṭamākac cuṟṟivantu pāṭiyāṭum kūttu, kuraviyām. itu iru poḻutāṭ ṭākat terikiṉṟatu.
2. aṟiyappaṭātavi
cāḻal, teḷḷēṇam mutaliyaṉa.
pantu puḷiyaṅkoṭṭi mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu viḷiyāṭum ōr āṭṭu, accuppūṭṭi viḷiyāṭutal eṉṉum peyarāl, rāṭṭilar (Rottler) akarātiyiṟ kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷatu.
II. paḷḷikkūṭa viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ
kōḻikkuñcu - 1
pala piḷḷikaḷ, oruvarpiṉ oruvarāka oruvar iṭuppi iṉṉoruvar cērntu kaṭṭikkoṇṭu allatu paṟṟik koṇṭu, variciyāka niṟpar. talimiyāṉa piḷḷi mutalil niṟkum. ap piḷḷikku etirē maṟṟoru piḷḷi niṟkum.
talimiyāṉa piḷḷi kōḻiyiyum, piṉṉāl niṟkum piḷḷikaḷ ataṉ kuñcukaḷiyum, etirē niṟkum piḷḷi kaḻukiyum, nikarppar. kaḻuku kaṭicik kuñcip piṭikka varum. varici vaḷintu kuñcukaḷ tappum.
kaḻuku kōḻiyi nōkki eṉakkoru kuñcu tā; iṉṟēl paṟantu vantu piṭittukkoṇṭu pōyviṭuvēṉ eṉṉum. kōḻi maṟuttu, kaḻuku kuñcip piṭikkātapaṭi maṟikkum. kaḻuku cuṟṟic cuṟṟi vantu kaṭicik kuñcip piṭittukkoṇṭu pōkum. iṅṅaṉamē, piṉpu ēṉik kuñcukaḷiyum ovvoṉṟāyp piṭittukkoṇṭu pōyviṭum.
kōḻi mika mika varuntum.
kōḻikkuñcu - 2
eṭṭup piḷḷikaḷ cērntukoḷvar. avaruḷ oru piḷḷi kō ḻiyiyum, aintu piḷḷikaḷ ataṉ kuñcukaḷiyum, maṟṟoru piḷḷi kaḻukiyum, maṟṟumoru piḷḷi nariyiyum nikarppar.
kōḻi taṉ kuñcukaḷik kūṇṭiṟkuḷ irukkac colliviṭṭu, iritēṭa veḷiyē ceṉṟuviṭum. kuñcukaḷ tāy pēccit taṭṭik kūṇṭiṟku veḷiyē varum. kaḻuku oru kuñci eṭuttukkoṇṭupōkum. ēṉik kuñcukaḷellām kūṇṭiṟkuḷ ōṭiviṭum. piṉpu caṟṟunēram poṟuttu mīṇṭum veḷiyē varum. kaḻuku mīṇṭum oru kuñci eṭuttukkoṇṭu pōkum. iṅṅaṉam nālu kuñcu pōṉapiṉ tāy tirumpivarum. eñciyuḷḷa kuñcu naṭantatic collum.
kōḻi kaḻukiṭam ceṉṟu, eṉ kuñcukaḷiyellāṅ koṭuttuviṭu. avaṟṟiṟkup patilāka nāṉ uṉakku nāḷikkuk kaṟitaruvēṉ eṉṟu vākkaḷittu, kuñcukaḷi mīṭṭukkoṇṭu pōkum.
maṟunāḷ, kaḻukiṟkuk kaṟi eṅṅaṉam koṭuppateṉṟu kōḻi kavaṉṟukoṇṭirukkumpōtu, nari ataṉiṭam vantu, nī kavalippaṭātē. kaḻukiṟkuk kaṟikoṭukka vēṇṭiyatilli. ataṉiṉṟu tappuvataṟku uṉakkoru valakkarām (tantiram) collikkoṭuppēṉ, eṉṟu collum. kōḻi ati nampikkoṇṭu cummā iruntuviṭum. piṉpu nari kaḻukiṭam ceṉṟu, kōḻi uṉakku vākkaḷittapaṭi kaṟi koṭukkātām. nī pōy ataṉ kuñcukaḷip piṭittukkoṇṭu vantāltāṉ uṉakkuk kaṟi kiṭikkum eṉṟu mūṭṭiviṭum. kaḻuku uṭaṉē pōy, ellāk kuñcukaḷiyum ovvoṉṟākap piṭittukkeṇṭu vantuviṭum. narikku oru kuñcu paṅku kiṭikkum.
kōḻi nariyi nampik keṭṭuppōṉati niṉittu mikat tuyaruṟum.
kuṟippu : mēṟkūṟiya viḷiyāṭṭiraṇṭum pētipparuvap peṇkaḷukkuriyavi. aiyāṭṭip paruvattārāyiṉ āṇ piḷḷikaḷum cērntukoḷḷalām.
III. paṇṭi viḷiyāṭṭu viḻākkaḷ
(1) puṉal viḷiyāṭṭu
paṇṭik kālattil, āṟṟarukē yirunta nakaramānta rellārum, āṇṭutōṟum āṟṟiṟ putuveḷḷam vantavuṭaṉ oruṅkē ceṉṟu, oru pakaliṟ perumpakuti av veḷḷattil tiḷittāṭi iṉpuṟṟa viḷiyāṭṭu viḻā, puṉal viḷiyāṭṭu allatu puṉalāṭṭu eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu. atu nīrviḻā, nīrāṭṭu, nīrāṭṭaṇi mutaliya peyar koṇṭum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
nīrāṭuvārellām, āṟṟiliṭuvataṟkup poṉṉālum veḷḷiyālum ceyta mīṉ mutaliya kāṇikkik karuvikaḷiyum, puṇi teppam parical mutaliya mitavik karuvikaḷiyum, kātalarmītu vāca neyyiyum vaṇṇa nīriyum teḷittaṟkut turutti kompu civiṟi mutaliya viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷiyum, nīrāṭiyapiṉ vēṇṭum ūṇ uṭi akil mutaliyavaṟṟiyum, tattamakku iyaṉṟavāṟu, yāṉi kutiri tēr mutaliyavaṟṟil ūrntum kālnaṭiyāy naṭantum, koṇṭu celvar. kuṭivāriyāka āṅkāṅku amikkappeṟṟa kuṟṟilkaḷum putukkaṭikaḷum, cērntu, oru viḻavūr pōlak kāṭciyaḷikkum.
nīntavallār caṟṟu āḻattilum allātār kariyi
yaṭuttum nīrāṭuvatum, pūcuñ cuṇṇam cāntu kuḻampu mutaliyaṉavaṟṟiṉ ēṟṟattāḻvupaṟṟip peṇṭir ikalāṭuvatum, tam kaṇavaṉmār piṟa peṇṭiroṭu kūṭip puṉalāṭiṉāreṉṟu maṉikkiḻat tiyar ūṭuvatum, pūcucāntam puṉintamāli uḻakkum nīrāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ kalaṅkal veḷḷam putumaṇam peṟuvatum, puṉalāṭṭu nikaḻccikaḷām.
nīrāṭiyavar mālikkālattil makiḻntum ayarntum maṉi tirumpuvar.
nīrāṭṭu viḻā uñci nakarattil 21 nāḷ nikaḻnta teṉap peruṅkati kūṟum.
ik kālattuk koṇṭāṭappeṟum āṭip patiṉeṭṭām perukku, paṇṭip puṉalāṭṭi oru puṭiyokkum.
(2) poḻil viḷiyāṭṭu
nakaravāṇar, iḷavēṉiṟ kālattil, ūrukkuc caṟṟut toliviluḷḷa oru cōlikkuc ceṉṟu, taṉittaṉiyākavum kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamākavum oru pakal camittuṇṭu makiḻnta viḻā, poḻil viḷiyāṭṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. iḵtu ikkālattil uṟaviṉarum naṇparumāka oru cilar ceṉṟuṇṇum kāṭṭuṇāp pōṉṟatākum.
cōliyi aṭintapiṉ, aṭicil toḻilil īṭu
paṭṭavaroḻinta ēṉiyarellām vevvēṟu viṉipaṟṟi vevvēṟiṭañceṉṟu viṭuvar. āṭavaruḷ periyōr vēṭṭiyāṭavum, ciṟiyōr maramēṟutal kāykaṉi paṟittuṇṭal viḷiyāṭutal mutaliya viṉi nikaḻttavum, pirintuviṭuvar. peṇṭiruḷ mūttōr aṭicil toḻilil amara, iḷiyōr, malar koytu māli toṭukkavum, pāvi puṉintu pārāṭṭi makiḻavum, ūñcalamittu untiyāṭavum, ciṟṟiliḻittuc ciṟucōṟu camikkavum, āṅkāṅku akaṉṟuviṭuvar.
cilar kaṭṭamutu koṇṭucelvatumuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, aṉṉār aṉivarum iṉpa viḷiyāṭṭil īṭupaṭuvar.
naṇpakal uṇavuṇṭapiṉ, cila nāḻiki nēram iḷippāṟi mālik kālam vīṭu tirumpuvatu iyalpākum.
putitāy maṇanta kātalar, tāmē ceṉṟu āṭum poḻilāṭṭum, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu potu nakara māntar koṇṭāṭum poḻilāṭṭum, vevvēṟām.
* * * * *
